### [34] [SimCortex v2: Joint Cortical Surface Reconstruction with Near-Zero Collisions and Self-Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07378)
*Kaveh Moradkhani,Sylvain Bouix*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimCortex v2 是一个深度学习框架，可从 T1 加权 MRI 同时重建左右半球的 WM 与 pial 四个皮层表面，在 14 个队列 560 例数据上达到平均对称表面距离 0.253 mm，且 92.14% 病例无表面间碰撞、自交比例仅 0.044%，全部代码、权重与数据划分公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮层薄且折叠紧密，传统重建易产生网格自交与表面间碰撞等几何伪影。尽管深度学习已将重建时间从小时级压缩到分钟级，这些几何伪影依然存在，因此需要一种能同时保证拓扑正确性与几何无碰撞的重建方法。

Method: 先用体分割估计拓扑正确的初始表面，再以 ribbon 条件化的类 U-Net 网络预测多尺度平稳速度场（stationary velocity field），对左右 WM 与 pial 四个表面进行联合配准式细化。

Result: 在 14 个队列 560 例（13 个训练未见，年龄 6-89 岁，健康与临床人群，三家厂商扫描仪）上，表面距离精度与最强基线持平（平均对称表面距离 0.253 mm），92.14% 病例未检测到表面间碰撞，自交比例 0.044% 为学习类方法中最低；所有基线在每一例中均至少出现一次碰撞。

Conclusion: SimCortex v2 在保持精度持平的同时显著改善了表面几何质量（无碰撞、低自交），并开源代码、配置、预训练权重、预处理数据与评估划分，可作为皮层表面重建的可复现基线。

Abstract: Reconstructing cortical WM and pial surfaces from structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is a prerequisite for surface-based neuroanatomical analysis, yet remains challenging because the cortex is thin and tightly folded. Reconstruction methods can produce geometric artifacts such as mesh self-intersections and collisions between cortical surfaces, and although recent deep learning methods have reduced reconstruction time from hours to minutes, these artifacts persist. We propose SimCortex v2, a deep learning framework for simultaneous reconstruction of the left and right WM and pial surfaces from T1-weighted MRI. SimCortex v2 estimates topologically correct initial surfaces from a volumetric segmentation and refines all four jointly using multi-scale stationary velocity fields predicted by a ribbon-conditioned, U-Net-like network. We evaluated SimCortex v2 on 560 cases from 14 cohorts, thirteen of them unseen during training, spanning ages 6-89, healthy and clinical populations, and scanners from three vendors. SimCortex v2 matched the surface-distance accuracy of the strongest baseline (average symmetric surface distance 0.253 mm) while showing no detected inter-surface collision in 92.14% of cases and the lowest self-intersection fraction (0.044%) among learning-based methods, whereas every baseline produced at least one collision in every case. Source code, configuration files, pretrained weights, preprocessed data, and the exact evaluation splits are publicly released.

</details>


### [35] [GeoWM: Efficient Direct World Modeling in Explicit Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07381)
*Mehrdad Noori,Guile Wu,Sam Hosseini,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoWM 提出一种几何世界模型，用几何基础模型把观测 RGB 帧编码为几何历史，再以 flow-matching transformer 直接预测指定未来时刻的场景几何，避免递归 rollout 带来的误差累积与高计算成本，并配合轻量相机运动预测器提供未来视角下的几何先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型范式多为预测未来图像或隐表示后再恢复几何，未显式建模几何结构，且依赖递归展开以延长预测时域，导致误差累积与推理成本随预测距离增长。

Method: GeoWM 以几何基础模型将观测 RGB 帧转化为几何历史作为条件，输入 flow-matching transformer 直接预测指定未来 horizon 的场景几何；额外训练轻量相机运动预测器估计未来视角，并将观测几何投影到预测视角作为几何先验以增强预测。

Result: 在覆盖城市驾驶、空中飞行与动态操作的四个数据集上，GeoWM 在深度、相机位姿与 3D 场景几何预测上优于所评估的世界模型，同时在长时域下显著降低推理时间。

Conclusion: 直接几何预测 + 非递归的 horizon 条件生成，是兼顾精度与效率的可行世界模型几何建模路线，未来视角投影先验对几何预测有实质增益。

Abstract: Modeling 3D scene geometry and its evolution over time is essential for autonomous driving and robotics. A common paradigm is to use world models to predict future images or latent representations of the environment and subsequently recover geometry from these predictions. However, this paradigm does not explicitly model geometric structure and typically relies on recursive rollouts to reach longer prediction horizons, leading to error accumulation and increasing computational cost. To address these limitations, we present GeoWM, a geometry world model that directly forecasts future scene geometry at specified future horizons without recursive rollout. The key idea is to leverage a geometry foundation model to transform observed RGB frames into a geometric history, which conditions a flow-matching transformer to predict the scene geometry at a specified future horizon. We further show that a lightweight camera-motion predictor can accurately estimate the future viewpoint, and that projecting the observed geometry into the predicted viewpoint provides an effective geometric prior for future geometry forecasting. Extensive experiments on four datasets spanning urban driving, aerial flight, and dynamic manipulation demonstrate that GeoWM outperforms the evaluated world models in forecasting depth, camera pose, and 3D scene geometry, while substantially reducing inference time at longer horizons.

</details>


### [36] [WildMatch: Weakly Supervised Image Matcher Adaptation for Wildlife Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07384)
*Turhan Can Kargin,Piotr Kubaty,Ekaterina Rostovskaya,Izabela Wierzbowska,Bartosz Zieliński,Marcin Przewięźlikowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用身份标签弱监督微调预训练关键点匹配器，实现野生动物个体重识别，无需关键点级标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法分为两类：全局嵌入分类法需大量个体标注且忽略局部纹理证据；直接使用通用图像匹配器则难以适配数据量小、无对应级标注的野生动物领域。作者希望在仅有身份标签的弱监督下适配匹配器。

Method: 用预训练匹配器挖掘信息量高的图像对，由身份一致性构造弱正/负监督信号，对比式微调匹配网络：增强同身份对的关键点对应、抑制异身份对的对应，无需关键点或几何对应真值。

Result: 在多个开源野生动物重识别数据集上，精度超过现成匹配器和一种局部-全局融合的SOTA方法；在留出个体的开放世界协议下仍有效，表明学到的是可迁移的对应先验而非记忆训练身份。

Conclusion: 这是首个匹配器级别、身份监督的动物重识别适配研究，可用监控数据集中已有的身份标注实现数据高效领域特化。

Abstract: Individual animal re-identification from camera-trap imagery is an instance retrieval problem central to non-invasive wildlife monitoring: a query image must retrieve the correct individual from a reference set of known animals. This requires computer vision models to recognize distinctive local patterns in fur, skin, or other visual markings. Current approaches either learn global embeddings as a classification problem, requiring many labeled images per individual while largely ignoring local evidence, or apply off-the-shelf, domain-agnostic image matchers. Although such matchers are pretrained on large and diverse image collections, adapting them to wildlife imagery is challenging because available datasets are small and lack correspondence-level annotations. We study weakly supervised adaptation of a pretrained keypoint matcher using only identity labels, without keypoint-level or geometric correspondence ground truth. We mine informative image pairs with the pretrained matcher, derive weak positive and negative supervision from identity agreement, and contrastively fine-tune the matching network to strengthen correspondences for same-identity pairs and suppress them for different identities. Across open-source wildlife re-identification datasets, our approach improves accuracy over off-the-shelf matchers and a state-of-the-art local--global fusion method. Under an open-world protocol with held-out individuals, it learns a transferable correspondence prior rather than memorizing training identities. To our knowledge, this is the first study of matcher-level, identity-supervised adaptation for animal re-identification. Our method enables data-efficient specialization of image matching models to wildlife domains using identity annotations already available in typical monitoring datasets.

</details>


### [37] [Semantic Capability Acquisition and Specialization During Vision-Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07385)
*Suguru Onda,Matthew Bailey,Ryan Farrell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出“语义能力轨迹”框架，追踪视觉语言模型微调过程中身份与属性检索能力的获取、峰值与后期衰退，并引入结构化语义路由（SSR）改善无名称属性画像检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM微调评估通常在单一最终检查点进行，无法判断某项语义能力是从未获得，还是早期出现后又在专业化过程中退化。作者希望厘清语义能力的获取时机、峰值、迁移性与部署时残留状态。

Method: 提出基于轨迹的框架，将能力获取、能力特定最优与后期专业化分离；在DFN、MetaCLIP、OpenAI CLIP等六种预训练骨干上追踪身份与属性类能力；引入结构化语义路由（SSR）研究监督表示形式如何影响所学内容，并测试随机名称分支dropout的效果。

Result: 微调可获得超越预训练状态的语义能力，并在留出评估中观察到增益；非结构化名称-属性监督产生强名称-属性检索但无名称属性画像检索较弱；SSR显著提升后者，随机名称分支dropout进一步增强；不同能力峰值阶段不同，按目标类名检索选择的检查点未必对应可迁移语义最优。

Conclusion: 持续优化可能在保持强目标类名检索的同时降低先前获得的可迁移语义能力；诊断研究显示这种后期专业化伴随跨模态语义可及性下降，而图像单模态类结构仍大量保留。

Abstract: Fine-tuning vision-language models (VLMs) is typically evaluated at a single downstream checkpoint, obscuring whether a semantic capability was never acquired or emerged earlier and later declined during specialization. We ask how semantic capabilities are acquired, when they peak, how well they transfer, and what remains at deployment. We study these dynamics as a semantic capability trajectory, tracking identity- and attribute-based capabilities over training. We formulate a trajectory-based framework that separates capability acquisition, capability-specific optima, and later specialization, and introduce Structured Semantic Routing (SSR) to study how the representation of supervision shapes what is acquired. Across six pretrained backbones spanning DFN, MetaCLIP, and OpenAI CLIP, we show that fine-tuning can acquire semantic capability beyond the pretrained state, including gains observed on held-out evaluations. Unstructured name-and-attribute supervision produces strong name-and-attribute retrieval with comparatively weak name-free attribute-profile retrieval, whereas SSR yields substantially stronger name-free attribute-profile retrieval and is further strengthened by stochastic name-branch dropout. Different capabilities can peak at different stages, so a checkpoint selected by target class-name retrieval need not coincide with a transferable semantic optimum. Continued optimization can therefore preserve strong target class-name retrieval while reducing previously acquired transferable semantic capability. In a representative diagnostic study, this late specialization is consistent with reduced cross-modal semantic accessibility while substantial image-only class structure remains available.

</details>


### [38] [ElasticFit: Fit-Aware 3D Object Insertion via VLM Reasoning and Generative Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07460)
*Tzu-Hsin Hsieh,Ricardo Marroquim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ElasticFit：VLM 引导的“适配感知”物体插入框架，用场景落地的结构化适配线索（落点、体积、朝向、适配模式）驱动 3D 生成与几何拟合，显著提升空间关系与支撑成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 与生成模型虽具备语义推理与视觉生成能力，但在“把物体塞进受限局部空间”这类任务上缺乏 3D 落地与几何控制力；仅选择合理位置不足以保证局部几何贴合、语义意图与物理合理性。

Method: 提出以场景落地的表示为中枢：由语言指令与渲染场景观测推断结构化适配线索（grounding 位置、占据体积、朝向、适配模式——刚性放置/等比缩放/弹性变形），将高层 VLM 推理转成显式 3D 约束；据此生成场景条件物体先验、重建为 3D、并按模式做网格拟合，施加碰撞避免、接触一致与物理接地约束。

Result: 在固定资产基线上，空间关系成功率由 50.8% 提升至 69.7%，支撑成功率由 48.3% 提升至 91.7%；并在复杂场景中支持生成式"make-it-fit"插入。

Conclusion: 将 VLM 语义推理显式转译为 3D 适配约束，是把生成式物体插入变为几何可控、物理可信任务的有效路径。

Abstract: Inserting objects into existing 3D scenes requires more than selecting a plausible location: the inserted object must also fit local geometry while preserving semantic intent and physical plausibility. Although recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and generative models enable semantic reasoning and visual content creation, they offer limited 3D grounding and geometric control when an inserted object must fit into constrained local spaces. We introduce \textbf{ElasticFit}, a VLM-guided framework for fit-aware object insertion centered on a novel scene-grounded representation. Given a language instruction and rendered scene observations, ElasticFit infers structured fitting cues that specify where the object should be grounded, what volume it should occupy, how it should be oriented, and its adaptation mode (rigid placement, uniform scaling, or elastic fitting). These cues convert high-level VLM reasoning into explicit 3D constraints that condition object generation and guide downstream geometric fitting. ElasticFit then generates a scene-conditioned object prior, reconstructs it in 3D, and refines the mesh through mode-specific fitting while enforcing collision avoidance, contact consistency, and physical grounding. In fixed-asset baseline comparisons, ElasticFit improves spatial relation success from 50.8\% to 69.7\% and support success from 48.3\% to 91.7\% over the strongest baseline, while providing novel support for generative "make-it-fit" insertions in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [39] [Protective Perturbations Must Survive the Resize: Scale-Robust Image Immunization against Malicious Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07464)
*Zhongliang Guo,Yan Lin,Yifei Qian,Daizong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将图像缩放建模为频率选择信道，分析了保护性扰动在不同缩放因子下的衰减规律，并提出 SRIM 方法通过锚点尺度采样与偏向最弱尺度的加权，实现对未知缩放范围的最坏情况保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性"保护扰动"（阻止指令引导的图像编辑）都在编辑者工作分辨率下优化与评估，而实际分享的照片达 10MP 以上且编辑者会先以未知因子降采样，导致保护失效。需要刻画缩放对扰动的影响并设计对未知尺度鲁棒的保护方法。

Method: 将降采样建模为频率选择信道；从理论上分析不同优化分辨率下扰动的衰减：原生分辨率扰动随缩放因子衰减且无缩放时也弱，固定工作分辨率扰动只保护一个尺度窗口；最坏情况保护仅随范围宽度对数退化，多尺度平均无法达到该界。据此提出 SRIM：在覆盖全范围的锚点尺度网格上采样，权重偏向当前最弱尺度，以牺牲变换期望为代价换取最坏情况鲁棒性。

Result: 在 9–30MP 全分辨率照片、2 至 8 倍降采样下，SRIM 将 FLUX.2-klein 编辑的最坏情况破坏从最强已发表保护的 0.192 LPIPS 提升至 0.463；同等可见性下保护约翻倍。对 9B 模型与 FLUX.2-dev 最坏情况分别为 0.450 和 0.386（已发表方法至多 0.184），在 InstructPix2Pix 上也领先。

Conclusion: SRIM 通过频率信道分析与最弱尺度加权的尺度网格采样，显著提升了对未知降采样因子的最坏情况保护，并在多个编辑模型上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Protective perturbations aim to stop malicious instruction-guided editing of personal photos, but they are optimized and evaluated at the editor's working resolution, whereas shared photos have 10 megapixels or more and editors first downscale them by an unknown factor. We model this resize as a frequency-selective channel. In this model, a perturbation computed at the native resolution decays with the downscaling factor and is weak even without a resize, and a perturbation computed at a fixed working resolution protects only a window of scales. The best worst-case protection over an unknown range of scales degrades only logarithmically with the width of the range, and averaging over scales does not reach it. Guided by this analysis, we propose SRIM, which samples a grid of anchor scales covering the whole range, with weights that favor the currently weakest scale, at the cost of standard expectation over transformation. On full-resolution photos of 9 to 30 megapixels and downscaling factors from 2 to 8, SRIM raises the worst-case disruption of FLUX.2-klein edits from 0.192 LPIPS, attained by the strongest published protection, to 0.463. At equal visibility, it roughly doubles the protection. The same protected photos also protect against the 9B model and against FLUX.2-dev, with worst cases of 0.450 and 0.386 against at most 0.184 for published protections, and SRIM leads on InstructPix2Pix as well.

</details>


### [40] [LARK: A Low-Cost, Accurate, Occlusion-Resilient, Kalman Filter-Assisted Tracking System for Image-Guided Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07561)
*George Sideris,Justin Cree,Andrew Stirling,Mamadou Ly,Étienne Léger,D. Louis Collins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LARK是一个基于消费级RGB相机的多相机光学跟踪系统，通过多视角冗余与融合实现手术器械跟踪，在遮挡条件下达到亚毫米级精度，硬件成本低于1000美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用立体红外跟踪器精度高但易受遮挡且成本高昂，许多场合难以负担，因此需要一种低成本、抗遮挡的光学跟踪方案用于图像引导手术。

Method: 采用商品化RGB硬件构建多相机系统，开发并评估两种完整跟踪方法：多视角单目位姿融合与多视角三角测量；结合自适应卡尔曼滤波，并在不同遮挡程度下用精密加工栅格与解剖头模进行评测。

Result: 五相机加自适应卡尔曼滤波下，三角测量点定位中位目标配准误差0.64 mm，位姿融合轨迹跟踪0.73 mm；相机子集实验显示可用视角减少时精度平缓退化；硬件成本低于1000美元。

Conclusion: LARK以低成本提供亚毫米级抗遮挡跟踪平台，硬件设计与软件开源，适用于图像引导手术研究。

Abstract: Image-guided surgery (IGS) depends on accurate tracking of surgical instruments to provide real-time navigation relative to anatomical structures. Commercial stereo infrared trackers are accurate but prone to occlusion and cost-prohibitive for many settings. This work presents LARK, a multi-camera optical tracking system using commodity RGB hardware and multi-view redundancy and fusion. We develop and evaluate two complete tracking methods: multi-view monocular pose fusion and multi-view triangulation. Both methods are assessed under varying occlusion levels using a precision-machined grid and an anatomical head phantom, and compared against a gold-standard stereo infrared system. With five cameras and adaptive Kalman filtering, LARK achieves median target registration errors of 0.64 mm for point localization with triangulation and 0.73 mm for trajectory tracking with pose fusion on the machined grid. Camera-subset experiments show graceful degradation in adaptive pose-fusion accuracy as fewer views remain available. With tracking hardware costing under $1,000 USD, LARK provides a low-cost platform for image-guided surgery research. Hardware designs and software are publicly available at this https URL , and datasets at this https URL .

</details>


### [41] [AIMS: Anchor-Integrated Multi-View Synthesis for Scalable Novel View Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07566)
*JooHyun Park,HanYoung Jang,HyeongYeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AIMS提出一种可扩展的前馈式新视角合成框架，通过最远点采样选择固定数量的锚点视角、将邻近观测分组并由轻量可学习集成器融合，从而将可用观测数与全局合成模型处理视角数解耦，在RealEstate10K和ScanNet上取得29.41/17.73 dB PSNR，平均每视角渲染7.24 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式新视角合成泛化性强，但扩展到大规模输入视角集时面临困境：基于Transformer的方法联合处理所有输入视角token，计算与内存随视角数快速增长；而简单的视角下采样又会丢弃潜在有用的观测信息。作者希望在不增加全局合成模型计算预算的前提下，让更多观测贡献于合成。

Method: AIMS将可用观测数量与全局合成模型处理的视角数量解耦。首先用最远点采样(FPS)选出空间上分布均匀的固定数量锚点视角；然后将每个锚点附近的观测分组；再用一个轻量可学习的integrator将其信息融合进增强的锚点表示中。这样额外观测可持续贡献信息，同时下游全局视角预算保持固定。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和ScanNet上分别达到29.41 dB和17.73 dB PSNR，平均每视角渲染7.24 ms，相较Transformer类和高斯类基线展现出更优的质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: AIMS通过锚点选择与分组融合机制，实现了对大规模输入视角集的可扩展前馈新视角合成，在不显著增加计算开销的情况下提升合成质量，为可扩展的多视角前馈三维重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward novel view synthesis methods achieve strong generalization from posed multi-view inputs, but scaling them to large input view sets remains challenging. Transformer-based approaches that jointly process all input-view tokens incur rapidly increasing computation and memory as the number of views grows, while simple view subsampling discards potentially useful observations. We introduce Anchor-Integrated Multi-View Synthesis (AIMS), a scalable framework that decouples the number of available observations from the number of views processed by the global synthesis model. AIMS selects a fixed set of spatially distributed anchor views using farthest point sampling, groups nearby observations around each anchor, and uses a lightweight learnable integrator to fuse their information into enriched anchor representations. This allows additional observations to contribute to synthesis while keeping the downstream global view budget fixed. Evaluations on RealEstate10K and ScanNet demonstrate a favorable quality--efficiency trade-off against transformer-based and Gaussian-based baselines. AIMS achieves 29.41 dB and 17.73 dB PSNR on the two datasets, respectively, with rendering averaging 7.24 ms per view.

</details>


### [42] [CETUS: How Far Do Representations Trained on Earth Transfer to Cassini SAR of Titan?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07576)
*Kevin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CETUS 在 Cassini 土卫六 SAR 镶嵌图上比较 DINOv2、DOFA、CROMA 与经典特征及未训练 ViT 的地球到土卫六跨域表征迁移能力，用专家地貌图标签在空间分离区域做逻辑回归与微调评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星遥感缺乏大规模标注，常依赖地球数据预训练的表征迁移；但"预训练表征在行星地形分类中是否真的优于经典影像特征"缺乏受控、跨域的实证检验。作者希望在土卫六这一具有独特地貌（沙丘、平原、湖盆）的目标域中，量化预训练编码器相对传统特征与随机初始化模型的迁移价值，并揭示评测设定对排序的影响。

Method: 构建 CETUS 基准，在美国地质调查局 Cassini SAR 镶嵌图上，对比三类预训练地球编码器（DINOv2、DOFA、CROMA）与经典影像测量特征及未训练 ViT。以专家地貌图为监督标签，在空间上分离的土卫六区域训练并预测；评估逻辑回归下的特征缩放、优化器与正则化的组合敏感性，并对各编码器进行土卫六域内继续训练，观察性能变化。

Result: 逻辑回归设定下，预训练编码器平均宏 F1 高于经典特征组合；但编码器之间的排序会随缩放、优化与正则化设置共同变化而不稳定。继续在土卫六上训练可提升 DINOv2、降低 DOFA、对 CROMA 结果混杂。架构与输入处理差异使对比无法隔离预训练本身的贡献。由于专家图本身部分源于同一雷达观测，评分实质上度量与专家解释的一致性。

Conclusion: 地球到土卫六的 SAR 表征迁移有益但不稳定：预训练优于经典特征，却难以归因于预训练本身，且排序高度依赖训练设定。行星制图的表征迁移评估需关注分类器拟合与单类表现，并将结果理解为与专家解释的一致性而非绝对准确率。该框架为行星遥感跨域迁移提供了可复用的评测思路。

Abstract: Cassini synthetic aperture radar (SAR) images reveal the dunes, plains, and lake basins of Titan, providing an instance of representations learned from Earth imagery for planetary terrain classification. Cross-domain Evaluation of Earth-to-Titan Transfer Using SAR (CETUS) compares features from DINOv2, DOFA and CROMA with classical image measurements and features from an untrained vision transformer on the U.S. Geological Survey's Cassini SAR mosaic. The classifiers learn terrain labels from an expert geomorphological map and predict those labels in geographically separate Titan regions. Under logistic regression settings, pretrained encoders achieve higher mean macro F1 than the combined classical features. Encoder rankings change when feature scaling, optimization, and regularization change together. Further training on Titan improves DINOv2 performance, degrades DOFA performance, and leads to mixed results for CROMA under the tested settings. Architectural and input processing differences prevent these comparisons from isolating the effect of pretraining. Classifier fitting and performance on individual terrain classes matter when assessing representation transfer for planetary mapping. Since the map draws partly on the same radar observations, the scores measure agreement with expert interpretation.

</details>


### [43] [REViT-v2: Hierarchical Windowed Roto-reflection Equivariant ViT for Equivariant Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07585)
*Sheir A. Zaheer,Jihwan Moon,Chan Y. Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可扩展的旋转-反射群等变视觉Transformer（REViT-v2），基于窗口化群卷积自注意力与层次化特征架构，可扩展至百万参数规模并在ImageNet上训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群等变视觉Transformer在保持对称性归纳偏置的同时规模化困难，此前工作难以扩展到百万级参数和实用尺寸图像的大规模数据集。

Method: 结合窗口化群卷积自注意力与层次化特征架构，构建旋转-反射群（p4m）等变的ViT，实现参数与数据规模的扩展。

Result: 证明该方案可扩展至百万参数级群等变ViT，并在ImageNet等实际尺寸图像数据集上训练；开源代码与预训练权重。

Conclusion: 群等变ViT具备规模化可行性，为对称感知的视觉骨干提供了可用的开源基础模型选项。

Abstract: We propose a scalable roto-reflection-group-equivariant vision transformer based on windowed group-convolutional self-attention and a hierarchical feature architecture. We demonstrate that our approach can be scaled to group-equivariant vision transformers (ViTs) with millions of parameters and large datasets with practically sized images, i.e., ImageNet. The code and pretrained weights for the proposed Hierarchical Windowed Roto-reflection Equivariant ViTs (REViT-v2) are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [44] [PhysLDM: Latent Diffusion for High-Fidelity Deformable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07609)
*Yu Zhang,Xudong Xu,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysLDM 提出统一的时空隐空间扩散范式，用整体式时空 VAE 实现高保真体积可形变体的一次性长时间模拟，并系统比较了回归与扩散范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率三维体积网格的长时间预测困难：自回归方法易累积误差，原生分辨率多帧直接预测计算代价过高。作者认为需要紧凑的时空隐表示，但此类表示在基于网格的体积物理中几乎未被探索；同时确定性回归与生成式扩散哪种预测范式更合适尚不清楚。

Method: 1) 构建整体式时空 VAE，避免标准时间压缩（如常见视频 VAE）产生的"阶梯"伪影，实现最高 78 倍 token 压缩，在米级场景上达到约 2.48 mm 重建精度；2) 在该可靠隐空间上系统对比回归与扩散方法；3) 发现可形变动力学常呈混沌特性，确定性回归会输出非物理的平均结果，而扩散能更好建模其分布；4) 据此采用隐扩散模型（LDM），仅用运动学监督在 Objaverse 规模数据集上训练；5) 利用可微性求解逆问题与高阶设计优化。

Result: 单个 PhysLDM 可零样本泛化到未见过的 OOD 数据集（GSO、Toys4K）；重建精度约 2.48 mm @ 米级场景、最高 78x token 压缩；在混沌动力学下扩散优于确定性回归；可微性支持逆问题与设计优化。

Conclusion: 据称是首个面向体积可形变动力学的高保真时空自编码器与隐扩散范式，为神经模拟提供了可扩展、鲁棒的方案，并给出"混沌动力学更适合扩散而非回归"的建模洞见。

Abstract: Neural simulation of high-fidelity deformable bodies is a foundational challenge in computer graphics and physical AI. Long-horizon prediction for high-resolution 3D volumetric meshes is hard: autoregressive methods are susceptible to error accumulation, while direct multi-frame prediction at native resolution is computationally prohibitive. This motivates a compact spatiotemporal latent representation, which is largely unexplored for mesh-based volumetric physics. Meanwhile, it remains unclear whether deterministic regression or generative diffusion is the more appropriate predictive paradigm. To address these coupled challenges, we introduce PhysLDM, a unified latent-diffusion paradigm for one-shot volumetric deformable simulation. Its core is a holistic spatiotemporal VAE that avoids the "staircase" artifacts of standard temporal compression (as in common video VAEs), achieving ~2.48 mm reconstruction precision on meter-scale scenes at up to 78x token compression. Based on this reliable latent space, we systematically compare regression and diffusion methods. Our experiments uncover a key modeling insight: complex deformable dynamics are often chaotic, and in this regime deterministic regression tends to produce non-physical averages, whereas diffusion better models their distribution. Accordingly, we employ a latent diffusion model that effectively learns from the chaotic data to generate physically plausible trajectories. Trained purely kinematically on an Objaverse-scale dataset, a single PhysLDM generalizes zero-shot to unseen OOD datasets (GSO and Toys4K). Its differentiability further enables efficient solution of inverse problems and higher-order design optimization. To our knowledge, PhysLDM is the first high-fidelity spatiotemporal autoencoder and latent-diffusion paradigm for volumetric deformable dynamics, offering a scalable and robust approach to neural simulation.

</details>


### [45] [Disentangling Dual Image References in Frequency Aware Diffusion Models for Personalized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07684)
*Haipeng Liu,Yang Wang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Dual-FDM，在频域中通过掩码策略解耦双参考图（定制参考与颜色/风格参考）的不同频带，统一处理定制风格迁移与颜色风格迁移两类个性化生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化图像生成此前被拆分为前景的图像定制与背景的风格迁移，扩散模型在去噪过程中存在文本与背景（定制任务）、文本与前景（风格迁移）的对齐失效问题。作者观察到其根源是去噪过程中混合频带的相互纠缠，因此希望用双参考与频域解耦来缓解。

Method: 以双参考（定制参考、颜色与风格参考）为条件的频域感知扩散框架 Dual-FDM。定制风格迁移中，用定制参考前景的中频带替换风格参考背景的中频带；颜色风格迁移中，用颜色参考前景与背景的低频带替换风格参考背景的低频带。替换后的频带作为 key 和 value，用于重建去噪后个性化图像的前景与背景 query。

Result: 实验表明 Dual-FDM 在个性化图像生成上优于当前最先进的扩散模型（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 频域掩码解耦双参考是缓解个性化生成中文本错配的有效范式，可同时覆盖定制风格迁移与颜色风格迁移，代码已开源。

Abstract: Personalized image generation aims to synthesize text-driven images conditioned on reference images, while mainly casting the generation as image customization for foreground and style transfer for background. Previous arts of diffusion models suffers from the text misalignment with background for image customization and foreground for style transfer during the denoising process. Such facts, as we observed, rooted from the entanglement among hybrid frequency bands during the denoising process. To address such salient limitation, in this paper, we study personalized generation based on dual references - customization and color and style reference - and propose a paradigm to disentangle these Dual image references within Frequency-aware Diffusion Models, dubbed Dual-FDM, to simultaneously tackle two crucial personalized image generation tasks: customization style transfer and color style transfer, by disentangling different frequency bands via mask strategy within frequency domain. For customization style transfer, we replace the mid-frequency band of the background in the style reference with that from the foreground of the customized reference. For color style transfer, we substitute the low-frequency band of the background in the style reference with that from both the foreground and background of the color reference. Both the substituted frequency bands are used as the key and value to reconstruct the query foreground and background of the denoised personalized this http URL experiments validate the superiority of Dual-FDM over the state-of-the-art diffusion models for personalized image generation. Our code can be accessed from this https URL .

</details>


### [46] [Unlocking Fine-Grained Perception in CLIP via Structurally-Aware Latent Masked Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07689)
*Juntong Li,Lingwei Dang,Haomin Wu,Ziyan Qiu,Qingxin Xiao,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SALM: 一种无需图文对的嵌入对齐框架，通过双矩阵显式对齐与潜掩码建模隐式对齐，将局部几何先验注入CLIP，提升稠密预测与零样本能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等VLM擅长全局语义对齐但缺乏细粒度感知，制约稠密预测及MLLM视觉潜力；现有引入视觉模型几何先验的方法难以同时深度对齐局部空间结构与全局语义，甚至可能破坏原始图文空间。

Method: SALM为无监督嵌入对齐框架：1) 双矩阵对齐策略显式校准样本内空间相关性与激活强度，注入局部几何先验；2) 潜掩码建模机制引导CLIP恢复目标模型缺失的语义细节，隐式将细粒度结构聚合进全局语义空间；3) 基于CLIP浅层特征具强空间观测力的经验发现，扩展出自蒸馏范式SALM-Self，无需外部模型。

Result: 大量实验表明SALM显著提升稠密预测任务性能，同时提高CLIP零样本准确率，并有效增强MLLM的细粒度理解能力。

Conclusion: SALM通过显式与隐式双路径协同实现局部与全局对齐，在无需图文对与外部模型条件下释放CLIP内在细粒度潜力，为增强MLLM视觉感知提供高效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP excel in global semantic alignment but often lack fine-grained perceptual capabilities. This hinders dense prediction tasks and bottlenecks the visual potential of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Existing research has attempted to enhance CLIP's visual representations by incorporating geometric priors from vision-centric models. However, these strategies often struggle to achieve deep alignment for both local spatial structures and global semantics, potentially even distorting the original image-text space. To address these limitations, we propose SALM, an unsupervised embedding alignment framework based on structurally-aware latent mask modeling. SALM effectively synergizes local and global alignment via a dual-path design combining explicit and implicit mechanisms, without requiring any image-text pairs. First, we introduce a dual-matrix alignment strategy that explicitly calibrates intra-sample spatial correlations and activation intensities, thereby effectively injecting local geometric priors. Based on this, we further design a latent mask modeling mechanism to guide CLIP to restore the missing semantic details of the target model, thereby implicitly aggregating fine-grained structures into the global semantic space. Furthermore, driven by the empirical observations that CLIP's shallow features inherently possess strong spatial observational capabilities, we naturally extend SALM to a highly efficient self-distillation paradigm, SALM-Self. This unlocks CLIP's intrinsic fine-grained potential without relying on any external models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SALM not only significantly improves performance in dense prediction tasks but also boosts CLIP's zero-shot accuracy, effectively enhancing the fine-grained understanding capabilities of MLLMs. Project page at this https URL .

</details>


### [47] [Anchor-driven Multi-modal Multi-scale Expert Selection for Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07694)
*Tao Zhou,Ying Hu,Huazhu Fu,Yi Zhou,Xiao-Jun Wu,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AM²ES框架，通过可学习语义锚点对齐病理全切片图像与转录组特征，并用分层混合专家模块进行跨尺度自适应选择，实现癌症生存预测的SOTA性能与细粒度可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多模态特征直接投影到共享隐空间而缺乏显式对齐，导致形态学线索与分子信号纠缠错配；同时融合策略对WSI的极端空间异质性一视同仁，缺乏为个体患者自适应优先临床相关组织尺度的机制。

Method: 1) Anchor-driven Multi-modal Fusion（AMF）：引入可学习语义锚点作为跨模态中介，通过对转录组特征与多尺度病理表征施加结构化正则化对齐，弥合语义鸿沟；2) Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts（H-MoE）：在已对齐语义空间上解耦层级预后选择，包含尺度内专家过滤（识别各放大倍数下显著肿瘤区域）与跨尺度层级路由（动态加权选择最具信息量的分辨率层级）。

Result: 在多个TCGA癌症队列上的大量实验表明，AM²ES取得state-of-the-art性能，并通过可视化特定分子通路如何驱动跨组织尺度的专家路由决策，提供细粒度可解释性。

Conclusion: 锚点驱动的跨模态对齐结合层级专家路由，可有效缓解多模态错配与尺度异质性难题，为计算病理生存预测提供兼顾性能与可解释性的方案。

Abstract: The integrative analysis of histopathological Whole-Slide Images (WSIs) and transcriptomic profiles holds significant promise for cancer survival prediction. However, existing methods typically project multi-modal features directly into a shared latent space without explicit alignment, leading to the entanglement of mismatched morphological cues and molecular signals. Furthermore, current fusion strategies often treat the extreme spatial heterogeneity of WSIs uniformly, lacking mechanisms to adaptively prioritize clinically relevant tissue scales for individual patients. To address these limitations, we propose an Anchor-driven Multi-modal Multi-scale Expert Selection (AM$^2$ES) framework for survival prediction. Specifically, we present an Anchor-driven Multi-modal Fusion (AMF) module, which introduces learnable semantic anchors as cross-modal mediators to bridge the semantic gap by enforcing a structurally regularized alignment between transcriptomic features and multi-scale pathology representations. Built upon this aligned semantic space, we further design a Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts (H-MoE) selection module to decouple the hierarchical prognostic selection process. Mimicking the pathologist's diagnostic workflow, H-MoE performs (i) Intra-scale Expert Filtering to discriminatively identify salient tumor regions within each magnification, and (ii) Inter-scale Hierarchy Routing to dynamically weight and select the most informative resolution levels. Extensive experiments on multiple TCGA cancer cohorts demonstrate that our AM$^2$ES achieves state-of-the-art performance while offering fine-grained interpretability by visualizing how specific molecular pathways drive the expert routing decisions across tissue scales. The code will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [48] [RBMatch: Dual-Level Class Rebalancing for Semi-Supervised Building Footprint Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07698)
*Akil Ahmad Taki,Shaikh Anowarul Fattah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RBMatch 双层级类别重平衡半监督框架，同时调节伪标签生成与无监督损失优化，用于高分辨率遥感影像的建筑轮廓提取，在 WHU、INRIA、Massachusetts 数据集 1%–10% 标注比例下取得最优 IoU/F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率遥感建筑轮廓提取需要密集像素级标注，成本高昂，因此采用半监督学习利用无标注影像；但遥感场景存在严重的前景—背景类别不平衡，会偏置伪标签生成与无监督优化。作者指出仅在一个阶段做平衡不足够：只平衡伪标签选择无法阻止背景偏置在无监督损失优化阶段重新出现，称之为"imbalance leak"（不平衡泄漏）。

Method: RBMatch 采用监督学习通路加自训练模块的双层级类别重平衡框架，包含三个组件：自适应类别特定阈值（ACT）用于平衡的伪标签筛选；置信度感知的类别平衡重加权（CACBR）用于缓解无监督损失中的类别偏置；分布对齐（DAL）使无标注数据的预测分布匹配有标注数据的先验分布。

Result: 在 WHU、INRIA、Massachusetts 三个建筑轮廓数据集上，1%–10% 标注比例下 RBMatch 的建筑 IoU 与 F1 均优于所评估方法。在极度不平衡的 Massachusetts 数据集上提升最明显：1% 标注比例下 IoU 比最强基线高 1.37 个点，并且是唯一在全部十二种"数据集—比例"设置下均超过全监督基线的方法。

Conclusion: 双层级（伪标签选择 + 无监督优化）联合重平衡是缓解半监督分割中类别不平衡泄漏的有效方案，可显著降低标注需求，在遥感建筑提取任务上稳定超越半监督与全监督基线。

Abstract: Accurate building footprint extraction from high-resolution remote sensing imagery is essential for urban planning, disaster response, and environmental monitoring. However, obtaining dense pixel-level annotations is costly, motivating the use of semi-supervised learning (SSL) to leverage unlabeled imagery. In remote sensing, severe foreground--background imbalance poses a particular challenge for self-training, as it can bias pseudo-label generation and the resulting unsupervised optimization toward the majority background class. We show that addressing this imbalance at only one stage is insufficient: balancing pseudo-label selection alone does not prevent background bias from re-emerging during unsupervised loss optimization, a failure mode we term \emph{imbalance leak}. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{RBMatch}, a dual-level class-rebalancing framework that jointly regulates pseudo-label generation and unsupervised optimization. RBMatch combines a supervised learning pathway with a self-training module comprising three components: adaptive class-specific thresholding (ACT) for balanced pseudo-label selection, confidence-aware class-balanced reweighting (CACBR) for mitigating class bias in the unsupervised loss, and distribution alignment (DAL) for matching the predicted unlabeled-data distribution to the labeled-data prior. Experiments on the WHU, INRIA, and Massachusetts building footprint datasets across labeled ratios of 1%--10% show that RBMatch consistently achieves the best building IoU and F1-score among the evaluated methods. The improvement is most pronounced on the highly imbalanced Massachusetts dataset, where RBMatch improves IoU by 1.37 points over the strongest baseline at a 1% labeling ratio and is the only method to outperform the fully supervised baseline across all twelve dataset--ratio settings.

</details>


### [49] [What Frame-Level Labels Can and Cannot Do for Small-UAV Point Detection in Thermal Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07705)
*Wonbin Son,Gyumum Choi,Junil Seo,Hyungjoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文分析一种仅用帧级“存在/缺失”标签训练的小型无人机点检测架构：冻结分类阶段学到的空间特征，用相同帧标签训练读出层生成空间得分图与点检测。在CST Anti-UAV与Anti-UAV410两个红外数据集上评估定位命中率与虚警约束下的检测率，并分析训练阶段、标签分配、合成数据与模型配置的影响，另在可见光AOT数据上做应用探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机使用增长使基于图像的无人机检测需求上升。基于学习的检测器依赖图像与标注，标注类型决定训练可用信息；当传感器或场景变化导致空间标注代价过高时，仅用帧级存在/缺失标签学习定位更为可取。作者希望厘清这种弱监督点检测架构的检测能力、学习行为与应用潜力。

Method: 采用现有点检测架构，无需外部检测器：先通过分类任务训练并冻结其学到的空间特征，再用同样的帧级存在/缺失标签训练读出层，输出空间得分图与点检测。在CST Anti-UAV和Anti-UAV410两个热红外数据集上，以定位命中率和虚警约束下的检测率进行评估；开展消融分析训练阶段、标签分配、合成数据与模型配置，并与边界框检测器对比；在AOT可见光图像及帧标签上探索迁移应用。

Result: 分类训练增强了与目标相关的空间响应，读出训练有助于稳定地提取这些响应。将相近数量的标签分散到更多视频中可获得更高定位命中率；合成数据效果随数据集与评价标准而异。更高的定位命中率并不总能改善虚警约束下的检测性能；当目标信号相对背景变化较弱时，以及跨数据集迁移时，仍存在失败案例。

Conclusion: 研究为帧级弱监督无人机点检测的标签分配、空间表征与读出设计、合成数据使用及虚警控制提供了实证指导，并指出弱信号条件与跨域迁移下的剩余局限。

Abstract: The growing use of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) has increased the importance of image-based UAV detection. Learning-based detectors are trained on imagery and annotations, with annotation type determining the information available during training. We focus on learning localization from frame-level target presence/absence labels when sensor or scene changes make spatial annotations for additional training burdensome. We analyze the detection capability, learning behavior, and potential applications of an existing architecture for point detection of small UAVs, trained with presence/absence labels and requiring no external detector. The architecture freezes spatial features learned through classification and trains a readout with the same frame labels to produce spatial score maps and point detections. On two thermal infrared datasets, CST Anti-UAV and Anti-UAV410, we evaluate localization hit rates and detection rates under false-alarm constraints, analyze the effects of training stages, label allocation, synthesis, and model configuration, and compare with bounding-box detectors. We also explore potential applications on Airborne Object Tracking (AOT) using its visible-light imagery and frame labels. Classification training strengthened target-related spatial responses, while readout training helped extract them consistently. Distributing similar label counts across more videos yielded higher localization hit rates, while synthesis effects varied by dataset and evaluation criterion. Higher localization hit rates did not always improve detection under false-alarm constraints, and failures remained when target signals were weak relative to background variation and under cross-dataset transfer. These findings provide guidance on label allocation, spatial representations and readouts, synthesis, and false-alarm control.

</details>


### [50] [Comprehensive Evaluation and Fine-Tuning of Foundational Cell Nuclei Segmentation Models in Renal Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07711)
*Ruijie Wu,Junlin Guo,Ruining Deng,Yu Wang,Shilin Zhao,Haichun Yang,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究扩展了人机协同标注框架，构建了跨越易/中/难三个难度的肾脏细胞核分割数据集（5901伪标签+860专家新标注+198专家共识失败案例），系统评估了7种单源与混合源微调策略在9种分割模型配置上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肾脏病理定量分析需要准确的细胞核实例分割，但通用模型在低对比度、核密集、形态复杂和背景染色强的场景下表现不佳，需要研究如何高效利用不同难度等级的标注数据进行模型适配。

Method: 在human-in-the-loop框架下混合三类标注：Easy（5901个来自分割良好病例的foundation-model伪标签）、Medium（860个新专家标注的未解决困难病例）、Hard（198个专家标注的共识失败案例）；并在9种细胞分割模型配置上对比7种单源与混合源微调策略。

Result: 微调使所有模型均有提升，Medium数据出现在9个最佳策略中的7个；LSP-DETR在Hard-only微调下取得最高F1=0.8725；StarDist提升最大，在Medium-only微调下从0.7380升至0.8332。

Conclusion: 跨越多个难度等级的标注能有效支持模型适配，但最优标注组成依赖于具体模型，需按模型选择微调数据配比。

Abstract: Accurate nuclei instance segmentation is essential for quantitative renal pathology, yet general-purpose models often struggle with low contrast, dense nuclei, complex morphology, and strong background staining. In this work, we extended a human-in-the-loop framework by combining 5,901 foundation-model-generated pseudo-labels from well-segmented cases (Easy), 860 newly expert-annotated unresolved challenging cases (Medium), and 198 expert-annotated consensus failure cases (Hard). These annotations, spanning different levels of segmentation difficulty, enabled the systematic evaluation of seven single-source and mixed-source fine-tuning strategies across nine cell segmentation model configurations. Fine-tuning improved all models, with Medium data included in seven of the nine best-performing strategies. LSP-DETR achieved the highest F1 score of 0.8725 with Hard-only fine-tuning, while StarDist showed the largest improvement, increasing from 0.7380 to 0.8332 with Medium-only fine-tuning. These findings show that annotations spanning multiple difficulty levels support effective model adaptation, although the optimal annotation composition remains model dependent.

</details>


### [51] [RefRoute: Decoupling Conditioning Cost from References via Compact Residual Conditioning and Spatial Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07720)
*Wanning He,Yuyao Zhang,Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefRoute提出一种面向多参考图像生成的扩散Transformer框架，通过紧凑残差条件与条件/注意力路由机制，降低参考数量增长带来的token与注意力开销，并配套构建RefRoute-Data训练数据与ManyRef100基准验证可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多参考图像生成方法（如FLUX系列）将参考图编码为稠密视觉token网格并全局注意力联合处理，导致参考数量与分辨率增加时条件成本与注意力开销急剧上升，难以扩展到"多参考"（10个以上）场景。

Method: 两方面机制：(1) 紧凑残差条件——用低分辨率latent token承载全局信息，并叠加从全分辨率像素提取的轻量残差特征，在压缩参考token数的同时保留细粒度外观线索；(2) 条件路由与注意力路由——将参考token与其被分配的目标区域对齐，限制参考间交叉交互，同时允许跨越区域边界的选择性参考访问以完成场景整合。此外构建RefRoute-Data训练数据与ManyRef100基准（覆盖人物、物体、混合组合，含10–17张参考）。

Result: 经多参考微调后，RefRoute在ManyRef100上取得36.06的总体Weighted-Ref-VIEScore，而FLUX.2-Klein-9B为8.88。推理成本评估显示随参考数量增加延迟增长显著更慢：16个参考时，50步与4步配置分别相对对应FLUX基线取得18.3×与14.2×加速。

Conclusion: 紧凑参考表示结合空间路由注意力是通向可扩展多参考图像生成的有效路径。

Abstract: Multi-reference image generation requires preserving the appearance of multiple subjects while composing them into a coherent scene. However, existing diffusion transformers commonly encode references as dense visual token grids and jointly process them with global attention, making conditioning increasingly expensive as the number and resolution of references grow. We present RefRoute, a framework that addresses both reference representation cost and attention overhead through two complementary mechanisms. Compact residual conditioning combines low-resolution latent tokens with lightweight residual features extracted from full-resolution pixels, reducing reference token counts while retaining fine-grained appearance cues. Condition routing and attention routing align reference tokens with their assigned target regions and restrict cross-reference interactions, while allowing selective reference access beyond region boundaries for scene integration. We further introduce RefRoute-Data for training many-reference generation models and ManyRef100, a benchmark spanning human, object, and mixed compositions with 10-17 references. After many-reference fine-tuning, RefRoute achieves an overall Weighted-Ref-VIEScore of 36.06 on ManyRef100, compared with 8.88 for FLUX.2-Klein-9B. Separate inference-cost evaluations show substantially slower latency growth as the reference count increases: at 16 references, our 50-step and 4-step configurations achieve $18.3\times$ and $14.2\times$ speedups over their corresponding FLUX baselines, respectively. These results establish compact reference representations and spatially routed attention as an effective approach to scalable many-reference image generation.

</details>


### [52] [Structure-aware Keypoint Localization for Videofluoroscopic Swallowing Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07726)
*Kai Zhou,Chuanshen Chen,Runhao Zeng,Meng Dai,Yifan Yang,Jinwu Hu,Daiyuan Li,Mingkui Tan,Fei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VFSSKep 数据集与 S$^3$KL 半监督关键点定位框架，用于 VFSS 吞咽障碍诊断，通过结构感知学习与分块打乱的一致性学习克服固定 X 光布局导致的空间偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VFSS 自动运动学分析依赖精确解剖关键点定位，但现有工作仅覆盖有限关键点（颈椎、舌骨），忽略软腭等关键区域，且只标注吞咽活跃片段而丢弃大量非吞咽数据，数据效率低；直接套用通用半监督方法在固定布局的医学 X 光上易产生空间偏置，模型倾向于记忆绝对坐标而非理解解剖结构。

Method: 1) 构建 VFSSKep 数据集，将标注扩展至软腭并纳入大规模无标注数据；2) 提出 S$^3$KL 结构感知半监督关键点定位框架，包含 Structure-Aware Learning 策略以提取高分辨率结构线索进行结构感知表征学习，以及带块打乱的 Structural Representation Consistency Learning 策略以强制不变的结构识别。

Result: 在半监督设定下取得 SOTA 性能；仅用 25% 标注数据加无标注数据即超过使用 100% 标注的全监督学习。代码与数据将公开。

Conclusion: 面向医学 X 光固定布局的空间偏置问题，结构感知的表征与一致性约束可显著提升半监督关键点定位的数据效率，减少对全标注的依赖。

Abstract: Videofluoroscopic Swallowing Study (VFSS) is one of the gold standard for diagnosing swallowing disorders, providing dynamic X-ray imaging of the swallowing process. Automated kinematic analysis in VFSS relies fundamentally on precise anatomical keypoint localization. However, existing studies focus on limited keypoints (e.g., cervical vertebrae or the hyoid) and overlook critical regions such as the soft palate, while annotating only active swallowing segments and ignoring abundant non-swallowing data, resulting in poor data efficiency. Moreover, leveraging this unlabeled data via standard semi-supervised learning is suboptimal, as generic methods are prone to spatial bias. In medical X-rays with fixed layouts, models tend to memorize absolute coordinates rather than understanding anatomical structures. To tackle these challenges, we introduce VFSSKep, a novel dataset that extends annotations to the soft palate and incorporates large-scale unlabeled data. We further propose S$^3$KL, a Structure-aware Semi-Supervised Keypoint Localization framework designed to overcome spatial bias. It integrates a Structure-Aware Learning strategy to extract high-resolution structural cues for structure-aware representation learning, and a Structural Representation Consistency Learning strategy with block shuffling to enforce invariant structural recognition. Experiments show our method achieves state-of-the-art semi-supervised performance, even with unlabeled and 25% labeled data surpassing fully supervised learning with 100% labeled data. Code and data will be made publicly available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [53] [Foveated Compression: Selective High-Resolution Preservation for Token-Efficient VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07729)
*Donghyun Han,Jangho Park,Yuseok Bae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Foveated Compression：对全分辨率图像编码一次，用"原生分辨率+压缩分辨率"混合视觉 token 表示，其中行为自蒸馏的 Foveated Merger 保持压缩 token 与原生的兼容性，轻量 Foveated Selector 在九个空间格中选择一格保留原生分辨率并获得预算匹配的干预监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉 token 是 VLM 推理成本的主要来源，而均匀降采样是意外强的压缩基线；因此提出互补问题：在固定 token 预算下，视觉保真度应保留在何处。

Method: 单次全分辨率编码 + 混合分辨率 token 表示；行为自蒸馏的 Foveated Merger 压缩局部 token 并保持与原生 token 兼容；轻量 Foveated Selector 通过穷举、预算匹配的干预监督在 9 个空间单元中选择一个以原生分辨率保留；用预算匹配的区域选择 oracle 与匹配探测对比预填充前信号与 LM 计算后信号。

Result: 11.11% token 预算下均匀 Foveated Compression 与等 token 降采样无显著配对差异；20.99% 时学习选择器显著优于随机与固定分配，但仍低于强整体缩放，说明局部保真并非普遍更优。预算匹配区域选择 oracle 达 82.73 macro accuracy，学习选择器仅 69.61，同空间动作空间内有大量余量；匹配探测显示预测"压缩是否破坏答案"的信号在 LM 计算后远比轻量无预填充选择器更易获得。

Conclusion: 均匀重分配 token 收益有限；区域选择与压缩区域保真度构成互补瓶颈；选择器可学习但远未饱和，LM 内部信号可能为更优选择提供监督来源。

Abstract: Visual tokens are a major source of inference cost in vision-language models, yet simple image downsampling remains a surprisingly strong compression baseline. This raises a complementary question: under a fixed token budget, where should visual fidelity be preserved? We introduce Foveated Compression, which encodes a full-resolution image once and represents it with a mixture of native- and compressed-resolution visual tokens. A behaviorally self-distilled Foveated Merger compresses local visual tokens while preserving compatibility with their native counterparts, and a lightweight Foveated Selector chooses one of nine spatial cells to retain at native resolution using exhaustive budget-matched intervention supervision. At 11.11% visual tokens, uniform Foveated Compression shows no significant paired difference from iso-token downsampling. At 20.99%, the learned selector significantly outperforms random and fixed allocation, but remains below strong whole-image resizing, showing that localized fidelity is not universally preferable. A budget-matched region-choice oracle reaches 82.73 macro accuracy versus 69.61 for the learned selector, revealing substantial headroom within the same spatial action space. Matched probing further shows that signals predicting when compression breaks the answer are substantially more accessible after language-model computation than to the lightweight prefill-free selector. These results expose complementary bottlenecks in region selection and compressed-region fidelity.

</details>


### [54] [Later Is Better: Token Reduction for ViTs Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07758)
*Hyeongheon Cha,Hyungjun Yoon,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现免训练token缩减方法在分布偏移下精度下降的关键在于"缩减调度"（各层移除比例的深度分布），提出单参数"后段集中幂律调度"，在ImageNet-C上将与未压缩模型的精度差距弥补83%（26%算力节省，+1.17pp），且无需额外推理成本或调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练token缩减方法（如ToMe、EViT）虽然在干净数据上能在极低算力下恢复大部分精度，但在真实分布偏移下与未压缩模型的精度差距随移除率扩大。作者认为该差距并非不可控，而是被当作实现细节而被固定的"缩减调度"（深度方向上的移除比例分配）所主导，因此希望以零额外推理成本的方式改善OOD鲁棒性。

Method: 提出单参数"后段集中幂律调度"（late-concentrated power-law schedule）：用幂律函数刻画各层移除比例的深度分布，使缩减更多集中在网络后段，仅一个可调参数，不引入额外推理成本、无需逐输入或逐域调优。通过单层探测实验分析机制：前段缩减会扰动还需经过更多剩余层的特征，从而把缩减误差前置到深度方向。

Result: 在ImageNet-C + DeiT-S上，后段调度在26%算力削减下弥补83%的精度差距（+1.17pp），在7%削减下弥补99%（+0.26pp）。收益不能归因于保留更多token或更多算力：在与flat调度等算力下，后段调度总移除token更多、末端留存更少却仍更优。结论在5种token缩减方法（ToMe、EViT、ATS、ATC、PiToMe）、9个骨干、全部ImageNet-C腐蚀类型、8个额外偏移测试集，以及视频与视觉语言QA两种模态上均成立；在干净数据上仍为正且随严重度单调上升至严重度5时约4倍；在6种测试时自适应方法下保持增益。

Conclusion: token缩减的"调度"（深度移除剖面）是影响OOD鲁棒性的决定性但被忽视的设计维度；后段集中的幂律调度是一种通用、免训练、零额外推理成本的改进策略，广泛适用于多种缩减方法、骨干、偏移类型与模态，且与测试时自适应正交。

Abstract: Training-free token reduction accelerates vision transformers by removing redundant tokens across layers, recovering most of the original accuracy at a fraction of the compute. These methods, however, are designed and evaluated primarily on clean data, and under real-world distribution shift their accuracy gap to the uncompressed model widens with the removal rate. We show that this gap is governed by the reduction schedule, the depth profile of removal, usually left fixed as an implementation detail. Concretely, we introduce a one-parameter late-concentrated power-law schedule that consistently improves out-of-distribution accuracy over flat at no extra inference cost. On ImageNet-C with DeiT-S, the late schedule closes 83% of that gap at a 26% compute reduction (+1.17pp), and 99% of it at a lighter 7% reduction (+0.26pp). The gain cannot be attributed to retaining more tokens or using extra compute: held to flat's compute, the late schedule removes more tokens in total and leaves fewer tokens at the end, yet still wins. Single-layer probes point to a mechanism: earlier reductions perturb features that pass through more remaining layers, front-loading reduction error in depth. The effect is broad, holding across five token-reduction methods (ToMe, EViT, ATS, ATC, PiToMe), nine backbones, all ImageNet-C corruption types, eight further shift suites, and two further modalities, video and vision-language QA. It is also specific to shift, still positive on clean and rising monotonically to ~4x that at the highest severity 5. The schedule keeps its gain under six test-time adaptation methods, and needs no per-input or per-domain tuning.

</details>


### [55] [From Laboratory to Road: Evaluating Wearable Gaze Accuracy for Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07783)
*William Engel,Fabian Flohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次建立统一的实车可穿戴视线精度量化框架：41个有效实车场景中，驾驶员注视车牌，平均角度误差4.58°，利用室内标定得到的偏移量校正后降至1.10°，但偏移量随会话变化，说明BEV监督需在线重标定与条件相关的视线不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV表征在自动驾驶感知与规划中被广泛使用，但只编码"场景中有什么"，不编码"对人类驾驶员行为上重要的东西"。视线（gaze）是弥补这一缺口的理想行为信号，然而可穿戴眼动仪的空间输出常被当作真值使用，而其对头部运动、光照和标定漂移敏感，缺乏真实驾驶条件下视线精度的量化依据。

Method: 构建统一的量化框架：实车研究包含41个有效场景，驾驶员注视某车辆车牌，视线误差定义为车牌中心与眼镜估计注视方向之间的角度差；并用同一驾驶员、同一设备的多个室内实验，系统分析距离、光照、头部运动、目标运动与注视偏心率对系统性偏差和注视精度的影响；再将室内估计的偏移量应用于实车数据。

Result: 实车平均误差4.58°；施加室内录制的偏移量后降至1.10°，并使41个场景全部改善；该偏移量在不同会话间存在变化。

Conclusion: 可穿戴眼动仪在实车条件下存在显著系统偏差，通过条件化的标定可大幅降低误差；但由于偏移量随会话漂移，可靠的BEV监督很可能需要在线重标定以及条件相关的视线不确定性估计。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations have become a widely used interface between perception and planning in autonomous driving, but they encode what is in a scene, not what is behaviorally relevant to a human driver. Gaze offers a compelling behavioral signal for this gap, yet wearable eye trackers are routinely deployed as if their spatial output were ground truth, despite known sensitivity to head motion, illumination, and calibration drift. We present, to our knowledge, the first unified framework for quantifying wearable gaze accuracy under real driving conditions. Our on-road study contains 41 validated scenes in which one driver fixated a vehicle's license plate. Gaze error is measured as the angular difference between the plate center and the gaze direction estimated by the glasses. Separate indoor studies with the same driver and device systematically analyze how distance, illumination, head motion, target motion, and gaze eccentricity affect both systematic bias and gaze precision. The mean on-road error was 4.58 degrees. Applying an offset estimated from the indoor recordings reduced it to 1.10 degrees and improved all 41 scenes. Because this offset varied between sessions, reliable BEV supervision may require online recalibration and condition-dependent estimates of gaze uncertainty.

</details>


### [56] [Image-Space Refraction Correction for Underwater 3D Reconstruction: Warping Flat-Port Views into Pinhole Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07788)
*Chelim Lim,Tobias Fischer,Emilio Olivastri,Beverley Gorry,Michael Milford,Alejandro Fontan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对平面端口防水壳相机的水下折射畸变，提出图像空间的物理射线追踪折射校正，可即插即用于现有SfM/SLAM流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级相机配平面端口防水壳成本低、易获取，广泛用于珊瑚礁与海底测绘，但平面界面折射会使重建场景与相机轨迹呈碗状变形，破坏测绘与导航所需的度量精度。作者希望在重建之前、在图像空间去除主导的折射畸变。

Method: 基于物理的折射校正在图像空间进行；通过射线追踪仿真刻画折射畸变（即建模空气中相机-平面端口-水介质的光路），将畸变校正后的图像作为下游重建与SLAM算法的直接输入，方法对下游流程保持无关性（downstream-agnostic）。在两个结构不同的真实水下数据集上验证校正效果，并与传统SfM及含折射建模的SfM对比。

Result: 校正后在两个真实水下数据集上消除了重建的碗状变形；相比传统与折射SfM，能注册更多帧且保持较低重投影误差；该校正进一步泛化到多种重建与视觉SLAM后端。

Conclusion: 图像空间物理折射校正是轻量、下游无关的预处理模块，可提升平面端口水下成像的重建度量精度与鲁棒性，适用于多种重建/VSLAM流水线；局限在于聚焦静态折射几何校正，未涉及开放水域动态场景与更广泛部署验证。

Abstract: Consumer-grade cameras in flat-port housings are widely used for underwater exploration and mapping of coral reefs and seafloor habitats due to their low cost and accessibility. However, refraction at flat-port interfaces causes bowl-shaped deformation in reconstructed scenes and camera trajectories, compromising the metric accuracy required for mapping and navigation. To remove the dominant refractive distortion before reconstruction, we introduce a physics-based refraction correction in image space. Our method is downstream-agnostic: the refraction-corrected images can be directly used as input to existing reconstruction and SLAM algorithms. We characterize the refractive distortion through ray-tracing simulations and validate our correction on two real underwater datasets with differing scene structures. Compared with conventional and refractive Structure-from-Motion (SfM), our approach removes reconstruction deformation while registering more frames and maintaining low reprojection error. The correction further generalizes across diverse reconstruction and VSLAM backends, demonstrating its broad applicability to downstream vision pipelines.

</details>


### [57] [Geometry-Constrained Bidirectional Point Cloud Registration for Thin, Sheet-Like Heritage Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07793)
*Yuezhe Zhang,Lei Wei,Jingnan Du,Shuai Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何约束的双向点云配准方法，用于脆弱薄片状文物的非接触三维重建，通过PCA归一化、厚度约束和旋转假设评估解决正反面配准的旋转歧义与结构塌陷问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄片状文物脆弱，无法悬挂或贴标记，正反面必须独立采集，而两面共享几何特征少、物理约束稀缺，导致配准存在旋转歧义、不稳定和结构塌陷风险。

Method: 语义引导预处理 + PCA几何归一化 + 厚度感知配准策略；将估计的物理厚度作为几何约束保持结构完整性；通过评估有限个全局旋转假设（各用point-to-plane ICP精化），并以与厚度尺度一致的几何感知适应度准则选出最优变换。

Result: 多数情况下性能相当或更优，尤其在投影面积一致性和物理合理的前后面对齐上；厚度约束与旋转假设评估降低了正反面误翻转却数值得分虚高的退化配置风险。

Conclusion: 该方法支持脆弱薄片文物的可靠非接触数字化，为文化遗产三维采集提供实用方案，代码实现已公开。

Abstract: Non-contact three-dimensional reconstruction of thin, sheet-like heritage artifacts poses significant geometric and registration challenges. Due to their fragility, these artifacts cannot be suspended or equipped with artificial markers, necessitating independent acquisition of their front and back surfaces. Subsequent registration proves difficult due to the limited number of shared geometric features and the scarcity of explicit physical constraints, which may result in rotational ambiguity, instability, and structural collapse during iterative optimization. To address these challenges, we propose a geometry-constrained bidirectional point cloud registration method specifically tailored for thin, sheet-like heritage artifacts. The method integrates semantic-guided preprocessing, Principal Component Analysis (PCA)-based geometric normalization, and a thickness-aware registration strategy. The estimated physical thickness is incorporated as a geometric constraint to preserve structural integrity during registration. Rotational ambiguity is resolved by evaluating a finite set of global rotation hypotheses, each refined using the point-to-plane Iterative Closest Point (ICP) algorithm, with the optimal transformation selected via a geometry-aware fitness criterion consistent with the thickness scale. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves competitive or improved performance in most cases, particularly in projected area consistency and physically plausible front-back alignment. In addition, the thickness-aware constraint and rotation hypothesis evaluation reduce the risk of degenerate configurations in which the two surfaces are incorrectly flipped while still yielding deceptively acceptable numerical scores, supporting reliable non-contact digitization of delicate and thin heritage artifacts. Implementation details are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [58] [Efficient Gaussian Splatting Sequence Compression with Standard Video Codecs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07795)
*Qi Yang,Shuting Xia,Le Yang,Geert Van Der Auwera,Zhu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GSCV：一种基于视频编解码器的3D高斯泼溅（GS）序列压缩方法，通过Inter-PLAS提升帧间相关性并采用高位深GS图像管线，在压缩率与重建质量上优于MPEG视频与点云类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的GS序列压缩依赖PLAS与跟踪基元信息将GS转为平滑2D视频；但跟踪信息在实际应用中多数不可得，而直接使用原始PLAS因其随机性会导致帧间相关性弱，压缩效率下降。因此需要一个不依赖跟踪信息、仍能保证帧间一致性的GS序列压缩方案。

Method: GSCV由两部分组成：1) Inter-PLAS：一种简单高效的帧间排列方法，使GS在I帧与P帧之间生成相近图像，显著增强视频编解码器的帧间预测性能，无需跟踪基元信息；2) 新的压缩管线：基于最先进视频编解码器，对高位深（high bit-depth）GS图像进行编码，在提升可压缩性的同时提高质量上限。

Result: 实验表明GSCV在GS序列压缩任务上明显优于MPEG视频类基线与点云类基线，兼顾更高压缩率与更高质量上限；代码已开源。

Conclusion: GSCV证明在不依赖跟踪信息的前提下，通过Inter-PLAS与高位深管线设计，可有效将GS序列压缩问题转化为高效的视频编码问题，为GS序列存储与传输提供实用方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel effective Gaussian Splatting (GS) sequence Compression method that utilizes the Video codec (GSCV). Existing video-based GS sequence compression relies on the Parallel Linear Assignment Sorting (PLAS) and tracked primitive information to convert GS into smooth 2D videos. However, tracked information is not available for most practical applications, and without it, using the vanilla PLAS can generate images exhibiting weak inter-frame correlation, due to its stochastic nature. GSCV incorporates a simple yet efficient Inter-PLAS method to produce close images between the I- and P-frames of GS, enhancing the inter-frame performance of video codec greatly. GSCV also realizes a new pipeline based on the state-of-the-art video codecs with high bit-depth GS images, achieving higher compressibility while simultaneously providing a higher quality upper bound. Experimental results show that the proposed GSCV exhibits obviously improved performance over MPEG video and point cloud-based anchors in GS sequence compression. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [59] [Towards benchmarking Western Bluebird detection in the wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07802)
*Estela Monserrat Arriaga Santana,Julian Rosas Scull,Ibeth P. Alarcón,Bibiana Montoya,Aylin Sosa Mejía,Hugo Jair Escalante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个西部蓝鸲（Sialia Mexicana）检测与分割的4K野外基准数据集（6000+标注图像，41个录制会话），系统评测了监督检测器、开放词汇模型（零样本与微调）及多种分割方法，并通过诊断分析揭示失败原因由表观尺度、亮度、对比度、杂乱、模糊、拥挤和会话差异共同决定，而非仅由目标尺寸解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然环境下鸟类监测受小目标、背景杂乱、光照变化和视角差异影响，且缺乏大规模真实数据集，难以支撑行为模式研究。作者希望填补这一空白，为小目标野外生态场景的检测与分割提供基准。

Method: 构建包含41个录制会话、6000+标注4K野外图像的西部蓝鸲检测与分割基准数据集（鸟类仅占图像极小区域）；评测三类方法：（1）监督检测器（Faster R-CNN、RT-DETR等），（2）开放词汇模型在零样本与微调设置下的表现（YOLO-World等），（3）分割方法（Mask R-CNN、YOLOv8-Seg、Grounded-SAM、SAM 3）；并开展诊断分析，量化表观尺度、亮度、对比度、杂乱、模糊、拥挤及录制会话等属性对失败的影响。

Result: 监督检测器整体最可靠：Faster R-CNN取得最高检测mAP，RT-DETR在精确率-召回率权衡上最优。开放词汇模型零样本表现差，但微调后显著提升，YOLO-World可与监督方法竞争，并取得最高精确率、F1和mAP@0.5。分割方面监督方法显著优于Grounded-SAM与SAM 3：Mask R-CNN取得最高mask mAP，YOLOv8-Seg精确率最高且推理最快。诊断分析表明失败不能仅由目标尺寸解释，而是表观尺度、亮度、对比度、杂乱、模糊、拥挤与录制会话差异共同作用的结果。

Conclusion: 零样本鸟类检测在杂乱生态场景中仍十分困难，域自适应对小目标设置至关重要；监督方法在精度与稳定性上仍占优，开放词汇模型需微调才能接近实用水平。

Abstract: Bird monitoring in natural environments is challenging due to the small size of some species of birds relative to the scene, background clutter, variability in illumination, and the observers' viewpoint. Progress is further limited by the scarcity of large-scale, realistic datasets, which are essential for understanding behavioral patterns. To address this gap, we introduce a new benchmark dataset for the detection and segmentation of Western bluebirds (Sialia Mexicana), comprising over 6,000 labeled images from 41 recording sessions. The dataset features high-resolution (4K) in-the-wild images in which birds occupy only a small fraction of the image. We evaluated supervised detectors, open-vocabulary models under zero-shot and fine-tuned settings, and segmentation approaches. Supervised detectors remain the most reliable overall, with Faster R-CNN achieving the highest detection mAP and RT-DETR offering the best precision-recall trade-off. Open-vocabulary models perform poorly in zero-shot settings; however, fine-tuning substantially improves their performance, with YOLO-World becoming competitive with supervised methods and achieving the highest precision, F1-score, and mAP@0.5. For segmentation, supervised methods significantly outperform Grounded-SAM and SAM 3: Mask R-CNN achieves the highest mask mAP, while YOLOv8-Seg provides the best precision and fastest inference. A diagnostic analysis further shows that failures are not explained by object size alone, but by a combination of apparent scale, brightness, contrast, clutter, blur, crowding, and recording-session variation. Overall, our findings highlight the difficulty of zero-shot bird detection in cluttered ecological scenes and underscore the importance of domain adaptation in small-object settings.

</details>


### [60] [CHARTER: Auditing Reference Substitution in Hierarchical Compact-Evidence Evaluation for Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07843)
*Hyun Do Jung,Jungwon Choi,Soojung Choi,Yujin Oh,Hwiyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CHARTER评估章程，将病理WSI的MIL中候选筛选与参考选择显式化，通过DECLARE/QUANTIFY/AUDIT三步检验紧凑证据保真度与比较结论的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层紧凑证据管线中，候选筛选会产生"候选条件预测"，与原始全包预测不同。若评估参考改变而目标不变，同一紧凑证据的保真度度量乃至候选策略间的比较结论都可能改变，这一依赖此前是隐式的。

Method: CHARTER参考感知评估章程：(1) DECLARE声明预期目标与评估参考；(2) QUANTIFY量化候选诱导的预测偏移；(3) AUDIT审计比较结论的稳定性，要求在五种子随机K实验与原生排序压力测试下复核比较结果是否反转。

Result: 在五个种子的Random-K审计的15组比较中，4组出现确定性反转；在匹配的原生排序压力测试中，ACMIL比较由REVERSED变为PRESERVED。

Conclusion: CHARTER把隐式的候选筛选与参考选择转化为可审计的评估规范，有助于区分"预期预测的真实保持"与"改变被解释预测所造成的表观增益"。

Abstract: In digital pathology, compact evidence is often used to explain or audit predictions made by whole-slide image multiple instance learning models. In hierarchical compact-evidence pipelines, candidate filtering introduces a strategy-specific candidate-conditioned prediction alongside the original full-bag prediction. If the evaluation reference changes while the intended target remains the original full-bag prediction, however, not only can the measured fidelity of the same compact evidence change, but comparisons between competing candidate strategies can also change. To make this dependence explicit, we introduce CHARTER, a reference-aware evaluation charter that asks researchers to DECLARE the intended target and reference, QUANTIFY candidate-induced prediction shift, and AUDIT the stability of comparative conclusions. Across the 15 comparisons in our main five-seed Random-K audit, 4 showed determinate reversals; in a matched native-ranking stress test, the ACMIL comparison changed from REVERSED to PRESERVED. CHARTER turns otherwise implicit candidate-filtering and reference choices into an auditable evaluation specification, helping distinguish genuine preservation of the intended prediction from apparent gains induced by changing the prediction being explained.

</details>


### [61] [CueRator: Agentic Search for Symbolic Rules to Adapt Frozen Multimodal Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07868)
*Sunchan Park,Beomkwon Cho,Kyeongbo Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CueRator，用多智能体离线搜索决策规则公式（符号化保证泛化），并用轻量策略网络按视频预测公式参数（保证适应），在不训练编码器的情况下提升开放词汇视听事件感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇视听事件感知方法在"可适应性"与"对未见类别的泛化性"之间存在权衡：可训练模块适应性强但泛化差，固定规则泛化好但不能适应。作者希望同时获得两者，且不微调冻结的对比多模态编码器。

Method: CueRator 是代理式框架：用符号公式把冻结的跨模态相似度映射为预测（保证泛化），再用一个轻量 policy 按视频预测公式参数（保证适应）。公式的发现由"报告引导的多智能体循环"离线完成，每个候选公式同时以"表达上限"和"可训练策略能否实现"两种信号评估。在 OV-AVEBench 等视听事件感知任务上验证，并做消融。

Result: 在 OV-AVEBench 上，总平均从 57.8 提升至 60.2，未见类别从 55.8 提升至 59.9，seen-unseen 差距由 7.1 缩小到 1.2；另两个视听事件感知任务上也优于各自基线，且发现的规则在换编码器时只需重训 policy 仍具竞争力。消融显示公式与策略均有贡献，两种反馈信号缺一不可。

Conclusion: 符号公式 + 轻量适应策略的组合可打破"适应-泛化"权衡，多智能体搜索是发现此类规则的有效手段，且发现的规则具备跨编码器可迁移性。该范式可推广到其他需要跨模态决策规则的开放词汇感知任务。论文未涉及机器人本体实验，属于感知侧决策规则发现，对齐具体化策略仍有距离。

Abstract: Large language model agents have been used to search over symbolic structures such as programs and equations. We propose CueRator, an agentic framework for policy-aware decision-rule discovery, which adapts frozen contrastive multimodal encoders by searching for the decision rule that converts their cross-modal similarities into predictions. We validate it on open-vocabulary audio-visual event perception, where existing methods involve a trade-off between adaptivity and generalization to unseen categories: trained modules adapt at the cost of generalization, and fixed rules the reverse. The framework pairs a symbolic formulation for generalization with a lightweight policy that predicts its parameters per video for adaptivity. A report-guided multi-agent loop discovers the formulation offline, evaluating each candidate on its expressive ceiling and on whether a trained policy can realize it. On OV-AVEBench, CueRator raises the total average from 57.8 to 60.2 and unseen-category performance from 55.8 to 59.9 over the best existing method, reducing the seen-unseen gap from 7.1 to 1.2. Ablations attribute the gains to both the formulation and the policy and show that both feedback signals are necessary for effective search. CueRator also improves over the respective baselines on two further audio-visual event perception tasks, and the discovered rule remains competitive across encoders with only the policy retrained. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [62] [Revar3r: gauge-aware perturbation uncertainty for feed-forward 3d reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07883)
*Sammam Mahdi,Fariha Binta Salim,Rakin Bin Rabbani,Aniqua Nusrat Zereen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReVar3R：在冻结的3R模型（VGGT/π³/MASt3R）输出上先对齐到公共相似变换帧再计算逐点方差，无需重训练即可得到误差无关的鲁棒不确定性，多数条件下优于内置置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练扰动式不确定性存在缺陷：当输出含未观测对称性（如整体相似变换）时，运行间差异反映的是对称性而非真实误差；而内置置信度在多条件下性能不足，训练式evidential head需模型专属监督。对point map而言还存在随场景尺度增长、与误差无关的闭式方差项，可能淹没真实信号。

Method: 理论推导point map中误差无关、随‖x_p‖²增长的闭式方差项，并用仿真复现该效应（30组真实VGGT视角集均呈现该特征）。ReVar3R在计算逐点方差前，将预测鲁棒地配准到公共相似变换帧，从而消除整体对称性带来的虚假方差；不改动、不重训冻结模型。可选的标定与融合使用留出划分。

Result: 在VGGT、π³、MASt3R三个骨干与六个数据集上，同一估计器在18个条件中的15个将AUSE降至内置置信度以下。分阶段评估：免标签核心11/18胜出，免标签等权融合12/18，留出权重14/18，纳入内置信号后15/18。与训练式evidential head相比为权衡：后者幅值标定更好且在其训练域领先，ReVar3R则跨骨干免适配迁移。其排序可改进点云滤波，但无法检测稳定的系统性偏置、不助力新视角合成、且标定不跨域迁移。

Conclusion: ReVar3R为冻结3R模型提供免训练、跨骨干的不确定性估计，核心贡献是指出并消除相似变换对称性导致的虚假方差，在多数条件下匹配或超越内置置信度与训练式方法，但受限于无法识别系统性偏置、不适用于新视角合成、标定不可跨域迁移。

Abstract: A correctly reconstructed distant point appears uncertain even when a frozen 3D model processes equivalent inputs because its output frame rotates fractionally. This exposes a weakness of trainingfree perturbation uncertainty: when outputs contain an unobserved symmetry, run-to-run variation potentially reflects symmetry rather than error. Existing alternatives have trade-offs: built-in confidence is outperformed in most evaluated conditions, while trained evidential heads require modelspecific supervision. For point maps, this research derives a closed-form, error-independent variance term that grows with scene extent and potentially overwhelms the desired signal. Simulation reproduces the effect; all 30 real VGGT view-sets tested exhibit its predicted $\|x_p\|^2$ signature. ReVar3R robustly registers predictions to a common similarity frame before computing per-point variance, without retraining or modifying the frozen model. Optional calibration and fusion use a held-out split. Across VGGT, {\pi}3, and MASt3R on six datasets, the same estimator on every backbone lowers AUSE below built-in confidence in 15 of 18 conditions. The staged evaluation yields 11 of 18 wins for the label-free core, 12/18 for label-free equal-weight fusion, 14/18 with held-out weights, and 15/18 when the built-in signal is included. Against a trained evidential head, the result is a trade-off: the head calibrates magnitude better and leads in its training domain, whereas ReVar3R transfers across backbones without adaptation. Its ranking improves point filtering, but it does not detect stable systematic bias, aid novel-view synthesis, or transfer calibration across domains.

</details>


### [63] [Unsupervised Long-Tailed Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07903)
*Keliang Chen,Yaxin Hou,Hui Liu,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文形式化"无监督长尾适配"(ULTA)新场景,揭示该场景下头部类性能骤降的反常现象,并提出 MARS 方法(边界保持对齐 + 间隔感知自精炼)加以解决,在9个基准上平均提升4.71个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型(VLM)无监督适配方法普遍假设未标注数据分布是均匀的,导致伪标签分布也均匀。然而真实数据分布往往呈长尾形态,这一分布错配使得适配后头部类边界被侵蚀、头部样本被推入易混淆类别,进一步放大模型固有偏差。作者因此定义 ULTA 场景以填补这一空白。

Method: 提出 MARS(Margin-Aware Refinement with Structural alignment)。其一是 Boundary-Preserving Alignment:以零样本 VLM 作为固定视觉参照,抑制训练目标中缺乏视觉支持的概率上升,从而缓解头部类边界侵蚀;其二是 Margin-aware Self-Refinement:采用动态调整策略精炼尾部类与易混淆类,同时防止预测偏差;整体结合结构对齐与间隔感知机制。

Result: 在九个基准数据集上,MARS 优于现有最先进方法,平均准确率提升 4.71 个百分点,并验证其对头部类边界退化与尾部/混淆类的双重改善效果。

Conclusion: 该工作明确了无监督长尾适配这一新问题,指出现有均匀分布假设的失效机制,并给出可推广的边界保持与间隔感知精炼框架,为 VLM 在真实长尾数据上的适配提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Adapting vision-language models to downstream tasks has achieved remarkable success by leveraging pseudo-labels generated from unlabeled data. Existing methods typically assume a uniform unlabeled data distribution, and thus the resulting pseudo-label distribution is likewise uniform. However, real-world data distributions are often long-tailed. To tackle this, we formalize a new scenario termed Unsupervised Long-Tailed Adaptation (ULTA). Under this scenario, existing methods exhibit a contrasting phenomenon: head-class performance drops sharply, which is distinct from supervised long-tailed learning where tail classes suffer the most. In particular, we uncover that the distributional mismatch not only erodes head-class boundaries, but also pushes head samples into confusable classes, reinforcing the model's inherent bias. To address these issues, we propose a novel model called Margin-Aware Refinement with Structural alignment (MARS). Specifically, we mitigate head-class boundary erosion via Boundary-Preserving Alignment, which takes the zero-shot VLM as a fixed visual reference to suppress probability increases that lack visual support in the training targets. Building upon this, we introduce Margin-aware Self-Refinement, which employs a dynamic adjustment strategy to refine tail and confusable classes while preventing prediction bias. Extensive experiments on nine benchmark datasets demonstrate that MARS outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 4.71 percentage points.

</details>


### [64] [Diverse Motion Customization via Control-based Dynamic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07911)
*Youngyoon Choi,Kihyun Kim,Jeongwoo Shin,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于控制的运动定制框架 CMC，用随机最优控制（SOC）重构学习目标以抑制参考视频的外观泄漏，训练加速 2.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成中的运动定制存在"内容泄漏"问题：参考视频的外观属性会无意迁移到生成结果中。作者将其归因于把学习目标直接建模为对参考视频的回归，导致生成过程坍缩到参考视频。因此需要一个结构上对内容泄漏鲁棒的训练框架。

Method: 提出 Control-based Motion Customization（CMC）。核心思路是用随机最优控制（SOC）形式化问题，将生成动力学引导向目标运动、同时避免向参考视频坍缩；使定制视频获得目标运动但仍落在预训练模型的提示条件分布内，外观由文本提示而非参考视频决定。为提升效率，将 SOC 针对运动定制场景简化：去除显式奖励项，并引入仅作用于早期生成阶段的时间步自适应运动代价。

Result: 训练加速 2.5 倍；大量实验表明 CMC 有效缓解内容泄漏，在多样场景下达到有竞争力的运动保真度，并保持基座模型的生成多样性。

Conclusion: 将运动定制从"参考回归"重构为受控生成动力学问题是缓解内容泄漏的原则性方案，兼顾运动保真度与基座多样性，并显著降低训练成本。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in video generation, motion customization remains challenging due to content leakage, where appearance attributes from the reference video unintentionally propagate into the generated output. We identify this issue as a consequence of the generative process collapsing toward the reference video, which arises from formulating the learning objective as a direct regression on the reference. To address this, we propose Control-based Motion Customization (CMC), a principled training framework that is structurally robust to content leakage. Our key idea is to steer generative dynamics toward desired motion while avoiding collapse toward the reference video, which we formalize using Stochastic Optimal Control (SOC). Under this formulation, customized videos acquire the target motion yet remain within the pre-trained model's prompt-conditional distribution, where appearance is determined by the text prompt rather than the reference video. Furthermore, to improve efficiency, we tailor the SOC formulation to motion customization by eliminating the need for an explicit reward and introducing a timestep-adaptive motion cost that focuses only on early generative stages, accelerating training by 2.5 times. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CMC effectively mitigates content leakage and achieves competitive motion fidelity while preserving the diversity of the base model across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [65] [Multimodal Knowledge Distillation for Gastric Adenocarcinoma Classification from Whole-Slide Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07913)
*Shrihari Dumbre,Bikash Santra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态知识蒸馏(MKD)框架，用低秩多模态融合(LMF)在训练时整合病理报告文本与全切片图像，使学生模型仅用图像即可准确进行胃腺癌亚型分类，在PatchGastric基准上平均准确率至少提升3.35%，且不依赖Transformer融合、多任务学习或大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃腺癌(GA)是全球癌症致死的主要原因之一，从全切片图像(WSI)中进行准确的病理亚型分类对治疗决策至关重要。现有多模态方法虽能结合病理报告文本改善分类，但普遍依赖计算开销大的Transformer架构和大型语言模型，部署与推理成本高。作者希望以轻量方式利用临床文本的监督信号，同时保证推理阶段仅需图像输入。

Method: 1) 将每张WSI表示为补丁包(bag of patches)，并配对切片级诊断描述(caption)；2) 使用预训练的WSI图像编码器与临床文本编码器；3) 通过低秩多模态融合(LMF)高效建模跨模态交互；4) 教师模型学习融合的图像-文本表征用于亚型分类；5) 学生模型通过知识蒸馏获得该能力，实现仅图像输入的推理。整体不使用Transformer融合、多任务学习或大语言模型。

Result: 在PatchGastric基准数据集上，平均准确率相比当前最优方法至少高出3.35%，同时避免了Transformer融合、多任务学习与大语言模型带来的计算负担。源代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过低秩多模态融合与知识蒸馏的组合，可以在不引入重型架构的前提下有效利用病理文本监督来提升WSI亚型分类性能，并保持推理阶段仅依赖图像的高效部署特性。

Abstract: Gastric adenocarcinoma (GA) is a leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide, and accurate histopathological subtype classification from whole-slide images (WSIs) is essential for effective treatment planning. While multimodal approaches that integrate pathology report text with WSIs can improve classification, existing methods often depend on computationally expensive transformer architectures and large language models. We propose a multimodal knowledge distillation (MKD) framework that combines a pretrained WSI image encoder and a clinical text encoder using Low-Rank Multimodal Fusion (LMF) to efficiently model cross-modal interactions during training. Each WSI is represented as a bag of patches paired with a slide-level diagnostic caption. The teacher model learns fused image-text representations for subtype classification, while the student model distills this knowledge to enable accurate image-only inference. We evaluate our method on the PatchGastric benchmark dataset and achieve at least 3.35% higher mean accuracy than state-of-the-art approaches, without relying on transformer-based fusion, multi-task learning, or large language models. The source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [66] [Can We Model the Artifacts Explicitly? Disentangle Artifacts via Pairwise Edit Relations for Image Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07916)
*Xuekang Zhu,Kaiwen Feng,Ruifeng Wang,Xiwen Wang,Xiaochen Ma,Bo Du,Changjiang Jiang,Chenfan Qu,Songyu Ye,Xia Du,Wentao Feng,Jian Liu,Ji-Zhe Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将图像篡改定位（IML）重新表述为潜变量问题，提出两阶段PAL+SL学习范式与EditGroup-45K数据集，通过特征解耦显式建模伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IML方法将任务视为完全监督的掩码估计，隐式建模伪影，作者认为这导致模型能力不足，因此需显式建模伪影潜变量z。

Method: 将IML改写为P(y|x)=∫P(y|z)P(z|x)dz；用两阶段范式（Pairwise Artifacts Learning估计P(z|x)、Standard Localization估计P(y|z)）依托编辑关系进行特征解耦；并构建按编辑组组织的source-anchored数据集EditGroup-45K支持配对构造。

Result: 在多种IML架构上PAL带来一致提升，实证分析表明其确实通过特征解耦显式捕获伪影；代码与数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 将IML重构为潜变量问题并显式建模伪影是有效方向，PAL具架构无关的通用性。

Abstract: Image Manipulation Localization (IML) is commonly formulated as a fully supervised learning task that estimates the optimal manipulation mask $y$ for a given image $x$. In this work, we first reveal the latent nature of artifacts and thus reinterpret IML as a latent-variable problem, $P(y|x)=\int P(y|z)\,P(z|x)\,dz$, where $z$ denotes the artifacts. Following this interpretation, we pinpoint the cause for the current IML models' insufficiency as their implicit artifacts modeling strategy, highlighting the necessity of modeling $z$ in an explicit manner. Without direct labels, feature disentanglement is the most appropriate solution for this explicit modeling. Accordingly, we propose a two-stage learning paradigm with the Pairwise Artifacts Learning (PAL) and Standard Localization (SL) phases to estimate $P(z|x)$ and $P(y|z)$ via edit relations. To support our edit-relation-based learning, we further curate EditGroup-45K, a source-anchored dataset organized into edit groups for pair construction. Extensive experiments show that our PAL paradigm yields consistent improvements across diverse IML architectures, and empirical analyses further verify that PAL does capture artifacts explicitly through feature disentanglement. Code and dataset are available at this https URL

</details>


### [67] [TF-PRVR: Training-Free Partially Relevant Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07925)
*Giyeol Kim,Chanho Eom*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TF-PRVR：首个免训练的部分相关视频检索框架，利用冻结视觉-语言特征构建视频自适应层次表示并做多尺度图传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分相关视频检索（PRVR）方法存在两个关键问题：固定的视频切分方案导致语义稀释；任务特定训练造成源域过拟合。

Method: 冻结的视觉-语言特征构建视频特有的层次表示；从帧级特征提取时序语义信号，用基于频率的多尺度分析自适应确定时序边界，得到事件级语义连贯的分层片段；在此基础上构建统一多尺度图，在时序与语义相关节点间传播查询相关性；再用时刻感知评分策略跨尺度聚合时序对齐的相关性，强化一致支持的片段、抑制孤立虚假响应。全程不做任务特定训练。

Result: 在视觉与时序特征多样的多个数据集上取得一致性能表现，说明免训练 PRVR 是可行方向。

Conclusion: 免训练框架保留了预训练视觉-语言模型的通用对齐能力，避免数据集特化过拟合，为 PRVR 提供了实用方向。

Abstract: Partially Relevant Video Retrieval (PRVR) aims to retrieve untrimmed videos containing moments relevant to a given text query. Despite recent progress, existing PRVR methods suffer from two key limitations: a fixed video decomposition scheme that causes semantic dilution, and source-domain overfitting induced by task-specific training. In this paper, we propose TF-PRVR, the first training-free framework for PRVR. TF-PRVR leverages frozen vision-language features to construct video-specific hierarchical representations. It derives temporal semantic signals from frame-level features and applies frequency-based multi-scale analysis to identify adaptive temporal boundaries, producing hierarchical segments with coherent event-level semantics.
