Abstract: Image Bitstream Fine-grained Understanding (IBFU) aims to directly perform fine-grained classification and semantic description generation from encoded image byte sequences. In contrast to conventional pixel-domain visual understanding, IBFU conducts semantic analysis without fully decoding images into the pixel domain. Since pixel-level visual content is not explicitly reconstructed during inference, this paradigm reduces visual exposure within the processing pipeline and suits privacy-friendly Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) applications. In this paper, we propose Bitstream Fine-grained Generator (BFG), a novel foundation model tailored for IBFU. BFG consists of two main components: a Bitstream Semantic Encoder (BSeE) and a Fine-grained Semantic Generator (FSeG). BSeE directly models semantic representations from encoded image bitstreams without explicit pixel reconstruction, while FSeG transforms the extracted bitstream semantics into detailed natural-language descriptions through autoregressive generation. To train BFG and comprehensively evaluate IBFU in practical AIoT scenarios, where image bitstreams may suffer corruption during transmission and storage, we construct a large-scale Corrupted-bitstream Fine-grained Understanding dataset (CFU-D), containing both intact bitstreams and corrupted variants across multiple corruption types and severity levels. Experiments show that BFG maintains stable fine-grained caption generation under bitstream corruption. For example, the performance only has slight change from 0.6339 to 0.6077 in terms of average CIDEr score on Stanford Dogs Caption dataset, while vision-language models, such as Qwen-VL-Chat, BLIP-2, GLM, Gemini, and GPT suffer severe performance decrease. This paper provides a practical paradigm for privacy-friendly fine-grained understanding in AIoT.

</details>


### [101] [Ariadne's Thread of LipSync: Unraveling Forgeries via Inconsistency between Lip Motions and Head Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08417)
*Tianyi She,Jiawei Liu,Weifeng Liu,Hanqing Zhao,Weiming Zhang,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LipDA：利用自然语音中唇动与头部姿态的生物学耦合不一致性，实现LipSync伪造检测与生成模型溯源归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LipSync生成技术已能实现高真实度换脸视频，社会风险严重。而现有防御方法失效，原因在于先进生成器既提升了唇同步质量又消除了视觉伪影；作者指出这些方法忽略了自然语音中唇动与头部姿态之间固有的生物学耦合关系。

Method: 提出LipDA统一框架，同时进行检测与归因。检测端通过对比真实vs伪造视频中的唇部特征与姿态特征，学习量化二者的不一致性（discrepancy）；归因端建模各生成模型特有的时序动态与音视频同步模式，作为模型指纹实现源追踪。此外构建了大规模多生成器数据集LipSync-A。

Result: 在两个公开的困难LipSync数据集及自建多生成器数据集上，检测AUC超过97%，模型归因准确率达97.5%，显著优于现有方法；代码与数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 唇-头生物学耦合是一种被忽视的、跨生成器鲁棒的伪造线索，可同时支撑检测与归因任务，为深度伪造防御提供了新的多模态生理先验视角。

Abstract: Recent advances in LipSync generation technology have led to the creation of highly realistic videos, posing severe societal risks. However, existing defense strategies struggle against LipSync forgeries, as advanced LipSync generation methods not only achieve better lip synchronization but also eliminate visual artifacts. An important reason is that they overlook an inherent biological coupling between lip movements and head poses in natural speech videos. In this paper, we propose LipDA, a novel framework for joint LipSync Detection and Attribution, which takes advantage of the inconsistency between head and lip. For detection, the framework learns to quantify this discrepancy by contrasting lip and pose features from authentic versus forged videos. For attribution, our method is designed to capture the unique temporal dynamics and audio-visual synchronization patterns that act as the fingerprint of models, enabling source tracing. We conduct extensive experiments on two challenging LipSync datasets as well as our own proposed large-scale and multi-generator dataset. LipDA achieves over 97\% AUC in detection and 97.5\% accuracy in model attribution, significantly outperforming existing methods. Code and the proposed LipSync-A dataset are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [102] [From the Drosophila Visual Connectome to General-Purpose Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08418)
*Zongyu Li,Akito Yamauchi,Huaizhi Liu,Vishwanatha Rao,Jia Guo,Frontotemporal Lobar Degeneration Neuroimaging Initiative,Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出ConnectomeX/FlyVision，一种保留果蝇视觉连接组中并行ON/OFF通路、循环计算与群体图交互的可训练架构，在MNIST、CIFAR-10、ImageNet-1K、皮肤疾病与胸片分类以及MRI脑龄预测上以极小参数量逼近或超越ResNet18。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物连接组编码了视觉计算的结构化解法，可能为人工视觉提供可复用的归纳偏置；作者希望将连接组启发的计算从紧凑任务扩展到大规模自然与生物医学视觉。

Method: 以FlyVision为核心构建ConnectomeX：保留并行ON/OFF处理、循环计算和群体级图交互，并可跨任务扩展容量；另提出BrainAGE，将共享的ImageNet预训练FlyVision Large编码器应用于每例扫描的24张矢状、冠状、轴状切片，通过置信度调制的Gaussian voting融合切片级年龄估计（三轴融合）。

Result: MNIST 99.34%（80,608参数）、CIFAR-10 78.03%（81,408参数）；ImageNet-1K上FlyVision Base/Large 60.79%/66.25% top-1（1.8M/3.7M参数），Large local-k7+低频分支66.53%，对比ResNet18 69.25%（11.7M）；22类皮肤病63.78%准确率、95.28% macro-AUROC（2.99M）；四类胸片92.60%/92.76%（1.33M/2.97M）优于ResNet18 91.56%（11.18M）；433例留出扫描上BrainAGE MAE 5.98年、R²=0.868。

Conclusion: 连接组启发的守恒计算可从紧凑识别扩展到大规模自然与生物医学视觉，在参数远少于ResNet18的情况下保持接近甚至更优的性能。

Abstract: Biological connectomes encode structured solutions to visual computation that may provide reusable inductive biases for artificial vision. We develop ConnectomeX around FlyVision, a trainable architecture that preserves parallel ON/OFF processing, recurrent computation and population-level graph interaction while scaling model capacity across tasks. FlyVision reached 99.34% accuracy on MNIST with 80,608 parameters and 78.03% on CIFAR-10 with 81,408 parameters. On ImageNet-1K, FlyVision Base and Large reached 60.79% and 66.25% top-1 accuracy with 1.8 and 3.7 million parameters, while a Large local-k7 model with a learned low-frequency branch reached 66.53%, compared with 69.25% for ResNet18 with 11.7 million parameters. On a 22-class skin-disease benchmark, FlyVision Large achieved 63.78% accuracy and 95.28% macro-AUROC with 2.99 million parameters. In four-class chest radiography, ImageNet-pretrained FlyVision Base and Large reached 92.60% and 92.76% accuracy with 1.33 and 2.97 million parameters, compared with 91.56% for ImageNet-pretrained ResNet18 with 11.18 million. BrainAGE extends FlyVision to volumetric T1-weighted MRI by applying a shared ImageNet-pretrained FlyVision Large encoder to 24 sagittal, coronal and axial slices per scan and combining slice-level age estimates by confidence-modulated Gaussian voting. On 433 held-out scans, three-axis fusion achieved a mean absolute error of 5.98 years and R^2 = 0.868. Across the 224x224 classification tasks, the best FlyVision configuration remained within three percentage points of ResNet18 on ImageNet-1K and skin-disease classification and exceeded it on chest radiography with substantially fewer parameters. These results show that a conserved connectome-informed computation can scale from compact recognition to large-scale natural and biomedical vision.

</details>


### [103] [Deformable CT-US Registration via Anatomy-Aware Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08419)
*Agnieszka Lach,Magdalena Wysocki,Feng Li,Mohammad Farid Azampour,Benjamin D. Killeen,Felix Ginzinger,Mathias Braun,Philipp Steininger,Heinz Deutschmann,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于SIREN隐式神经表示的CT-超声可变形配准框架，利用CT HU值导出的组织刚度作空间变化正则，并加入探头接触位移先验与扇形几何约束，采用NGF损失逐帧优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助微创干预中，光学跟踪只能提供初始刚性配准，但探头接触会造成软组织形变，导致对齐失准，需要引入CT解剖先验来约束可变形配准。

Method: 机器人辅助光学跟踪建立初始刚性配准；随后逐帧优化正弦隐式神经表示（SIREN）拟合可变形场。由CT HU值近似组织刚度作为空间变化正则（骨等刚性结构抑制形变，软组织更柔顺）；另加两项物理约束：接触区位移先验（使探头下方组织受压）与基于波束方向及凸阵探头视场的扇形几何正则项；以归一化梯度场（NGF）优化参数。

Result: 相比刚性初始化对齐精度提升17%，优于经典可变形配准基线，且拓扑折叠近乎为零。

Conclusion: 将CT解剖先验与探头接触物理约束嵌入隐式神经可变形配准，可在机器人辅助超声介入中有效抑制伪形变并提升配准精度。

Abstract: Slice-to-volume registration between ultrasound (US) and preoperative computed tomography (CT) imaging would enhance many minimally invasive interventions, for example by locating soft tissue structures intra-operatively that are discernible in CT. While optical tracking enables initial rigid registration, contact from the probe induces soft tissue deformations that inhibit accurate alignment. In this work, we introduce a deformable CT-ultrasound registration framework that incorporates anatomical priors derived from CT to improve registration under deformation. Rigid registration is first established using a robot-assisted optical tracking system, after which a deformable transformation is estimated using a sinusoidal implicit neural representation (SIREN) optimized per frame. Tissue stiffness is approximated from CT-based HU values and used as spatially varying regularization, suppressing deformation in rigid structures such as bone while allowing more flexibility in soft tissue. Two additional constraints capture the physics of probe contact: a contact-zone displacement prior that drives the displacement field to compress tissue below the probe face, and a fan-geometry regularization term based on beam direction and convex transducer field of view. Model parameters are optimized with a normalized gradient field (NGF). The proposed approach improves alignment over rigid initialisation by 17% and outperforms classical deformable baselines while maintaining near-zero topological folding.

</details>


### [104] [HuC-VideoMAE: Human-Centric Video Masked Autoencoding from synthetic data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08433)
*Ricardo Pizarro,Roberto Valle,José M. Buenaposada,Luis M. Bergasa,Luis Baumela*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作研究在合成人体运动数据集上对视频Transformer进行自监督预训练，提出基于人体关键点和边界框的人本掩码策略，在NTU RGB+D和Toyota-Smarthome上显著优于标准VideoMAE，缩小了与Kinetics预训练差距的49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kinetics-700等网络爬取视频数据集存在受试者未同意的伦理问题，而BEDLAM2.0等基于动捕的合成视频数据提供了伦理替代方案，但直接在合成数据上做标准VideoMAE掩码预训练效果远逊于Kinetics预训练，需针对性改进。

Method: 提出以人为中心的掩码方案：利用人体关键点与人体边界框区域引导掩码，使模型在预训练阶段聚焦人体运动的结构与动态，替代VideoMAE的统一随机管状掩码。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D与Toyota-Smarthome上，该方法显著优于合成数据上的标准VideoMAE预训练；在NTU RGB+D跨视角-跨主体设置下，无需任何真实帧即缩小了与Kinetics预训练的49%差距；预训练模型将公开。

Conclusion: 人体先验引导的掩码可有效提升合成数据预训练的视频Transformer迁移性能，为伦理合规的动作识别提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Modern action recognition models rely on video transformers pretrained on massive collections of web-crawled videos, such as Kinetics-700. However, the use of such data raises ethical concerns, as subjects' consent is typically not obtained. Recent high-quality synthetic video datasets generated from motion-capture data, such as BEDLAM2.0, offer a promising ethical alternative. In this work, we investigate self-supervised pretraining of video transformers on synthetic human-motion datasets. We first show that directly applying the standard VideoMAE masking strategy leads to substantially worse performance than pretraining on Kinetics. To address this limitation, we propose a human-centric masking scheme that leverages body keypoints and person bounding box regions. Our approach encourages the model to focus on the structure and dynamics of human motion during pretraining. Experiments on NTU RGB+D and Toyota-Smarthome demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms standard VideoMAE pretraining on synthetic data, closing 49% of the gap to Kinetics pretraining on NTU RGB+D cross-view-subject without using a single real frame during pretraining. To promote the use of ethical action recognition models, we will publicly release our pretrained models.

</details>


### [105] [Knee3DVLM: Dual-Sequence Full-Volume Vision-Language Modeling for Comprehensive Knee MRI Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08482)
*Maryam Baizhigitova,Andrew Seohwan Yu,Po-Hao Chen,Naveen Subhas,Sixu Chen,Xinxin Wang,Kunio Nakamura,Richard Lartey,Xiaojuan Li,Mingrui Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Knee3DVLM：面向膝关节MRI的双序列（DESS+TSE）全容积视觉-语言模型，预测57个MOAKS派生二分类诊断目标，融合配置在1,074例测试集上取得最佳平均准确率72.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在三维医学影像中的应用日益增多，但在膝关节MRI上的应用仍有限，尤其难以处理临床中互补序列（结构敏感DESS与液体敏感TSE）的联合解读；同时缺少能输出结构化、解剖定位报告的系统。

Method: 提出序列感知的Knee3DVLM，输入全容积DESS与液体敏感TSE MRI，输出源自MOAKS的57个解剖分区的二分类诊断目标，用于结构化报告；在Osteoarthritis Initiative受试者不相交划分上对比DESS单序列、TSE单序列与DESS-TSE配对三种配置，并做与3DReasonKnee队列对齐的多分类二级分析。

Result: 在1,074例留出检查中，融合模型平均准确率72.98%、平衡准确率71.17%、平均ROC-AUC 78.96%、宏平均ROC-AUC 78.74%，均为三种配置中最优；多分类分析中五个病理类别上数值上优于已报道的最强3DReasonKnee配置。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用双序列全容积建模进行全面的膝关节MRI评估，序列互补性对结构化诊断预测有增益。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being applied to three-dimensional medical imaging, but their application to knee MRI remains limited, particularly for interpreting the complementary sequences used in clinical practice. We introduce Knee3DVLM, a sequence-aware VLM that uses full-volume DESS and fluid-sensitive TSE MRI to predict 57 anatomically resolved binary diagnostic targets derived from the MRI Osteoarthritis Knee Score (MOAKS) for structured reporting. We evaluated DESS-only, TSE-only, and paired DESS-TSE configurations using subject-disjoint Osteoarthritis Initiative partitions. In a held-out cohort of 1,074 examinations, the fused model achieved 72.98% average accuracy, 71.17% balanced accuracy, 78.96% mean ROC-AUC, and 78.74% macro ROC-AUC, the highest values among the three configurations. In a secondary multiclass analysis aligned with the released 3DReasonKnee cohort, Knee3DVLM was numerically higher than the strongest reported 3DReasonKnee configuration across five pathology categories. These findings support dual-sequence full-volume modeling for comprehensive knee MRI assessment.

</details>


### [106] [2D Spatial Reasoning with Adaptive Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08518)
*Martin Spitznagel,Janis Keuper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应神经细胞自动机(aNCA)架构，利用可变形卷积动态调整感受野，在网格化数据上迭代推理2D空间关系，在数独、迷宫最短路径等图像谜题上取得SOTA且可解释、泛化性强的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习方法在处理空间推理任务时仍显不足，缺乏利用感知实体的几何信息及其相互空间关系来解决问题的能力。

Method: 提出aNCA架构，使用可变形卷积（deformable convolutions）动态调整感知/感受野，并在图像等网格结构上进行迭代式空间关系推理。

Result: 在公开基准上于图像类谜题（数独、迷宫最短路径）取得SOTA、可解释的结果，并具备较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 可变形卷积驱动的自适应细胞自动机为网格化数据的空间推理提供了有效且可泛化的方案。

Abstract: Many modern learning approaches are still struggling with spatial reasoning tasks, i.e. they lack the ability to utilize geometric information of perceived entities and their spatial relation to each other to solve problems. We introduce a novel Adaptive Neural Cellular Automata (aNCA) architecture which uses deformable convolutions to dynamically adapt the perceptive field and iteratively reason over 2D spatial relations on grid-like data structures (e.g. images). Empirical results on public benchmarks show state of the art comprehensible results with high generalization abilities for solving image based puzzles like Sudoku or finding the shortest path in a maze.

</details>


### [107] [MedCORE: Criteria-Grounded Clinical Reasoning for Interpretable Medical Image Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08528)
*Asim Khan,Samee Ullah Khan,Dwarikanath Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedCORE：将医学图像诊断分解为临床标准，经空间定位、多尺度证据编码、图注意力建模标准间依赖，并与临床文本对齐与原型强化，用不确定性校准加权聚合，在三模态数据集上优于多类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度模型直接从图像特征映射到疾病标签，绕过临床结构化推理，缺乏诊断透明度，可能影响临床安全部署。

Method: 视觉-语言架构中操作化临床推理：将诊断分解为临床定义标准；对每条标准做空间定位；多尺度表示编码宏观结构与微观纹理证据；图注意力网络建模标准间依赖；标准表示与临床文本描述符对齐、以类级视觉原型强化；不确定性校准加权聚合，按置信度折扣低置信证据。

Result: ISIC 2018：89.2% 准确率、85.7% macro-F1、96.4% AUC；BUSI：96.1%、95.2%、98.4% AUC；IDRiD：84.3%、80.2%、92.8% AUC，一致优于 CNN、Transformer、生物医学视觉-语言、概念型与原型型基线。

Conclusion: 结构化的标准导向推理可在保持多模态泛化性的同时提升医学图像诊断性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis is inherently a structured reasoning process, yet existing deep learning models often bypass this structure by mapping image features directly to disease labels without explicitly interrogating the morphological and textural criteria that clinicians systematically evaluate. This limits diagnostic transparency and may compromise safe clinical deployment. We present MedCORE (Medical Criteria-Oriented Reasoning and Evidence), a structured diagnostic framework that operationalizes clinical reasoning within a vision-language architecture. For each input image, MedCORE decomposes the diagnostic process into clinically defined criteria, spatially localizes each criterion to diagnostically relevant image regions, encodes evidence through multi-scale representations that capture macro-structural and micro-textural pathological characteristics, and refines criterion representations using a Graph Attention Network that explicitly models inter-criteria dependencies. Criterion representations are further aligned with clinical text descriptors, reinforced through class-wise visual prototypes, and aggregated using uncertainty-calibrated weighting that proportionally discounts low-confidence diagnostic evidence. MedCORE is validated across three clinically heterogeneous imaging modalities, including dermoscopic lesion classification on ISIC 2018, breast ultrasound lesion characterization on BUSI, and diabetic retinopathy grading on IDRiD. Quantitatively, MedCORE achieves 89.2% accuracy, 85.7% macro-F1, and 96.4% AUC on ISIC 2018; 96.1% accuracy, 95.2% macro-F1, and 98.4% AUC on BUSI; and 84.3% accuracy, 80.2% macro-F1, and 92.8% AUC on IDRiD. These results demonstrate consistent improvements over strong CNN, transformer, biomedical vision-language, concept-based, and prototype-based baselines.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Perturbation Magnitude: Direction-Dependent Responses in Multimodal Geometric Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08533)
*Yongsheng Luo,Wengan He,Yu Li,Rouying Wu,Wei Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出方向性几何响应（DGR），用Gram行列式体积梯度刻画多模态几何一致性分数对扰动（视频模糊、音频噪声）的响应机制，证明位移方向而非仅位移大小决定响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态几何对齐分数（基于Gram行列式）可紧凑建模模态间高阶一致性，但其在模态退化下如何响应尚不清楚。作者追问：该分数的响应是否主要由扰动位移的模长决定。

Method: 使用MSR-VTT（N=878）与DiDeMo（N=980）的冻结cohort，施加受控视频模糊与音频噪声；对Gramian体积作一阶闭式展开，得到DGR——位移在局部体积梯度上的投影，联合刻画干净工作点、位移模长与位移方向；并检验无方向替代方案与预设增益归一化候选V/(g_V+eps)。

Result: 位移模长最多解释绝对响应样本外方差的15%，等模长对的响应系统性不同；一阶DGR绝对项样本外R²达0.838–0.969，等模长排序准确率0.864–0.963，响应符号准确率0.909–0.989，而无方向替代方案在该评估协议下弱或不稳定；V/(g_V+eps)未通过可预测性与干净序门控。

Conclusion: 几何响应取决于表示在何处运作、退化将关系几何移动多远、以及朝哪个方向移动；DGR使用退化态位移，属解释性量而非部署期预测器。

Abstract: Geometric alignment scores based on Gram determinants provide a compact way to model higher-order consistency among modalities, yet how such scores respond to modality degradation is poorly understood. This paper asks whether the response of a multimodal geometric score is determined primarily by the magnitude of the perturbation-induced displacement. Using frozen cohorts from MSR-VTT (N=878) and DiDeMo (N=980), we apply controlled video blur and audio noise and analyze the response in the relational geometry on which the score is defined. Displacement magnitude explains at most 15% of the out-of-sample variance in the absolute response, and magnitude-matched pairs respond systematically differently, so scalar magnitude does not organize the response. The closed-form first-order expansion of the Gramian volume yields the Directional Geometric Response (DGR): the projection of the displacement onto the local volume gradient, which jointly captures the clean operating point, displacement magnitude, and displacement direction. The absolute first-order DGR term explains the observed response with out-of-sample R^2 of 0.838-0.969, matched-magnitude ranking accuracies of 0.864-0.963, and response-sign accuracies of 0.909-0.989, whereas the tested direction-free alternatives remain weak or unstable under the corresponding evaluation protocols. A pre-specified gain-normalization candidate, V/(g_V+eps), fails its predictability and clean-order gates. DGR uses the observed degraded-state displacement and is therefore an explanatory quantity, not a deployment-time predictor: geometric response depends on where the representation operates, how far degradation moves the relational geometry, and in which direction it moves.

</details>


### [109] [RSJEV: Discriminative Remote Sensing Scene Classification with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08539)
*Dongchen Si,Di Wang,Mingzhen Xu,Jing Zhang,Bo Du,Liangpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RSJEV将遥感场景分类重构为候选条件化的多模态判别决策过程，用OnePass Decider一次前向估计候选类别概率，避免自回归解码，在UCM、AID、NWPU-RESISC45上优于CNN/Transformer/Mamba/CLIP/MLLM方法，且仅0.8B参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感场景分类方法存在三类局限：任务专用视觉分类器依赖预定义标签空间且泛化受限；CLIP类方法仅做静态图文对齐、缺乏任务自适应；MLLM将分类建模为自回归文本生成，在候选类别明确时产生冗余token级生成开销。因此需要一种兼顾视觉-语言交互与高效判别决策的范式。

Method: 提出RSJEV单次前向多模态决策框架，将分类形式化为候选条件化（candidate-conditioned）的多模态判别决策：联合建模视觉表征、任务指令与候选类别语义；核心组件OnePass Decider提取多模态决策状态，直接在候选类别空间上估计类别概率，去除自回归解码但保留视觉-语言交互。使用紧凑的0.8B参数模型。

Result: 在UC Merced、AID、NWPU-RESISC45三个遥感场景分类基准上，RSJEV相较代表性CNN、Transformer、Mamba、CLIP及MLLM方法取得更优分类精度；同时显著降低推理成本，在0.8B参数规模下实现更好的精度-效率折中。代码将开源。

Conclusion: 结果表明状态条件化的多模态判别决策对高效遥感图像理解有效：将MLLM的分类任务从生成式改为判别式候选打分，可在保持视觉-语言交互的同时消除解码开销，为高效遥感场景分类提供新范式。

Abstract: Remote sensing scene classification is a fundamental task in Earth observation and geospatial analysis. Existing approaches mainly follow three paradigms: task-specific visual classification, vision-language similarity matching, and autoregressive multimodal generation. However, visual classifiers rely on predefined label spaces, CLIP-based methods perform recognition through static image-text alignment, and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) introduce unnecessary token-level generation for classification tasks with explicit candidate categories. To address these limitations, we propose RSJEV, a one-pass multimodal decision framework for remote sensing scene classification. Unlike conventional MLLMs that formulate classification as autoregressive text generation, RSJEV reformulates scene classification as a candidate-conditioned multimodal discriminative decision process, where visual representations, task instructions, and candidate category semantics are jointly modeled. Specifically, we introduce a OnePass Decider that extracts multimodal decision states and directly estimates category probabilities within the candidate category space, eliminating autoregressive decoding while preserving vision-language interactions. Extensive experiments on three widely used remote sensing scene classification benchmarks, including UC Merced, AID, and NWPU-RESISC45, demonstrate that RSJEV achieves superior classification performance compared with representative CNN-, Transformer-, Mamba-, CLIP-, and MLLM-based methods. Moreover, RSJEV significantly reduces inference costs and achieves a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off with only a compact 0.8B-parameter model. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of state-conditioned multimodal decision making for efficient remote sensing image understanding. The code will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [110] [Have I Seen Enough? Frozen Video-Language Models Encode Evidence Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08560)
*Dan Ben-Ami,Kobi Cohen,Chaim Baskin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 冻结的流式VideoLLM激活中已线性可读地编码"证据是否已到齐"（证据就绪度）信号，且该信号是问题条件化的、与置信度/不确定性解耦；作者据此提出Readiness Gating答案时机策略，在匹配视频时长下最高提升+9.75个百分点，且增益随任务可用精度余量变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频语言模型不仅要决定"答什么"，还要决定"当前问题的证据是否已到达"。现有系统把这一决策训练为一个独立的触发器（trigger），作者质疑：未修改的模型是否已自行计算了这一决策？若已存在，则可用极低代价读出并用于答案时机控制。

Method: 1) 以时间戳证据（而非模型输出）构造就绪度标签，在7个共享的字节级相同评测上训练线性探针（probe）；2) 严格"未就绪"采样（strictest not-ready sampling）下测AUROC，并与拟合时钟（fitted clock）基线对比；3) 跨基准家族泛化测试（探针未见该家族视频）；4) 问题条件化对照：字节相同窗口仅改问题，观察读出翻转率，并与问题盲控制对照；5) 在错误回答子集上测AUROC，检验就绪度与正确性解耦；6) 与不确定性估计器及其监督组合在延迟匹配答案选择上比较，并与独立人类判断对比；7) 将已发布流式触发器作为线性读出，比较其与就绪度探针在其自身基座模型激活上的几何关系与解码精度；8) 实例化为Readiness Gating策略，在26种配置下测精度增益与"精度余量"的关联，并用同像素干预做因果验证。

Result: 就绪度信号在全部7个模型中线性可解，严格未就绪采样下AUROC为0.733–0.905（拟合时钟接近随机）；探针在未见过的基准家族上仍有解码能力；字节相同窗口下仅改问题即可在66.1%的问题对上翻转读出，问题盲控制按构造处于随机水平；在错误回答中AUROC仍达0.722；就绪度在延迟匹配答案选择上优于不确定性估计器及其监督组合，且比置信度更贴近独立人类判断；已训练触发器在其自身基座激活上与就绪度近似正交、解码精度远低于探针；Readiness Gating在匹配视频时长下精度提升最高+9.75 pp，计算开销可忽略，且增益随任务精度余量变化（26种配置），同像素干预可移动增益。

Conclusion: 未修改的冻结流式VideoLLM内部已存在线性可读、问题条件化的证据就绪度表征，且与置信度/不确定性、与已训练触发器均解耦。该表征可直接转化为答案时机策略（Readiness Gating），以近乎零额外开销提升流式视频问答精度，增益大小由任务可用的精度余量决定。

Abstract: Streaming video-language models must decide not only what to answer, but whether the evidence needed for the current question has arrived. Existing systems learn that decision as a separate trigger; we ask whether an unmodified model already computes it. We show that frozen VideoLLMs carry a linearly readable evidence-readiness signal, labelled from timestamped evidence rather than from model output. It decodes in all seven models of a shared byte-identical evaluation (AUROC 0.733-0.905 under the strictest not-ready sampling, where a fitted clock is near chance), and a probe fitted without any of a benchmark family's footage still reads that family. It is question-conditioned: on byte-identical windows, changing only the question reverses the readout on 66.1% of pairs, while every question-blind control is at chance by construction. The model can answer incorrectly and still encode readiness: AUROC remains 0.722 among wrong answers. Readiness also beats uncertainty estimators and their supervised combination on latency-matched answer selection, and tracks independent human judgments more closely than confidence. Released streaming triggers are also linear readouts, yet a trained trigger read on its own base model's activations is approximately orthogonal to readiness and decodes it far less accurately than a probe. We turn the readout into Readiness Gating, an answer-timing policy that improves accuracy by up to +9.75 pp at matched video duration with negligible computational overhead. How much it gains varies with the accuracy headroom the task makes available: across 26 configurations the gain tracks that headroom, and an intervention that moves it over identical pixels moves the gain with it.

</details>


### [111] [Sparse2comm: Towards Robust Cooperative 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08573)
*Lei Yang,Boqi Li,Chunmian Lin,Li Wang,Ziying Song,Shaoqing Xu,Heye Huang,Haibao Yu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sparse2comm：把不可靠协同感知视为"渐进式复原"，用稀疏前景掩码传输→稠密语义重建→延迟运动补偿→自监督空间校准的有序流水线，在三个V2X数据集上兼顾带宽效率与混合退化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同3D检测依赖车-路互补观测，但实际部署受带宽限制与协同不可靠（丢包、延迟、空间失配）制约；已有方法多只降通信开销或只补偿单一退化，对耦合扰动处理不足。

Method: Sparse Feature Encoding：通信内容为随机掩码采样的前景特征，自车从中重建稠密语义表征，稀疏到稠密机制同时实现超低带宽传输与丢包恢复；Latency-Aware Alignment 在语义复原特征上预测运动流以补偿延迟消息；Self-Calibrating Fusion 以自监督方式估计残余空间偏移后进行自适应跨智能体融合。三级按序复原语义完整性、时间一致性与空间对齐。

Result: 在 DAIR-V2X、OpenV2V、V2V4Real 上保持有竞争力的干净精度，在单一及混合真实退化下鲁棒性一致提升；相对 Where2comm，混合设置 AP@0.5/AP@0.7 分别提升 +20.15/+11.79、+12.66/+11.07、+15.36/+12.61。

Conclusion: 将协同退化统一建模为渐进式特征复原，为带宽受限下的鲁棒协同3D检测提供了一条有效且可推广的框架。

Abstract: Cooperative perception improves autonomous driving by sharing complementary observations among vehicles and roadside infrastructure for 3D object detection. However, practical deployment is constrained by limited bandwidth and unreliable cooperation, where packet loss, transmission delay, and spatial misalignment jointly degrade the cooperative feature stream. Existing methods often reduce communication cost or compensate for one degradation type, leaving coupled disturbances insufficiently addressed. To address this problem, we propose Sparse2comm, a bandwidth-efficient and robust cooperative 3D object detection framework that treats unreliable cooperation as progressive restoration over degraded cooperative features. Sparse Feature Encoding first encodes communication as randomly mask-sampled foreground features transmitted by collaborating agents, from which the ego vehicle reconstructs dense semantic representations. This sparse-to-dense mechanism learns to infer missing object-centric content from sparse observations, enabling ultra-low-bandwidth communication and packet-loss recovery within the same representation. On the semantically restored features, Latency-Aware Alignment predicts motion flow to compensate delayed messages, and Self-Calibrating Fusion estimates residual spatial offsets in a self-supervised manner before adaptive cross-agent fusion. Sparse2comm therefore restores semantic completeness, temporal consistency, and spatial alignment in an ordered pipeline. Extensive experiments on DAIR-V2X, OpenV2V, and V2V4Real show that Sparse2comm maintains competitive clean accuracy and consistently improves robustness under individual and mixed real-world degradations. Compared with the selective feature communication baseline Where2comm, Sparse2comm improves mixed-setting AP@0.5/AP@0.7 by +20.15/+11.79, +12.66/+11.07, and +15.36/+12.61 on the three datasets, respectively.

</details>


### [112] [FedDermaSeg: Federated Learning for Dermatological Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08574)
*Anabik Pal,Ganesh Patidar,Bikash Santra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究联邦学习在皮肤病变分割中的可行性，利用ISIC 2018训练/验证集模拟分布式环境训练联邦分割模型，并在ISIC 2018测试集与PH2上评估。结果显示联邦模型性能与集中式训练相当，且稳定优于各本地模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割通常依赖集中式训练，需将图像与掩码汇聚到中央服务器，带来隐私风险和集中算力开销；作者希望验证联邦学习能否在不上传医疗数据的前提下实现可用的皮肤病变分割。

Method: 基于ISIC 2018皮肤病变分割挑战赛的训练集与验证集模拟分布式客户端环境，训练联邦分割模型，并将该模型在ISIC 2018测试集和PH2数据集上评估性能与泛化性。

Result: 联邦模型达到与集中式训练相当的分割性能，并一致优于仅在本地数据上训练的模型；在跨数据集（ISIC 2018测试集、PH2）上也具备一定泛化能力。

Conclusion: 联邦学习可在不集中汇聚医学图像的前提下支持协作式皮肤病变分割，是隐私保护医学图像分析的一条可行路径，但属于医学影像应用验证，缺少面向通用机器人与具身智能的可迁移方法创新。

Abstract: Skin cancer is a major global health concern, and early detection and accurate lesion delineation are important for effective diagnosis and treatment planning. Automated skin lesion analysis can assist dermatologists, with lesion segmentation serving as a fundamental step in computer-aided diagnostic systems. Conventional deep learning-based segmentation models typically rely on centralized training, where images and their corresponding segmentation masks are collected on a central server. Such data aggregation raises privacy concerns in medical applications and requires substantial centralized computational resources. To address these limitations, we investigate the feasibility of federated learning for privacy-preserving skin lesion segmentation. The training and validation sets of the ISIC 2018 Skin Lesion Segmentation Challenge dataset are used to simulate a distributed learning environment and develop a federated segmentation model. The resulting model is evaluated on the ISIC 2018 test set and the PH2 dataset to assess its performance and generalizability. Experimental results demonstrate that the federated model achieves performance comparable to centralized training while consistently improving upon the locally trained models. These findings demonstrate the potential of federated learning for collaborative skin lesion segmentation without requiring centralized aggregation of medical images.

</details>


### [113] [LiDAR Resolution Recovery via Foundation-Model-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08620)
*Samed Doğan,Nico Leuze,Alfred Schöttl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用预训练Stable Diffusion作为骨干，以2D基础模型的伪深度为监督微调LiDAR条件深度模型，训练时随机抽取不同beam预算，量化从稀疏输入恢复密集LiDAR扫描的恢复/分辨率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高线数LiDAR昂贵，但感知流程需要密集角度采样；本文想验证能否用生成式基础模型的先验，从低线数（如4线）输入"上采样"恢复密集扫描，并明确其收益与代价。

Method: 以Stable Diffusion为backbone，构建LiDAR条件深度模型；用2D基础模型产生的伪深度作目标进行微调；训练中随机对LiDAR条件做不同beam预算的decimation；在nuScenes上对物理held-out真实beam评估，将"恢复"（recovery）与"拟合精度"（fit accuracy）分开报告，并做类别分层误差分析。

Result: 在极稀疏情形优势最大：4-beam输入下δ1.25达66.8%，而scattered interpolation仅45.1%。误差分层显示平面表面最先恢复，引入深度不连续的目标最早退化。

Conclusion: 基础模型引导的LiDAR增强存在明确的恢复/分辨率权衡，方法在极稀疏区间最有效，但对深度不连续场景敏感；为低成本LiDAR密集化提供了量化参考。

Abstract: High-beam-count LiDAR sensors are costly, yet many perception pipelines require dense angular sampling. Using a pretrained Stable Diffusion model as the backbone, we fine-tune a LiDAR-conditioned depth model with pseudo-depth targets from a 2D foundation model. During training, the LiDAR conditioning is randomly decimated at different beam budgets. We then investigate how much of a LiDAR scan can be recovered from heavily decimated input and characterize performance across the input beam budget. We evaluate against physically held-out real beams on nuScenes and report recovery separately from fit accuracy. Our model yields its largest advantage in very sparse regimes, achieving a $\delta_{1.25}$ accuracy of $66.8$% from $4$-beam input where scattered interpolation reaches only $45.1$%. A class-stratified error breakdown further reveals that planar surfaces recover first while objects introducing depth discontinuities degrade earliest. Together, these results quantify the recovery/resolution trade-off for foundation-model-guided LiDAR enhancement.

</details>


### [114] [Forensic Reserve: Eliciting Latent Knowledge for Image Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08639)
*Jiahua Li,Zixu John,Tom Zhong,Fuping Wu,Tianhao Xu,Jianqing Zheng,Yuanhan Mo,Fei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Reserve-Guided Elicitation (RGE) 框架，将预训练模型中对真实性敏感的稀疏内部组件视为"取证储备"，通过结构与方向约束实现轻量适配，用于生成图像伪造检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成图像日益逼真，可靠取证检测对维护视觉信息信任至关重要；但现有方法主要依赖任务特定监督去适配视觉基础模型表征，未能充分利用模型内部的取证知识来指导检测。

Method: 1) 用 Forensic Lens (F-lens) 将各层与 token 组的激活分解为独立成分，并按真实/生成图像响应差异全局筛选出储备位点与方向；2) 将选定方向映射回隐状态空间构造固定储备子空间，仅在识别位点插入 Forensic Reserve Adapters (FRA)；3) 固定主干参数、预拟合参考分类器与子空间基，只训练 FRA 系数图，产生受子空间约束的输入相关残差更新。

Result: 仅用 500 张标注训练图像、可训练参数不足主干 0.2%，在三个检测基准上无需目标基准适配即取得有竞争力性能；在 8 个自监督与视觉-语言预训练编码器上均稳定优于对应冻结检测器。

Conclusion: RGE 以极低标注与参数成本激发跨多种预训练视觉模型普遍共享的潜藏取证能力。

Abstract: As generated images become increasingly realistic, reliable forgery detection is essential for maintaining trust in visual information. However, existing methods primarily rely on task-specific supervision to adapt vision foundation model representations, without fully exploiting internal forensic knowledge to guide detection. To address this limitation, we propose Reserve-Guided Elicitation (RGE), a framework that treats sparse, origin-sensitive internal components in pretrained models as a forensic reserve and translates their localization into structural constraints for lightweight adaptation. Specifically, we first use the Forensic Lens (F-lens) to decompose activations across layers and token groups into independent components and globally screen them by their response differences between real and generated images, identifying reserve sites and directions. Next, we map the selected directions back to hidden-state space to construct fixed reserve subspaces and insert Forensic Reserve Adapters (FRA) only at the identified sites. Finally, with the backbone parameters, previously fitted reference classifier, and subspace bases fixed, we train only the FRA coefficient maps to generate input-dependent residual updates constrained to the corresponding subspaces, strengthening existing forensic responses. Using only 500 labeled training images and a trainable parameter budget below 0.2% of the backbone, RGE achieves competitive performance across three detection benchmarks without target-benchmark adaptation. Furthermore, RGE consistently improves over the corresponding frozen detectors across eight encoders spanning self-supervised and vision-language pretraining, eliciting a latent forensic capacity broadly shared across pretrained vision models.

</details>


### [115] [Stable Scores, Unstable Answers: Frame Phase and Option Order in Video Multiple-Choice Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08649)
*Lichen Zhu,Yiheng Wang,Yueqian Lin,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频语言模型评测中均匀采样网格存在"相位"这一未被报告的隐含变量，改变相位可使约1/5答案变化而总分几乎不变；PHASEFUSION通过融合三种偏移网格（多相分量）的选项后验，在同等预算下匹配32帧单次前向的准确率，并将半相位偏移导致的答案变动率从18.2%降至10.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频语言基准仅报告采样的"速率"（帧数），而忽略"相位"（起始偏移）。作者发现仅半帧步长的相位差异就能让23.6%的选择题答案不同，而模型得分差异却在1分以内，说明当前排行榜与部署采样器之间存在严重的评测混淆，答案对呈现方式（相位、选项顺序）而非内容敏感。因此需要量化相位敏感性并给出稳健的解码方案。

Method: 将密集均匀网格视为多相分解，取三个相位偏移的网格分别解码，再对选项后验（option posteriors）做平均融合（PHASEFUSION）；同时用单次前向的答案margin来区分并标记仅由选项顺序（呈现方式）引起的答案翻转。

Result: 仅相位偏移半帧的两个已部署采样器，对23.6%的问题给出不同答案而总分相差不到1分；两个家族四个版本仅改相位，在控制选项顺序后约1/5答案变化。PHASEFUSION在预设误差界限内匹配32帧单次前向的准确率（logit打分），并将三网格半相位偏移引起的答案改变率从18.2%降到10.1%。

Conclusion: 视频语言评测的相位（时间偏移）是隐藏且影响巨大的变量，应随预算一同报告，否则需将其边缘化；多相融合是以相同计算预算换取相位稳健性的有效手段。

Abstract: Video-language models are ranked by multiple-choice accuracy on frames from a uniform grid. The grid has two parameters, a rate and a phase, and benchmarks report only the rate. The phase moves answers: two deployed samplers differing only by a half-step phase offset answer 23.6% of questions differently while scoring within a point, and across four releases from two families shifting only the phase changes roughly one answer in five after controlling option order. PHASEFUSION decodes three offset grids and averages the option posteriors. The grids are the polyphase components of the dense grid. Fusion matches a 32-frame single pass in accuracy within a prespecified margin (logit-scored) and cuts the answers a half-step shift of all three grids changes from 18.2% to 10.1%. Option order, which changes only the presentation, is flagged instead by a one-pass answer margin. Report the phase convention with the budget, or marginalize it.

</details>


### [116] [Selective Transfer of RL Updates for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08659)
*Suxin Ji,Hungtao Wan,Mingjun Liu,An Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Selective-RL：从RL后训练阶段提取参数增量，保留其主导的矩阵级方向并按幅度缩放后迁移到VLM语言模块，在3个模型族、5个视觉推理基准上15组中12组优于整体更新插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并式能力迁移基于端点（微调前后整体权重）进行插值，会把模型原本的差异与RL后训练带来的变化混在一起，无法识别"哪些能力是后训练获得的、哪些可跨模型迁移"。作者希望从训练阶段更新的角度重新定义能力迁移。

Method: 1) 显式分离RL阶段更新Δθ（而非端点权重差）；2) 分析发现Δθ各成分的跨模型可迁移性差异大，主导方向比完整更新更可迁移；3) Selective-RL：对RL更新做矩阵级方向分解，保留主导方向并保持原有幅度，注入VLM的语言模块。

Result: 在3个模型族、5个视觉推理基准、与全量更新插值对比的15组实验中12组更优；Qwen受体上MathVision提升8.55个百分点；对照实验表明仅靠更新幅度或任意低秩近似无法复现该增益。

Conclusion: 论文区分了"后训练中获得的能力"与"可跨模型迁移的成分"，为跨模型能力迁移提供了训练阶段视角；但该工作面向VLM推理能力迁移，与机器人/具身场景无直接实证关联。

Abstract: Model merging provides a training-free way to transfer reasoning capabilities from language models to vision-language models (VLMs), but endpoint-based transfer can conflate pre-existing model differences with changes acquired during reasoning post-training. We instead formulate capability transfer around the training-stage update, isolating the parameter changes induced by reinforcement learning (RL). Yet transferring this update in full remains suboptimal: we find that its components differ substantially in cross-model transferability, with dominant directions transferring more effectively than the complete update. Based on this finding, we introduce Selective-RL, which isolates the RL-stage update, retains its dominant matrix-wise directions with magnitude preservation, and transfers them to the language modules of a VLM. Across three model families and five visual-reasoning benchmarks, Selective-RL improves full-update interpolation in 12 of 15 comparisons, including an 8.55 percentage-point MathVision gain on the Qwen recipient. Matched controls show that update magnitude or arbitrary low rank alone does not reproduce these gains. These results highlight a distinction between what is acquired during post-training and what remains transferable across models, providing a training-stage perspective on cross-model capability transfer. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [117] [Knowing When to Trust a Prior: Reliability-Gated Cue Fusion for Video Gaze Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08663)
*Lichen Zhu,Yueqian Lin,Yiheng Wang,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FocusGate 提出一种带门控的免注视先验集成方法用于视频注视预测：逐帧门控读取离焦图的三个形状统计量，仅在估计器平均优于随机的帧上启用先验，被拒帧退化为基线，配合中位秩归一化实现零参数弃权；在电影、体育、网络视频上显著正向，并在四个有监督预测器（含 NTIRE 2026 冠军）上提升全部 16 个模型-域单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频注视预测主要由注视数据训练的模型主导，而免注视先验虽携带这些模型未吸收的信号，却缺乏"何时信任它们"的机制；无条件融合在部分域（如体育）反而有害、在网络视频上无效，因此需要可控、可弃权的融合策略。

Method: FocusGate 是一个允许成员弃权的免注视先验门控集成：每帧门控读取离焦图的三个形状统计量，选择估计器平均优于随机的帧进行融合，被拒帧精确退化为基线；采用中位秩归一化使全零先验以零参数弃权；仅将门控融合作为有监督预测器的附加项。

Result: 门控融合在电影、体育、网络视频上均显著为正，而无条件融合在体育上有害、在网络视频上为零；附加到四个有监督预测器（含 NTIRE 2026 冠军）后，在 shuffled AUC 上改善全部 16 个模型-域组合，其中 15 个显著（一个域为预注册单次评分），仅使冠军延迟增加 1%；单独使用时以 16 帧因果均值在电影数据集上超过 TASED-Net 与 UNISAL。

Conclusion: 免注视先验在正确门控下可为注视预测提供互补信号并稳定提升有监督模型，且几乎不增加延迟；但该工作面向通用视频注视预测，未涉及机器人、具身导航或 3D 重建等核心具身智能场景。

Abstract: Video gaze prediction is led by gaze-trained models, yet gaze-free priors carry signal those models have not absorbed, if one knows when to trust them. We propose FocusGate, a gated ensemble of gaze-free priors whose members may abstain. A per-frame gate reads three shape statistics of a defocus map and selects the frames on which the estimator is above chance on average, so rejected frames reduce to the base exactly, while midrank normalisation lets an all-zero prior abstain at zero parameters. Gated fusion is significantly positive on film, sports and web video, whereas unconditional fusion is harmful on sports and null on web. Added to four supervised predictors, the NTIRE 2026 champion among them, FocusGate improves all sixteen model-domain cells in shuffled AUC, fifteen significantly, one domain pre-registered and scored once, while adding only 1% to the champion's latency. Alone, it surpasses TASED-Net and UNISAL in shuffled AUC on film with a 16-frame causal mean.

</details>


### [118] [PDB: Point-Based Deformation Blending for Facial Animation Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08672)
*Sihun Cha,Hyeonseung Shin,Suah Yu,Junyong Noh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PDB：基于点的形变混合实现无网格依赖的表情重定向，权重每目标仅算一次，控制点随源表情变化，仅用自重建监督即支持跨身份迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨不同结构网格的表情重定向需要在保持面部运动的同时避免表面伪影。现有方法依赖预定义 cage、预计算坐标、学习式逐元素形变解码器或全局重建求解，流程复杂且易产生表面瑕疵。作者希望用更简洁的参数化表示直接完成重定向。

Method: 从源中性-表情对预测紧凑的一组形变控制点，从目标中性网格预测混合权重。权重组级每目标计算一次并可跨帧复用，控制点随源表情变化。用 ReLU 保证权重非负并允许精确零值，再做行归一化。目标网格由权重与控制点直接相乘重建，无需 cage、预计算坐标、学习式逐元素解码器或全局求解。仅以自重建监督训练。

Result: 实验显示重定向准确、推理速度快，学到的权重具有局部支撑性。在表情精度与局部表面保持的联合评估中，相比所比较的稠密位移方法减少了表面伪影，同时保留预期运动。感知评估在自重定向与跨重定向下均支持其表情保真度与视觉质量。

Conclusion: PDB 提供了一种轻量、无网格依赖的表情重定向方案，通过控制点-权重分离设计避免了多种传统组件，仅自监督即可泛化到跨身份迁移，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Mesh-agnostic facial animation retargeting transfers expressions across meshes with different structures, but preserving facial motion without surface artifacts remains challenging. To address this, we present PDB, Point-Based Deformation Blending for facial animation retargeting. PDB predicts a compact set of deformed control points from a source neutral-expression pair and blending weights from the target neutral mesh. The weights are computed once per target and reused across frames, while the control points vary with each source expression. ReLU enforces non-negative weights and permits exact zeros, followed by row-wise normalization. The target mesh is reconstructed directly by multiplying the weights and control points, without a predefined cage, precomputed coordinates, a learned per-element deformation decoder, or a global reconstruction solve. Trained only with self-retargeting reconstruction supervision, PDB supports cross-identity transfer without paired cross-identity training expressions. Experiments demonstrate accurate retargeting, fast inference, and localized support in the learned weights. Joint evaluation of expression accuracy and local surface preservation shows reduced surface artifacts relative to the evaluated dense displacement method while retaining the intended motion. Perceptual evaluations further support expression fidelity and visual quality in both self- and cross-retargeting.

</details>


### [119] [EC-RAG: Event Chain Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08674)
*Yuhao Qin,Junbo Wang,Yuke Li,Yining Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的事件链检索增强生成框架 EC-RAG，将长视频切分为语义连贯的事件段并链接成显式事件链，在事件级进行多模态检索以支撑问答，在 Video-MME、MLVU、LongVideoBench 上稳定超越帧级检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频语言模型（LVLM）多独立处理帧，难以建模跨事件时序依赖；已引入的检索增强方法多停留在帧或片段级，无法刻画事件随时间的演化与相互关系。

Method: EC-RAG 为免训练框架：先把视频切分为语义连贯的事件段，用多模态信号（语音、文本、视觉）表示每段；再按时序链接为结构化事件链，保留事件间关系；给定查询后在链上定位相关事件并聚合关联模态证据，实现事件级抽象、结构化多模态融合，并可即插即用接入现有 LVLM 主干，无需额外训练或专有模型。

Result: 在 Video-MME、MLVU、LongVideoBench 三个长视频基准上，事件中心设计一致优于帧级检索基线，验证了时序结构建模对长视频理解的重要性。

Conclusion: 以事件链为单位组织与检索视频内容是长视频理解的有效范式，且无需训练即可提升现有 LVLM，具备良好兼容性与实用性。

Abstract: Current large video-language models (LVLMs) still face challenges when dealing with long videos, mainly because frames are often processed independently, making it difficult to capture temporal dependencies across events. Although retrieval-augmented approaches have been introduced to provide additional context, most of them operate at the frame or snippet level, which limits their ability to model how events evolve over time and relate to each other. In this paper, we propose Event Chain Retrieval-Augmented Generation (EC-RAG), a training-free framework that organizes video content into an explicit event chain before question answering. Instead of retrieving isolated frames or text segments, EC-RAG first partitions the video into semantically coherent segments, represents each segment using multi-modal signals, and then links them into a structured chain that preserves temporal order and captures inter-event relationships. Given a query, the system identifies relevant events within this chain and gathers supporting evidence from the associated modalities. Our approach offers several practical advantages: (i) event-level abstraction that better reflects how video content is naturally structured, enabling more reliable localization compared to frame-level retrieval; (ii) structured multi-modal fusion that aggregates speech, text, and visual cues at the event level, allowing complementary information to be more effectively utilized during reasoning; and (iii) plug-and-play compatibility with existing LVLM backbones, requiring no additional training or reliance on proprietary models. Experiments on Video-MME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench show that this event-centric design consistently outperforms frame-level retrieval baselines, highlighting the importance of modeling temporal structure for long-video understanding.

</details>


### [120] [RenderBench: Benchmarking Render-to-Real Video Transfer with Reconstructed Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08684)
*Dicong Qiu,Zhiyuan Xu,Yaosheng Liu,Feng Han,Bo Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RenderBench 是一个面向"渲染到真实"(render-to-real)视频生成能力的基准，包含 12 个重建真实场景（静态+动态、大尺度室内与自我中心视角），每个评测单元提供外观参考、留出真实目标视频、可编辑数字孪生、配对代理渲染与渲染器原生标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估视频模型从真实外观参考与指定场景结构/视角/运动的代理渲染生成真实视频的能力，需要同场景演化的真实目标视频与几何配准的可编辑 3D 副本作为配对监督，而此类数据传统上依赖大量人工建模、标定与动画制作，成本高且难以规模化。

Method: 构建流水线融合视觉几何、神经重建与辅助式 3D 编辑：将每个场景分解为静态物体与动态演员，配准到采集相机，经多视角几何与时序校验后才纳入。每个评测单元含外观参考图、留出真实目标视频、可编辑数字孪生、匹配代理渲染与渲染器原生场景标注。评测时对迁移模型与配对真实目标视频比较，保留 PAI-Bench-C 兼容的结构投影，并利用场景标注按物体、可见性、关节运动与运动模式定位失败。

Result: 首版保留 14 个配准样本中的 12 个（85.7%），共 1,496 对真实-代理帧。所有发布场景通过文件完整性与环境编辑审计；代理诊断得到深度 si-RMSE 0.2170、实例 mIoU 0.3673。

Conclusion: RenderBench 提供配对的真实观测与可编辑场景状态，可同时评估外观保真度以及几何与动态的保持程度，为 render-to-real 视频生成模型提供可控、可定位失败的评测基础。

Abstract: Modern video models can generate realistic videos from real appearance references and proxy renders that specify scene structure, viewpoint changes, and motion. Evaluating this render-to-real capability requires a real target video depicting the same scene evolution, paired with an editable, geometrically registered 3D replica. Such data has traditionally required substantial manual modeling, calibration, and animation effort. We introduce RenderBench, a benchmark of 12 reconstructed real-world scenes spanning large-scale indoor environments and egocentric viewpoints, with both static and dynamic settings. Our construction pipeline combines visual geometry, neural reconstruction, and assisted 3D authoring. Each scene is decomposed into static objects and dynamic actors, registered to the capture cameras, and accepted only after multi-view geometric and temporal validation. Each evaluation unit contains appearance reference images, a held-out real target video, an editable digital twin, a matched proxy render, and renderer-native scene annotations. We evaluate transfer models against paired real target videos, retain PAI-Bench-C-compatible structural projections, and use scene annotations to localize failures by object, visibility, articulation, and motion. The first release retains 12 of 14 registered samples (85.7%), comprising 1,496 paired real-proxy frames. All released scenes pass file-integrity and environment-edit audits, while proxy diagnostics yield a depth si-RMSE of 0.2170 and instance mIoU of 0.3673. RenderBench provides paired real observations and editable scene state for assessing both appearance fidelity and preservation of geometry and dynamics.

</details>


### [121] [SpaTime: Streaming Vision-Language Models for Spatio-temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08713)
*Hairong Yin,Huangying Zhan,Shin-Fang Chng,Yi Xu,Raymond A. Yeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaTime：将因果几何token逐帧融合进语言模型的流式VLM，并用响应时间损失监督回答时机；在流式空间时序基准上达49.2%准确率且平均响应时间误差降低66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需在视频尚在到达时进行3D空间推理并"看够了就答"。现有含3D几何先验的VLM性能强但只能离线运行（需完整视频）；流式VLM可因果处理帧并自行决定何时回答，却缺乏显式3D表征。作者希望统一二者：流式推理＋显式3D几何＋自适应回答时机。

Method: 提出SpaTime流式VLM：每帧仅用已观测帧生成因果几何token并融合进语言模型。训练上提出响应时间损失（response-time loss），将逐帧响应概率映射为可微的期望响应时间，并惩罚其与标注真实回答帧的距离，从而监督"何时回答"。评测上构建StreamVSTI-Bench与StreamVSI-Bench，为VSTI-Bench/VSI-Bench的流式改编版。

Result: 在StreamVSTI-Bench上总体准确率49.2%；相对最强流式基线，平均响应时间误差降低66%。

Conclusion: 将因果几何token融合与可微响应时间监督结合，可在流式设定下同时提升流式空间推理准确率与回答时机控制，为在线具身空间推理提供可行方案。

Abstract: Embodied agents must reason about 3D space while the video is still arriving, answering questions as soon as they have observed enough of the scene. VLMs that incorporate 3D geometric priors achieve strong spatial reasoning, but they operate offline, i.e., the full video must be available before they produce an answer. Streaming VLMs process frames causally and decide for themselves when to respond, yet they lack explicit 3D representations. We present SpaTime, a streaming VLM that fuses causal geometry tokens into the language model at every frame, using only the frames observed so far. To supervise when the model answers, we propose a response-time loss that maps per-frame response probabilities to a differentiable expected response time and penalizes the distance from the ground-truth frame. For evaluation, we construct StreamVSTI-Bench and StreamVSI-Bench, streaming adaptations of VSTI-Bench and VSI-Bench. On StreamVSTI-Bench, SpaTime reaches 49.2% overall accuracy and reduces the mean response-time error by 66% relative to the strongest streaming baseline.

</details>


### [122] [Co-Evolving Paths and Flows via Path-Flow Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08717)
*Zeyu Michael Li,William Xingxu Chen,Xiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究flow matching中的路径-流对齐统一训练目标，联合训练保端点的路径网络与流网络，发现路径过拟合问题（对齐损失下降但样本质量变差，源于低熵瓶颈），并提出随机路径正则化提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统flow matching固定插值路径仅学习速度场，作者希望将路径本身也变为可学习对象，从而以统一的alignment损失同时优化路径网络与流网络。但仅用alignment损失作为路径学习准则不可靠，存在路径过拟合风险，因此需要诊断该失败模式并提出正则化方案。

Method: 联合训练保端点的路径网络与流网络，共享同一alignment损失：流网络匹配路径速度，路径网络将速度对齐到当前流。诊断出路径过拟合失败模式，并将其归因于诱导概率路径中的低熵瓶颈（中间边缘分布过度集中）。据此提出随机路径正则化器，通过向路径网络隐藏部分源信息（同时保持精确端点）为随机训练路径边缘分布提供显式熵下界。

Result: 在ImageNet-256x256上，以SiT为主干，方法在不同模型规模下一致提升FID，可扩展到model-guidance训练，且推理时架构与采样器保持不变。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 随机路径正则化有效抑制学习采样器中的瓶颈，使联合路径-流训练真正生效，为flow matching提供了一种统一且实用的训练目标改进。

Abstract: We study path-flow alignment as a unified training objective for flow matching. Instead of fixing the interpolation path and learning only the velocity field, we jointly train an endpoint-preserving path network and a flow network using the same alignment loss: the flow learns to match the path velocity, and the path learns to align its velocity to the current flow. Although every fixed learned path defines a valid flow-matching objective, the alignment loss alone is not a reliable criterion for path learning. We identify path overfitting, a failure mode in which the alignment loss decreases while sample quality worsens. We find that this failure is associated with low-entropy bottlenecks in the induced probability path, where the learned path routes samples through overly concentrated intermediate marginals. Motivated by this diagnosis, we introduce a stochastic path regularizer that hides part of the source information from the path network while preserving exact endpoints. The resulting regularization gives an explicit entropy floor for the stochastic training-path marginals and empirically suppresses the bottleneck in the learned sampler, making joint path-flow training effective. On ImageNet-256x256 with SiT backbones, our method consistently improves FID across model scales, extends to model-guidance training, and leaves the inference-time architecture and sampler unchanged. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [123] [Post-Training Semantic Lifting for 3D Gaussian Splatting: Separating Detector, Lifting and Representation Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08756)
*Iván Verdugo Guerra,Ezequiel López Rubio,Jorge García González*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种后训练提升（lifting）方法，在3DGS上逐类累积多视图2D检测证据并按可见性加权，用双阈值（β与γβ）过滤高斯并借助连通域扩展，再把标签迁移到可见且被标注的网格顶点，从而分离检测器、提升、表示迁移三类误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一个3D高斯在多视图下类别不一致：部分视图被遮挡、检测置信度随视角变化；同时由于两次训练产生的高斯不同，真值只能以标注网格给出，导致高斯级监督难以定义。需要一种能融合全部视图信息、且与具体训练实例无关的语义提升方案。

Method: 后训练、单目标类别的提升方法：对每个高斯在每个视图中累积target/non-target证据，权重为该高斯在该视图的可见性；随后用主阈值β选高置信种子、用较低阈值γβ纳入其连通分量；评估时将标签从高斯迁移到同时可见且被标注的网格顶点，从而把误差归因拆分为2D检测器、lifting、跨表示迁移三部分。阈值与迁移算子仅在Replica验证场景上调参，再以同一组超参在ScanNet++上测试。

Result: Replica验证场景平均mIoU：数据集标注掩码0.93，YOLO掩码0.65；ScanNet++测试场景为0.80与0.54。相较逐视图阈值化的旧版本，本方法在测试mIoU上提升0.24，并使得两个数据集的所有类别与场景可共用单一阈值。误差分析表明剩余误差主要来自2D检测器。

Conclusion: 多视图证据融合加双阈值连通域扩展能显著提升3DGS语义提升的鲁棒性与跨场景可迁移性，且提供了细粒度的误差分解；主要瓶颈在上游2D检测器，而非提升或标签迁移环节。

Abstract: The same Gaussian of a 3D Gaussian Splatting model is seen from many views, and these views do not always agree on the class it belongs to. The Gaussian may be occluded in some of them, and the confidence of the detector is not the same from one view to another. The ground truth, on the other hand, is given as an annotated mesh, because two training runs do not produce the same Gaussians. In this work, we propose a post-training lifting method that works with one target class at a time and combines the information coming from all the views. Target and non-target evidence are accumulated simultaneously, weighted by the visibility of each Gaussian in each view. After that, the Gaussians are filtered with two thresholds: a main threshold $\beta$ selects the high-confidence seeds, and a lower one $\gamma\beta$ adds the connected components around them. For the evaluation, the labels are transferred from the Gaussians to the mesh vertices that are both visible and annotated. With this design, we can separate three sources of error: the 2D detector, the lifting and the transfer between representations. The thresholds and the transfer operator are chosen on seven Replica validation scenes, and the method is evaluated on ten held-out ScanNet++ scenes with the same values for every scene and class. The mean mIoU on the validation scenes was 0.93 with masks from the dataset annotations and 0.65 with YOLO masks, and on the ScanNet++ test scenes it was 0.80 and 0.54. Compared with thresholding the evidence per view, as a previous version of the method did, the fraction improves the test mIoU by 0.24 and makes it possible to use a single threshold for all the classes and scenes of both datasets. Finally, the error analysis shows that most of the remaining error comes from the detector.

</details>


### [124] [Data Leakage in Patch-Based Hyperspectral Image Classification: Quantifying the Impact of Spatial Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08770)
*Mohammed Q. Alkhatib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了 patch-based 高光谱图像分类中的同类别训练-测试空间重叠导致的"数据泄漏"问题，提出重叠百分比(OP)与平均重叠比率(AOR)两个度量，在 Pavia University 数据集上对比随机与非随机空间采样下多种模型(SVM、MLP、2D/3D-CNN、ViT、MorpMamba)的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: patch-based 学习虽提升了 HSI 分类性能，但常见的随机训练-测试采样会引入空间 patch 重叠，属于数据泄漏，导致评测结果虚高、性能被高估，现有评测流程缺乏对同类重叠的系统量化。

Method: 提出两类度量：OP（全局统计重叠的测试 patch 像素比例）与 AOR（衡量受影响测试 patch 的局部重叠严重度）；在 Pavia University 数据集上，采用随机与非随机空间采样两种设置，对 SVM、MLP、2D-CNN、3D-CNN、ViT、MorpMamba 六种模型进行对照实验，并分析 patch 尺寸(5x5→19x19)对重叠的影响。

Result: 随机采样下深度 patch 模型精度极高，3D-CNN 达 96.17% OA；非随机空间采样下性能大幅下降，3D-CNN 降至 55.20%，ViT 与 2D-CNN 分别下降 40.71 与 38.81 个百分点。patch 尺寸由 5x5 增至 19x19 使随机采样 OP 从 23.28% 升至 77.02%。

Conclusion: 随机 patch 评估会显著虚高分类性能，尤其对强依赖空间上下文的模型；该工作揭示了评测协议缺陷，提示需采用非随机空间采样以避免数据泄漏，代码已开源。

Abstract: Patch-based learning improves hyperspectral image (HSI) classification by exploiting local spectral-spatial information, but random train-test sampling from the same image can cause spatial patch overlap, leading to data leakage and optimistic performance estimates. This paper investigates same-class train-test spatial overlap in patch-based HSI classification using two measures: overlap percentage (OP), which quantifies the global amount of overlapped testing patch pixels, and average overlap ratio (AOR), which measures the local severity among affected testing patches. Experiments on the Pavia University dataset compare random and non-random spatial sampling using SVM, MLP, 2D-CNN, 3D-CNN, ViT, and MorpMamba. The results show that deep patch-based models achieve high accuracy under random sampling, with 3D-CNN reaching 96.17% Overall Accuracy (OA), but drop substantially under non-random spatial sampling, where 3D-CNN decreases to 55.20% and ViT and 2D-CNN drop by 40.71 and 38.81 percentage points (PP), respectively. Patch-size analysis further shows that increasing the patch size from 5x5 to 19x19 raises the random-sampling overlap percentage from 23.28% to 77.02%. These findings demonstrate that random patch-based evaluation can substantially inflate classification performance, especially for models that strongly exploit spatial context. The code associated with this paper is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [125] [Backend-Agnostic Sparse Attention for Fast High-Resolution Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08772)
*Liao Ma,Jiayi Song,Yunfeng Wu,Songhua Liu,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BASA：一种后端无关的稀疏注意力，用结构化移位局部窗口注意力替代DiT中的视觉自注意力，通过跨层窗口移位实现全局信息交换，在保持生成质量的同时实现接近理论值的实际加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT全注意力二次复杂度导致高分辨率生成昂贵；分块窗口注意力效率高但窗口孤立产生网格伪影；滑动窗口注意力能恢复跨窗口交互但计算不规则，理论加速与实际加速差距大且需硬件专用kernel。

Method: BASA将视觉自注意力替换为shited local-window attention，在DiT各层间采用结构化窗口移位方案，使被窗口边界分割的token在后续层中通信，从而实现全局信息交换并消除窗口伪影；不引入额外不规则算子或定制kernel，可直接部署于现有注意力后端。

Result: 在FLUX上实测加速超过理论估计的90%，在Wan上实现4.52倍注意力加速，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: BASA通过后端无关的结构化窗口移位稀疏注意力，兼顾视觉质量与实际加速，缩小了理论稀疏性与实际加速之间的差距，便于在现有注意力后端部署。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have achieved strong performance in image and video generation, but the quadratic complexity of full attention makes high-resolution generation computationally expensive. Window attention offers an efficient alternative, yet existing methods face a practical trade-off: partitioned window attention typically achieves computational efficiency consistent with its theoretical complexity. However, isolated windows block cross-window interaction, often introducing visible grid-like artifacts in the generated results. Fine-grained sliding-window attention effectively restores interactions across neighboring windows and improves visual quality. However, its irregular computation patterns create a substantial gap between theoretical and practical speedups and require specialized kernels tailored to each hardware backend. To tackle these challenges, we propose BASA, a backend-agnostic sparse attention, which brings the best of both worlds: visual quality and practical acceleration. Specifically, BASA replaces visual self-attention with shifted local-window attention. By introducing a structured window-shifting scheme across DiT blocks, we allow tokens divided by window boundaries in one layer to communicate in the following layers, thereby achieving global information exchange and eliminating window-induced visual artifacts. Notably, our design introduces no additional irregular operators or customized kernels, making it readily deployable on existing attention backends and closing the gap between theoretical sparsity and practical acceleration. Experiments demonstrate that BASA achieves measured speedups exceeding 90\% of the theoretical estimates on FLUX and delivers a 4.52$\times$ attention speedup on Wan while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [126] [CtrlCache: Accelerating Interactive Video World Models with Control-Aware Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08777)
*Shangye Song,Dong Gong,Hong Jia,Yun Sing Koh,Xinyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CtrlCache：一种免训练、控制感知的缓存框架，根据动作变化调度视频世界模型的去噪计算并复用残差，实现 1.21–1.41× 加速且提升 WBench 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型需按 chunk 自回归生成并快速响应用户控制，但每个 chunk 仍需多次昂贵的去噪迭代。已有免训练缓存策略仅依据模型内部去噪动态决策，忽略了控制转换这一提前可得、无需前向计算的关键信号。

Method: 分析发现动作变化处相邻 chunk 结构相似度骤降、低频结构比高频细节更持久。据此提出 CtrlCache：动作感知调度与刷新策略在 chunk 间与 chunk 内检测动作变化，将 chunk 标记为初始、过渡、转弯、稳态；在某内部去噪步，初始/过渡 chunk 保留全计算，转弯/稳态 chunk 复用同 chunk 内最近一次完整计算步的 transformer 残差。并引入频率混合历史先验引导，从上一干净 latent 引入互补信息且不增加 DiT 前向。

Result: 在 Matrix-Game 2.0 与 LingBot-World v1/v2 上，无需重训练即取得 1.21×–1.41× DiT 骨干加速，且三个模型的 WBench Overall 分数均优于原始推理。

Conclusion: 显式利用控制信号进行控制感知缓存，可在不重训练的前提下同时提升交互视频世界模型的生成效率与质量控制响应质量。

Abstract: Interactive video world models need to generate each video chunk efficiently while responding faithfully to user controls. Many systems use chunk-wise autoregressive generation with few-step denoising, but each chunk still requires several costly denoising iterations. Training-free caching can reduce this cost, yet existing policies make reuse decisions primarily from model-internal denoising dynamics and do not explicitly account for control transitions. Actually, interactive generation explicitly exposes a signal they do not use: the controls for a chunk arrive before it is denoised, so a schedule derived from them costs no forward pass. To this end, we analyze adjacent chunks under different control regimes and find that structural similarity drops around action changes, while low-frequency structure remains more persistent than high-frequency detail. Motivated by these observations, we propose CtrlCache, a training-free control-aware caching framework that adapts computation to the current control sequence. Specifically, the action-aware scheduling and refresh policy detects action changes across and within chunks, and labels each chunk as initial, transition, turning, or steady state. At one selected interior denoising step, initial and transition chunks retain full computation, while turning and steady chunks reuse the transformer residual from the most recent fully computed step in the same chunk. To exploit the persistence of low-frequency structure during steady interaction, we further introduce a frequency-mixed history prior guidance that incorporates complementary information from the preceding clean latent without an additional DiT forward pass. Evaluated on Matrix-Game 2.0 and LingBot-World v1/v2, CtrlCache achieves 1.21x to 1.41x DiT-backbone speedups without model retraining while improving WBench Overall scores over original inference across all three models.

</details>


### [127] [ALIVE: Interaction-Aligned Object Insertion for First-Frame-Guided Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08779)
*Zhenghong Zhou,Zhe Lin,Jiebo Luo,Yuqian Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALIVE：一种让插入对象"活起来"的视频编辑框架，通过编辑首帧+仅命名新增对象的指令，实现与源视频内容的连贯交互，无需用户额外输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑器能插入对象，但插入对象难以参与"被拿起/被操作"等交互，缺乏与源视频内容的协调行为，破坏源动作的完整性。

Method: 1) 构建35,800对编辑数据，融合3D渲染、模型生成、真实视频及ROSE通用编辑对，每对仅在目标对象是否存在上不同、保留周边动作，以教授对象行为协调与源内容保持；2) 训练VLM从相同输入预测交互引导；3) 提出ALIVE-interaction基准，用统一VLM协议评估交互保真度、源保持与视觉连贯性。

Result: 无VLM引导时，ALIVE在两个基准上Overall分别比最强基线提升43.9%和4.4%；VLM预测的引导可在无额外用户输入下再提升ALIVE-interaction分数0.95。

Conclusion: 通过配对数据学习对象协调行为并在推理时引入VLM预测的交互引导，可显著提升视频对象插入中的交互真实感与源内容保持能力。

Abstract: Current video editors can insert objects but often struggle to make them participate in interactions such as being picked up or manipulated. We introduce ALIVE, a framework that makes inserted objects "alive" through coherent interactions with the source video's contents, using an edited first frame and an instruction naming only the added object. We curate 35,800 editing pairs combining 3D-rendered, model-generated, and real-world videos with general editing pairs from ROSE. Each pair differs in the target object's presence while preserving the surrounding action, teaching editors coordinated object behavior and source preservation. We further train a vision-language model (VLM) to predict interaction guidance from the same inputs. We introduce the ALIVE-interaction benchmark to assess interaction fidelity, source preservation, and visual coherence using a unified VLM-based protocol, and evaluate on the general video object insertion benchmark. Without VLM guidance, ALIVE improves Overall over the strongest evaluated baseline by 43.9% and 4.4% on the two benchmarks, respectively. VLM-predicted guidance further improves the ALIVE-interaction score by 0.95 points without additional user inputs.

</details>


### [128] [4D-HOF: Hand-Object Flow Matching for Feed-Forward 4D Interaction Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08782)
*Shiqi Li,Sean Cho,Yijie Li,Fengzhi Guo,Bowen Wen,Cheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4D-HOF：基于视觉基础模型粗估计与条件流匹配的前馈式4D手-物交互重建框架，并在生成过程中引入测试时引导以注入物理约束与2D观测证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D手-物重建方法多依赖昂贵的逐序列优化，而生成式方法从随机噪声合成交互，导致交互预测不稳定。作者希望构建一个既免于逐序列优化、又能稳定利用物理先验的前馈框架。

Method: 将视觉基础模型输出的粗糙手-物状态作为起点，学习一个条件流匹配模型，把该状态输运至交互流形上，从而前馈地修正平移、旋转与对齐误差；关键设计是在输运过程中直接施加物理交互约束与观测到的2D证据进行测试时引导，使重构与生成过程融为一体，而非事后单独优化。

Result: 在跨域（out-of-domain）基准上取得state-of-the-art性能，生成的4D手-物重建更稳定、更准确，并在复杂in-the-wild场景中展现出稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 用条件流匹配替代随机噪声生成与逐序列优化，是提升手-物交互重建稳定性与泛化性的有效路径；将物理约束作为生成过程中的测试时引导而非后处理，是一种通用且可迁移的建模思路。

Abstract: Existing methods for 4D hand-object reconstruction often rely on costly per-sequence optimization, while generative approaches typically synthesize interactions from random noise, which can lead to unstable interaction prediction. We introduce 4D-HOF, a feed-forward framework that reconstructs 4D hand-object interactions from coarse but informative estimates produced by vision foundation models. Concretely, we learn a conditional flow matching model that transports foundation-model-derived hand-object states toward an interaction manifold, allowing the model to correct errors in translation, rotation, and alignment in a feed-forward manner. A key advantage of our generative formulation is that it naturally enables test-time guidance within the transport process. Rather than applying a separate post-hoc optimization after reconstruction, we directly steer the evolving generative states using physical interaction constraints and observed 2D evidence, allowing the reconstruction to be refined as part of the generative process itself. By training the generative model on diverse datasets, 4D-HOF generalizes robustly to challenging in-the-wild scenarios. Experiments on out-of-domain benchmarks show that 4D-HOF achieves state-of-the-art performance, producing more stable and accurate 4D hand-object reconstructions.

</details>


### [129] [Building Rome from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08790)
*Jiraphon Yenphraphai,Fang Li,Tianshuo Xu,Depu Meng,Quentin Herau,Yihan Hu,Raymond A. Yeh,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将物体中心式3D生成器（Trellis 2）改造为室内外通用的单图场景生成方法：自适应分块、2D-3D对应建模、合成约4000个室外场景数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练3D物体生成器具备强形状先验，但受限于固定规范体、面向孤立物体且以室内数据为主，室外3D数据稀缺，导致单图场景生成难以覆盖未观测区域。

Method: 三点设计：(a) 按相机距离自适应分块，近处小块保留细节、远处大块覆盖建筑等结构；(b) 通过提升图像特征并区分自由空间/已观测表面/未观测区域，使生成器具备显式2D-3D对应感知；(c) 合成约4000个室外场景扩充训练数据。

Result: 在Tanks and Temples、ScanNet++及真实场景图像上，室内外几何精度与感知质量均优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 以物体中心先验为起点，结合自适应分块与显式2D-3D对应，可有效扩展到室内外单图场景级前馈生成。

Abstract: Single-image scene generation aims to produce a complete 3D scene mesh from a single image, including surfaces the camera did not observe. While pretrained 3D object generators encode a strong shape prior, they are mainly designed for isolated objects in a fixed canonical volume and focus mostly on indoor scenes, since diverse 3D data for outdoor scenes are quite limited. In this work, we present a method that redesigns such an object-centric generator, e.g., Trellis 2, to work on both indoor and outdoor scenes while retaining its prior. We accomplish this by (a) partitioning the scene into adaptive chunks that scale relative to the distance to the camera; nearby chunks have a smaller size to keep the finer detail, while distant structures, e.g., buildings, are covered by large chunks; (b) making the generator capture explicit 2D-3D correspondence by lifting image features and making the model aware of the free space, observed surface, and unobserved region; (c) synthesizing around 4,000 outdoor scenes to broaden the training data, as existing scene datasets are largely indoor. Experiments on Tanks and Temples, ScanNet++, and in-the-wild images show that our method outperforms all baselines in geometric accuracy and perceptual quality across both indoor and outdoor scenes.

</details>


### [130] [World Models' Last Exam in Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08791)
*Mingju Gao,Qingle Liu,Yuzhao Peng,Xinjie Lin,Ziming Qin,Zheng Jiang,Wenyi Li,Calvin Xiao,Youjie Zheng,Kaisen Yang,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于测量的视频世界模型物理一致性评测基准 WML-EP（40 类受控任务，覆盖力学、光学、流体、热与相变、电磁、表面张力），无需参考视频，用任务特定物理量测量打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型可生成视觉逼真但物理不一致的序列，影响其在具身智能预测与规划中的可靠性；现有评测依赖模型判别或参考视频，直接物理检验又局限于力学。

Method: 构建 40 个受控任务，每个含初始图像、生成提示与预定义物理判据；评测器由任务可观测性筛选与任务特定定量物理测量组成，支持可解释的物理关系检验；在 8 个视频生成模型、1280 段视频上验证，并用物理关系已知的合成视频检验测量模块有效性。

Result: 八个模型普遍存在物理不一致且任务间差异大，最佳模型总分 57.76/100；测量模块在合成视频上得到有效性证据；评测器在任务内排序与成对比较中与人类判断的一致性高于直接使用 VLM 的基线。

Conclusion: 该基准以可测量证据与显式测量局限为评分基础，跨物理域提供可解释的物理不一致诊断能力，可作为追踪视频世界模型物理一致性进展的工具。

Abstract: Video world models can produce visually convincing yet physically inconsistent sequences, raising concerns about their reliability for prediction and planning in embodied AI systems. Existing evaluations often rely on model-based judgments or reference videos, while direct physical tests largely focus on mechanics. We introduce World Models' Last Exam in Physics, a measurement-based benchmark for evaluating physical consistency in video world models. The benchmark comprises 40 controlled tasks spanning mechanics, optics, fluids, thermal and phase-change phenomena, electromagnetism, and surface tension. Each task pairs an initial image and a generation prompt with predefined physical criteria, enabling interpretable tests of observable physical relationships without requiring reference videos. Its evaluator combines task-observability screening with task-specific quantitative physical measurements. Experiments on eight video generation models across 1,280 videos reveal persistent physical inconsistencies and substantial variation across tasks, with the best model achieving an overall score of 57.76 out of 100. Evaluation on synthetic videos with known physical relationships provides evidence for the validity of the measurement module under controlled conditions. The evaluator also achieves higher agreement with human judgments than a direct vision-language model baseline in both within-task rankings and pairwise comparisons. By combining coverage across physical domains with scores grounded in measurable evidence and explicit measurement limitations, the benchmark provides an interpretable basis for diagnosing physical inconsistencies and tracking progress toward physically consistent video world models.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Generalizable Robustness Testing of DNN-Based Robotic Navigation Systems via XAI-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06862)
*Khizra Sohail,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可解释性引导的多目标进化方法，为DNN控制的机器人系统生成稀疏鲁棒性测试扰动，并在Gazebo与真实LeoRover上验证仿真到真实的失败迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN鲁棒性测试多针对单张图像优化扰动且仅在仿真中评估，泛化性与实际有效性不足；需生成跨运行观测仍有效的测试，并检验失败能否从仿真迁移到真实机器人。

Method: 基于视觉与行为聚类选取代表性图像，聚合Integrated Gradients引导变异方向，多目标进化搜索生成稀疏扰动；在Gazebo的LeoRover上评估，做消融实验，并对分层子集在实体机器人上验证。

Result: 中位成功率70.0%（无引导53.85%），中位超体积0.65→0.73；多图像优化使成功率50.0%→57.5%，XAI引导进一步提至70.0%；仿真到真实迁移精度0.95、召回0.67，失败时间显著正相关0.617。

Conclusion: 多图像优化结合可解释性引导搜索可提升DNN控制机器人系统的鲁棒性测试效果；仿真能有效识别与排序可迁移失败，但部分真实失败无法在仿真复现，故实机验证仍属必要。

Abstract: **Context:** Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) increasingly control Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs), yet small input perturbations can cause unsafe system-level behavior. Existing approaches often optimize perturbations for individual images and evaluate them only in simulation, limiting their generalizability and practical validity. **Objectives:** This work aims to generate robustness tests that remain effective across operational observations and to evaluate whether the resulting failures transfer from simulation to a physical robot. **Methods:** We propose an explainability-guided multi-objective evolutionary approach that generates sparse perturbations over representative images selected through visual and behavioral clustering. Aggregated Integrated Gradients guide mutations toward influential image regions. We evaluate the approach on a DNN-controlled LeoRover in Gazebo, conduct an ablation study, and validate a stratified subset of perturbations on the physical robot. **Results:** The approach achieved a median success rate of 70.0%, compared with 53.85% for unguided search, and increased median hypervolume from 0.65 to 0.73. Multi-image optimization improved the success rate from 50.0% to 57.5%, while XAI guidance further increased it to 70.0%. In the sim-to-real evaluation, simulation achieved 0.95 precision and 0.67 recall, and simulated and physical failure times showed a significant positive correlation of 0.617. **Conclusion:** Combining multi-image optimization with explainability-guided search improves robustness testing for DNN-controlled robotic systems. Simulation effectively identifies and prioritizes transferable failures, but physical validation remains necessary because some real-world failures are not reproduced in simulation.

</details>


### [132] [RMRRT: Riemannian Barrier Metric RRT for Inequality-Aware Steering on Equality Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06863)
*Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Riemannian Barrier Metric RRT (RMRRT)，将等式约束与不等式约束统一到同一几何框架下，用于高维机器人系统的采样规划，并通过障碍度量偏置探索远离不等式边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统采样规划中，等式约束靠投影（projection）满足，不等式约束靠二元有效性检查（如碰撞检测）处理，二者割裂，导致探索效率低下：采样器无法感知约束边界，产生大量被拒绝样本。

Method: RMRRT 在等式约束流形上构造统一局部几何：先由对不等式敏感的 barrier 项构造环境空间（ambient）barrier metric，再通过与该等式约束相关的 (G)-正交投影诱导切空间度量。该切空间度量同时用于 steering 与最近邻选择，使探索偏向远离不等式边界，同时保持一阶等式一致性。度量由基于 signed distance 的几何代理不等式实例化，提供含碰撞信息的切空间方向；硬可行性仍由标准有效性检查单独保证。

Result: 在多种受约束操作任务中（仿真与真实世界）均达到 100% 成功率，相对代表性约束规划基线降低规划时间。消融实验表明该度量通过减少被拒绝样本、缩短路径长度提升了探索质量。

Conclusion: 将等式与不等式约束统一为单一黎曼几何形式，是提升高维受约束采样规划效率的有效途径；度量仅用于偏置探索，硬可行性仍需独立校验。代码与实验视频已开源。

Abstract: This paper presents a motion planning framework that unifies equality and inequality constraints within a single geometric formulation for sampling-based planning in high-dimensional robotic systems. In conventional sampling-based planners, equality constraints are typically enforced through projection, whereas inequality constraints are handled separately through binary validity checks such as collision testing, often leading to inefficient exploration. To address this limitation, we propose Riemannian Barrier Metric RRT (RMRRT), which constructs a unified local geometry for planning on equality-constrained manifolds. RMRRT first builds an ambient barrier metric from inequality-sensitive barrier terms and then induces a tangent-space metric via a (G)-orthogonal projection associated with the equality constraints. The resulting tangent-space metric is used consistently in both steering and nearest-neighbor selection, biasing exploration away from nearby inequality boundaries while preserving first-order equality consistency. In this work, the metric is instantiated from signed-distance-based geometric proxy inequalities to provide collision-informative tangent-space directions; hard feasibility is enforced separately through standard validity checks. Experimental results show that RMRRT achieves a 100% success rate across diverse constrained manipulation tasks in both simulation and real-world settings, while reducing planning time relative to representative constrained planning baselines. Ablation studies further demonstrate that the proposed metric improves exploration quality by reducing rejected samples and shortening path length. Experiment videos and source code are available at: this https URL

</details>


### [133] [Geometric Coherence via Weighted Matching for 3D Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reach-Avoid Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06882)
*Prajwal Vijay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究3D异构多智能体reach-avoid博弈中的任务分配质量，指出现有仅按基数匹配的方法在几何结构化场景下会出现"Geometric Sprawl"失效模式，并提出以HJI拦截值为次级权重的基数优先加权顺序匹配方法，在35个场景基准上显著优于无权基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体reach-avoid博弈中，最大基数匹配往往有多解，仅按基数进行分配会产生空间不连贯的配对，导致追击轨迹低效甚至求解超时。作者希望引入几何/博弈层面的信息（HJI拦截值）来区分同基数下的不同分配方案，从而提升分配质量与追击效率。

Method: 基于Yan等人的evasion-space框架，提出基数优先的加权顺序匹配：以Hamilton-Jacobi-Isaacs拦截值z_I(s,j)作为次级分配权重，每一阶段用min-cost max-flow求解；无权基线使用同一求解器但去掉权重项。在7个家族×5种初始化共35个确定性场景上，分别测试诊断性静止未匹配设置和带目标导向未匹配逃逸者的混合鞍点设置。

Result: 在诊断设置下，加权方法解决了15个几何压力测试实例中的13个（这些实例使无权基线反复超时）；在混合设置下，平均捕获数从3.20提升到3.91（+22%），平均拦截高度从3.87提升到5.36（+40%）。加权方法还呈现更低的路径曲折度和角度努力代理值，表明在一阶仿真模型中追击轨迹更平滑。

Conclusion: 几何感知的次级权重能有效缓解仅按基数匹配的Geometric Sprawl问题，提升3D reach-avoid博弈中任务分配的鲁棒性与效率。作者开源了仿真器与基准套件以支持可复现评估。

Abstract: We study assignment quality in 3D heterogeneous multi-agent reach-avoid games and identify a recurring failure mode of cardinality-only matching in geometrically structured scenarios, which we term \emph{Geometric Sprawl}. In these cases, multiple maximum-cardinality assignments are available, but some induce spatially incoherent pairings and inefficient pursuit trajectories. Building on the evasion-space framework of Yan et al.~\cite{yan2022}, we introduce a cardinality-first weighted sequential matching method in which the Hamilton--Jacobi--Isaacs interception value $z_I(s,j)$ is used as a secondary assignment weight. Each sequential stage is solved with a min-cost max-flow backend, while the unweighted baseline uses the same solver with the weight term removed. We evaluate both methods on a deterministic 35-scenario benchmark (7 families & 5 initialization variants) under two regimes: a diagnostic stationary-unmatched setting and a hybrid saddle-point setting with goal-directed unmatched evaders. On this benchmark, the weighted method resolves 13 of 15 geometric stress-test instances that cause repeated timeouts for the unweighted baseline in the diagnostic regime, and under the hybrid regime improves mean captures from $3.20$ to $3.91$ (22\% increase) and mean interception height from $3.87$ to $5.36$ (40\% increase). We also report lower path tortuosity and lower angular-effort proxy values, suggesting smoother pursuit trajectories in this first-order simulation model.
