We propose Physics-Guided Visual Prompting (PG-VP), a plug-and-play multimodal perception module that instead reuses what a frozen VLA model already does well: avoiding visible obstacles. Given a proximal radiation or thermal source, PG-VP performs a physics-guided risk assessment to determine the avoidance direction and overlays a corresponding virtual obstacle that moves across consecutive frames (Dynamic Visual Prompting). The navigation policy then naturally detours around this invisible hazard. The identical virtual obstacle is used regardless of hazard type, so the visual prompting pattern remains fixed as sensors are added. When no hazard is detected, nothing is rendered, and the policy behaves exactly as it would without PG-VP. We evaluate PG-VP on OmniNav using the val-unseen splits of R2R-CE and RxR-CE, where it guides the policy toward intended low-risk actions in 84.9% and 83.2% of cases, at a cost of 6.8 and 7.9 percentage points in navigation success rate. We further test it with distinct scenarios on a real robot in the presence of actual thermal and radiation sources, all without any retraining. The real test shows that PG-VP effectively avoids these invisible hazards, improving worst-10% average trajectory safety by 63.45% and 32.59% against thermal and radiation sources, respectively.

</details>


### [172] [OpenSplatGraph: From Dense Semantic Maps to Structured Scene Graphs for Open-Vocabulary Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07569)
*Binh Long Nguyen,Kien Nguyen,Clinton Fookes,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenSplatGraph：从在线高斯开放词汇语义地图直接构建持久3D场景图的统一框架，含可靠性感知语义场与查询条件化物体提取，支持语言引导物体定位与结构化关系推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅地图具备高保真几何与高效开放词汇感知，但语义以非结构化特征场表示，限制物体级推理；而3D场景图虽显式建模物体与关系，却多基于稀疏几何构建，未充分利用稠密语义地图。二者亟需统一。

Method: 在在线高斯开放词汇语义地图上增强一个可靠性感知语义场，维护轻量观测统计以支持置信度感知、查询条件化的物体提取；提取的物体实例与持久图节点关联，随观测与查询增量更新属性与关系，实现稠密语义建图与持久物体中心表示的紧耦合。

Result: 在标准3D场景理解基准与真实机器人实验中，在线开放词汇感知与下游机器人任务取得有竞争力的性能；同时保持高斯建图的几何保真度。

Conclusion: 将稠密开放词汇语义建图与持久物体中心图结构紧耦合，可行且有效，为机器人结构化感知提供统一方案；但摘要未给出量化指标、实时性数据及消融细节，实际增益需看正文与项目页。

Abstract: Dense 3D mapping with semantic understanding is essential for robotic perception in complex environments. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting-based mapping approaches enable high-fidelity geometry and efficient open-vocabulary perception, but typically represent semantics as unstructured feature fields that limit object-centric reasoning. In contrast, 3D scene graphs explicitly model objects and their relationships for structured reasoning, but are commonly constructed from sparse geometric representations that do not fully exploit dense semantic maps. In this work, we present OpenSplatGraph, a unified framework that constructs persistent 3D scene graphs directly from an online Gaussian-based open-vocabulary semantic map. The proposed framework augments the dense semantic map with a reliability-aware semantic field that maintains lightweight observation statistics for confidence-aware, query-conditioned object extraction. Extracted object instances are associated with persistent graph nodes, allowing object attributes and relationships to be incrementally updated across observations and queries. By tightly coupling dense semantic mapping with persistent object-centric representations, our framework supports both language-guided object grounding and structured relational reasoning while preserving the geometric fidelity of Gaussian-based mapping. Comprehensive evaluations on standard 3D scene understanding benchmarks and real-world robotic experiments demonstrate that OpenSplatGraph achieves competitive performance for online open-vocabulary perception and downstream robotic tasks. Project page: https://csiro-robotics.github.io/OpenSplatGraph.

</details>


### [173] [BiGym 2.0: Benchmarking Learned and Agent-Developed Policies for Humanoid Household Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07594)
*Zexi Zhang,Zecheng Zhu,Zidong Chen,Zulkhuu Tuya,Stephen James*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiGym 2.0：面向Unitree G1人形机器人的20项家务操作基准，统一全身控制器，含每任务60条VR人类演示，横向评测VLA微调、模仿学习、演示驱动RL与冷启动编码智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形家务操作要求手臂在身体平衡、迈步与姿态变换的同时执行操作，需要真实的全身协调评测平台；现有基准缺少统一全身控制、同步多相机的人类演示与跨方法公平对比，故将BiGym迁移到Unitree G1并构建统一评测套件。

Method: 在Unitree G1上适配BiGym，覆盖20项家务任务，使用统一全身控制器同时用于演示采集与评测；每任务提供60条原生人类VR演示，附同步多相机视角与全身执行记录。在相同onboard视角、本体感知与全身控制器条件下，对比VLA微调、模仿学习、演示驱动强化学习，以及给定在线RL交互预算的冷启动编码智能体。

Result: 在九任务均值上VLA微调最高；智能体自行编写的程序在该均值上超过所有演示驱动RL基线，并在双臂抓取上领先；跨工作空间堆叠仍未被解决，π0.5在pick-box上表现仍低；多物体运输对模仿学习、演示驱动RL与编码智能体均困难。

Conclusion: BiGym 2.0提供了统一全身控制下的人形家务评测协议与丰富人类演示，揭示VLA微调与代码生成智能体的相对优势及跨工作空间堆叠、pick-box、多物体运输等开放难题；环境、人类演示与评测轨迹均已开源。

Abstract: Humanoid household manipulation requires the arms to act while the body balances, steps and changes posture. We present BiGym 2.0, an adaptation of BiGym for the Unitree G1 across 20 household tasks using a unified whole-body controller for demonstration and evaluation. The suite provides 60 native human virtual-reality demonstrations per task with synchronised multi-camera views and full-body execution records. We benchmark vision-language-action fine-tuning, imitation learning, demo-driven reinforcement learning, and cold-start coding agents given the interaction budget of online reinforcement learning. With the same onboard views, proprioception and whole-body controller for every method, vision-language-action fine-tuning has the highest nine-task mean, and agent-developed programs outperform every demo-driven reinforcement learning baseline on this mean and lead on bimanual reaching. Cross-workspace stacking remains open, $\pi_{0.5}$ stays low on pick-box, and multi-object transport is hard for imitation learning, demo-driven reinforcement learning and coding agents. All environments, human demonstrations, and evaluation traces are open-sourced at this https URL .

</details>


### [174] [The Robot Is Not Its Description: GaugeBench for Representation Robustness in Morphology-Aware Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07597)
*Rahath Malladi,Arshia Sangwan,Rajesh K. Gupta,Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaugeBench 通过物理等价的“规范重写”隔离出机器人描述约定（关节轴方向、零位、命名/顺序）对形态感知策略的影响，发现描述改变比换机器人更具破坏性，并给出修复与训练级缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨形态评估通常变机器人而固定描述约定，导致“机制鲁棒性”与“表示鲁棒性”混淆；作者关心的是：机器人不变、仅描述改变时行为是否存活。

Method: 构建 GaugeBench：对固定机构施加物理等价的重描述（轴反转、零位平移、关节/连杆重排重命名），验证物理与策略接口不变，再评估同一策略权重；在 MetaMorph、ModuMorph 及 PyBullet 框架上做消融，区分描述派生特征与关节状态/力矩坐标的影响，并提出精确两描述传输与跨等价轴约定训练。

Result: MetaMorph 在 80 个熟悉机器人上得 4030.6，重描述后仅 51.6，而 98 个真正留出机器人得 1489.6；轴反转单独即可复现崩溃，零位变化几乎无害，重排居中；仅改关节状态与力矩坐标即可致失效，仅改描述派生特征不会。两描述传输可恢复原控制器，跨轴约定训练使轴反转下的保留回报从 3.6% 升至 80.6%。

Conclusion: 策略对“描述规范”远比对机制本身脆弱，跨形态评估必须同时测试机制鲁棒性与表示鲁棒性。

Abstract: A robot description does more than specify a physical mechanism: it also encodes arbitrary conventions, such as joint-axis direction, joint-angle zero, and the order and names of links and joints. Morphology-aware policies consume interfaces built from these descriptions, yet cross-embodiment evaluation typically changes the robot while keeping those conventions fixed. This leaves a simple question unanswered: does behavior survive when the robot stays fixed but its description changes? GaugeBench isolates this case by rewriting a fixed mechanism under physically equivalent conventions, verifying that its physics and policy interface are preserved, and then evaluating the same policy weights. The result is stark: three MetaMorph policies score 4030.6 on 80 familiar robots, but only 51.6 when those same robots are equivalently re-described, while 98 genuinely held-out robots score 1489.6. A new description can therefore be more damaging than a new robot. Tracing the failure reveals that axis reversal alone reproduces the collapse, joint-angle zero changes are nearly harmless, and reordering lies between them; moreover, changing joint-state and torque coordinates alone is sufficient to cause the failure, while changing description-derived features alone is not. The same phenomenon appears in ModuMorph and an unrelated PyBullet framework. Yet it is not irreversible: exact two-description transport restores the original controller, and training across equivalent axis conventions raises retained return under axis reversal from 3.6% to 80.6%. Together, these results separate mechanism robustness from representation robustness and show that cross-embodiment evaluation should test both.

</details>


### [175] [Modeling Latent Disturbances for Robust Decision-Making in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07599)
*Junwon Seo,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在世界模型潜空间中，将潜扰动建模为对学习动力学的悲观但合理的扰动，用动力学感知相似度+OOD检测构建不确定性集合、共形预测校准，通过博弈论优化联合求解鲁棒动作与最坏情况潜扰动，实现潜空间安全滤波与生成策略的采样-验证式策略引导，真机Franka上失败率分别降低70%和54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒优化需要显式动力学与物理意义上可解释的扰动，而世界模型的状态空间与动力学均从高维观测中学习得到，潜空间扰动缺乏物理含义，难以忠实表达底层系统不确定性，因此难以把鲁棒优化原则直接迁移到WM潜空间。

Method: 把潜扰动定义为对学习到的潜动力学的扰动，以诱发悲观但合理的想象转移；用动力学感知相似度度量刻画合理转移，用OOD检测排除不合理潜状态，从而构造合理潜动力学集合；用共形预测对该不确定性集合做校准，使其既保持合理又不过度悲观；随后以博弈论优化联合求解鲁棒动作与最坏情况潜扰动，并用于潜空间安全滤波和生成控制策略的采样-验证式策略引导。

Result: 受控仿真实验表明潜扰动可在WM潜空间直接支撑鲁棒决策；Franka机械臂真机实验中，安全滤波失败率降低70%，采样式策略引导失败率降低54%。

Conclusion: 在WM潜空间中定义并校准潜动力学扰动是可行且有效的，可为策略引导提供鲁棒性，无需显式物理动力学模型即可实现悲观但合理的鲁棒决策。

Abstract: In this paper, we study robust decision-making in the latent space of world models (WMs). Robust optimization is a mathematical framework where, given explicitly specified dynamics and physically meaningful disturbances, a robot can select actions that remain effective even under worst-case disturbances. However, applying this principle to the learned latent space of WMs introduces a fundamental challenge: because WMs have fully learned state spaces and dynamics inferred from high-dimensional observations, it is unclear how to define latent-space disturbances that faithfully represent uncertainty in the underlying system. Our key idea is to model a latent-space disturbance as a perturbation to the learned latent dynamics that induces pessimistic but plausible transitions. Specifically, we construct a set of plausible latent dynamics by combining a dynamics-aware similarity metric that captures plausible transitions with out-of-distribution detection that excludes implausible latent states. We calibrate this uncertainty set over latent dynamics using conformal prediction, ensuring that WM imaginations induced by the latent disturbance remain plausible without becoming overly pessimistic. We then jointly optimize robust robot actions and the worst-case latent disturbances through game-theoretic optimization. We leverage this latent-space robust optimization to robustify policy steering, considering two paradigms: latent safety filtering and sample-and-verify steering of a generative control policy. Our controlled simulation experiments show that our latent disturbance enables robust decision-making directly in WM latent spaces, and hardware experiments with a Franka manipulator show that modeling latent disturbances enables robust policy steering, reducing failures by 70% in safety filtering and 54% in sampling-based policy steering. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [176] [Learning Grasp Targeting from Point Clouds for Log Pile Clearing on a Hydraulic Crane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07613)
*George Sideris,Lucas Bessai,Heshan Fernando,Elie Ayoub,Nicolas Lemieux,Inna Sharf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向原木堆场液压抓具的抓取策略学习：从无分割点云中分类抓取点并预测深度与抓具姿态，同一网络支持模仿学习(BC)、强化学习(RL)与部署，仿真+12次现场试验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原木堆场中原木密集接触堆叠，每次抓取都会改变下一次可抓取的堆形；人工启发式规则难以适应这种动态耦合，且现场点云含支架轨道与立柱等干扰，需要端到端、可直接部署的学习策略。

Method: 以无分割(未滤波)点云为输入，策略网络在观测点上分类选择抓取点，并回归该点的深度与抓具朝向。同一网络结构支持三种训练范式：成功"堆顶"演示的行为克隆(BC)、在克隆策略上微调的RL(BC→RL)、以及从零训练的RL。仿真训练后在拖车式液压林业起重机上原样部署，与几何启发式基线对比。

Result: 仿真中BC在100个200根原木的堆中清空98个，BC→RL进一步改善负载稳定性。12次现场试验中，BC与BC→RL分别搬运池化库存的93.8%与88.9%，优于启发式的80.4%；BC→RL在83.6%的循环中成功放置原木(启发式79.6%、BC65.7%)；但仿真中的稳定性提升未迁移到真实起重机。策略仍能在含机架轨道与立柱的未滤波点云上工作。

Conclusion: 纯仿真训练的策略可零微调部署到真实液压臂，表现优于人工过滤的启发式规则，并对传感器噪声与场景杂物具鲁棒性；但RL的稳定性收益未实现sim-to-real迁移，提示仿真中稳定性相关的动力学建模仍需改进。

Abstract: In mill yards, log loaders clear dense piles by a sequence of bundle grasps: hundreds of logs rest in contact, and each removal changes the pile available to the next grasp. A learned policy chooses where to place and orient the grapple from unsegmented point clouds and runs on a trailer-mounted hydraulic forestry crane. The policy classifies at which observed point to grasp and predicts depth and grapple orientation there. The same network outputs support behavior cloning (BC), reinforcement learning (RL), and deployment. BC learns from successful top-of-pile demonstrations; RL explores for improvements by fine-tuning the cloned policy (BC$\to$RL) or by training from scratch. In simulation, BC clears 98 of 100 piles of 200 logs, while BC$\to$RL improves load stability. Twelve field trials compare a geometric heuristic, RL from scratch, BC, and BC$\to$RL through complete grasp-transport-deposit cycles. BC and BC$\to$RL deposit 93.8% and 88.9% of pooled inventory, against 80.4% for the heuristic. BC$\to$RL deposits logs on 83.6% of its cycles, against 79.6% for the heuristic and 65.7% for BC, while its simulated stability gain does not carry over to the crane testbed. Trained entirely in simulation and run unchanged on the crane, the learned policies clear more than the hand-filtered heuristic while observing unfiltered clouds that still contain the storage rack's rails and poles.

</details>


### [177] [Nine Trials to Recover: A Reproducible Benchmark for Repertoire-Free Soft-Robot Damage Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07616)
*Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无repertoire依赖的软体机器人损伤恢复基准，含9项在线试验；GP-EI参考控制器在75体队列上优于随机搜索、Sobol、CEM、CMA-ES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人损伤恢复研究缺乏可复用、可比较的评测协议：既有方法常依赖预置行为库（repertoire），且开发与评估混淆，难以衡量"学习式损伤先验"的实际增益。

Method: 构建repertoire-free基准：同形态内配对损伤掩码，保留实测nominal回退策略，开发集与评估集（新机体）分离。参考控制器采用高斯过程-期望改进（GP-EI），用3次初始化探测加6次反馈选择rollout自适应调整致动器相位。对比随机搜索、Sobol、CEM、CMA-ES（等预算）及TuRBO式局部GP。评估含固定控制器回放的恢复与几何边缘应变、no-added-demand部署门控，以及物理移除10%占据体素的冻结开发压力测试。

Result: 在两个互斥75体队列上，GP-EI在69/75确认体上提升最差掩码增益，平均最差掩码奖励超出四个基线0.235–0.310；TuRBO式局部GP最接近，配对置信区间含零。回放得5.06体素宽度平均恢复，最差掩码p99几何边缘应变增加0.0031；严格部署门控保留48.7%平均增益。冻结压力测试中GP-EI提升71/75体，平均最差掩码增益超官方BoTorch TuRBO 0.356。

Conclusion: 可回放控制器、体级推断与冻结队列评估为衡量学习式损伤先验及未来自适应方法的附加价值提供了参照基准；但增益同时伴随几何应变上升与部署门控下的显著折损。

Abstract: We present a repertoire-free benchmark for soft-robot damage recovery under nine online trials. The protocol pairs damage masks within each morphology, retains a measured nominal fallback, and separates method development from evaluation on new bodies. A Gaussian-process expected-improvement (GP-EI) reference controller adapts actuator phases using three initialization probes and six feedback-selected rollouts. Across two disjoint 75-body cohorts, it outperforms random search, Sobol, CEM, and CMA-ES under equal budgets. GP-EI improves the worst-mask gain on 69/75 confirmation bodies and exceeds these four baselines by 0.235-0.310 mean worst-mask reward. A TuRBO-style local GP is the closest comparator; the paired confidence interval includes zero. Post-confirmation fixed-controller replay finds 5.06 voxel widths of mean recovery together with a 0.0031 increase in worst-mask p99 geometric edge strain; a strict no-added-demand deployment gate retains 48.7% of mean gain. In a frozen development stress test that physically removes 10% of occupied voxels, GP-EI improves 71/75 bodies and exceeds official BoTorch TuRBO by 0.356 mean worst-mask gain. Together, the replayable controllers, body-level inference, and frozen-cohort evaluation provide a reference for measuring the added value of learned damage priors and future adaptation methods.

</details>


### [178] [TacZero: Training-Free Peg Insertion Using a General-Purpose Vision-Language Model with Tactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07621)
*Kazutoshi Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacZero 将预训练通用视觉语言模型（VLM）直接用作接触丰富操作的策略：把相机图像、机器人状态与三轴触觉响应（数值或矢量叠加在图像上）作为输入，由 VLM 输出目标末端位置与夹爪开合指令，交由底层控制器执行，全程无需触觉/操作训练或任务规则；在真实圆柱插销实验中数值触觉输入 20 次成功 15 次，无触觉输入 10 次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作要求机器人推断物体间接触及其引发的运动并据此选择动作。已有方法要么手工设计估计模型与触觉反馈控制律，要么为物体运动估计、动作结果预测与动作选择专门学习模型，均依赖任务特定训练或设计。作者希望验证：通用预训练 VLM 能否零样本、零额外训练地完成触觉-视觉接触推理与动作选择。

Method: 提出 TacZero：不对 VLM 做任何触觉或操作微调，不添加任务专用规则。观测编码方式包括相机图像、机器人状态，以及三轴触觉响应——既可表示为数值文本，也可表示为叠加在图像上的矢量。VLM 依据当前观测与交互历史生成命令（目标末端执行器位置、夹爪开/闭），由低层控制器执行，形成闭环。

Result: 真实世界圆柱插销（cylindrical-peg insertion）实验中，数值触觉输入下 20 次试验成功 15 次；无触觉输入时成功 10 次，说明触觉信息对 VLM 的接触推理有实质贡献。研究同时指出了沿此方向推进所面临的挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作为使用通用 VLM 进行接触丰富操作提供了具体起点，验证了"零触觉训练 + 通用 VLM"路线的可行性，并明确了后续需解决的开放性难点。

Abstract: Robots that autonomously determine their actions from language instructions and sensory observations could perform new contact-rich manipulation tasks without task-specific training or hand-designed rules. To perform these tasks, robots must infer how objects contact one another and move as a result, then select actions. For contact inference and action selection, prior approaches involve designing estimation models and tactile feedback control laws, or learning models for object-motion estimation, action-outcome prediction, and action selection from tactile data. Instead, we propose TacZero, which uses a pretrained general-purpose vision-language model (VLM) to interpret visual and tactile observations and select robot actions without additional tactile or manipulation training or task-specific rules for contact interpretation or action selection. TacZero provides the VLM with camera images, robot state, and three-axis tactile responses represented as numerical values or vectors overlaid on the images. From these observations and interaction history, the VLM generates commands specifying target end-effector positions and gripper opening or closing, which a low-level controller executes. In real-world cylindrical-peg insertion experiments, TacZero succeeded in 15 of 20 trials with numerical tactile input, compared with 10 of 20 without tactile input. This study provides a concrete starting point for further research on contact-rich manipulation using general-purpose VLMs and highlights challenges in pursuing this direction.

</details>


### [179] [Beyond Task Reward: A Controller-Restriction Protocol for Evaluating Embodiment-Dependent Competence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07629)
*Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"受限控制能力"（restricted-control competence）作为共设计形态的可报告属性：在显式声明的低复杂度控制器族、环境、任务与搜索预算下，测量形态保留的任务能力。实验显示其与常规任务奖励相关性低（方差解释率仅39%/33%/10%），重测信度高但对控制器族敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共设计（co-design）方法用"完全优化后的联合体任务奖励"这一单一数值评判形态，无法区分那些对控制器依赖程度差异极大的形态——某些形态的高奖励可能完全来自控制器的强拟合能力，而非身体本身优良。作者希望找到一种能刻画"形态自身固有能力"的评价方式。

Method: 提出受限控制能力指标：将控制器限制在显式声明的低复杂度控制器族内，在固定环境、任务与搜索预算下记录形态保留的任务能力。在三个 EvoGym 运动任务上验证，采用 run-grouped 交叉验证检验几何描述子的预测力，用 ICC(2,k) 评估优化器重启下的测量信度，并通过"按执行器序号相位驱动 vs 按位置排序相位"改变控制器族以测试指标依赖性。进一步将其用作第二搜索目标，以及在奖励-only 搜索的 top 奖励带内做形态选择。

Result: 任务奖励仅解释受限控制能力方差的39%、33%、10%；几何描述子无法预测。测量信度高（ICC(2,k)=0.956–0.986），但控制器族替换后同一批形态排名相关性仅 Spearman 0.50–0.63，并会反转奖励匹配的形态对。作为第二目标时，5 组配对运行中 3 组在匹配奖励下提升能力（未达预注册门槛 4 组）；作为奖励-only 搜索后的选择器，在 8 次有选择余地的运行中全部选出不同身体，奖励代价≤0.10，其中 6/8 在留出控制器族下得分更高。

Conclusion: 受限控制能力是共设计形态的一种可报告属性，但只能在定义它的控制器族语境下解释——它并非绝对量，而是"相对于某控制器复杂度假设"的能力度量。该结果为共设计评估提供了超越单一任务奖励的新维度，同时揭示了形态评价对控制器假设的强依赖性。

Abstract: Co-design methods optimize a robot's body and controller jointly and judge the result by one number, the task reward of the fully optimized pair. That number cannot separate morphologies whose competence depends on the controller to very different degrees. We evaluate a morphology by restricting its controller instead, recording the task competence it retains under an explicitly declared, low-complexity controller family, environment, task and search budget. On three EvoGym locomotion tasks, task reward explains only $39\%$, $33\%$ and $10\%$ of the variance in this quantity, and geometric descriptors do not predict it under run-grouped cross-validation. The measurement is reliable across optimizer restarts (ICC$(2,k) = 0.956$--$0.986$) but depends on the declared family: phasing the drive by actuator index instead of position ranks the same morphologies at Spearman $0.50$--$0.63$ and reverses reward-matched pairs. As a second search objective the axis improved competence at matched task reward in $3$ of $5$ paired runs, short of a pre-registered bar of $4$. Used after an ordinary reward-only search instead, to choose within its top task-reward band, it selected a different body in all $8$ runs offering a choice, at a cost of at most $0.10$ reward units, and in $6$ of $8$ that body also scored higher under a held-out family. Restricted-control competence is therefore a reportable property of a co-designed morphology, interpretable only with the controller family that defines it.

</details>


### [180] [OntoPlan: An Ontology-Grounded Scene Representation and Agentic Framework for Scalable Robot Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07649)
*Hyeongwoo Nam,Woongje Cho,Juwon Kim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出本体论场景表示与OntoPlan智能体框架，实现大场景长时程机器人任务规划，成功率0.89，token用量降低5.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM机器人任务规划在开放指令上表现良好，但在大环境长时程任务中退化：文本传递空间信息导致空间上下文丢失、token成本随环境规模增长；直接生成动作序列难以满足世界状态与动作前置条件。

Method: 构建本体论锚定的场景表示，将物体、空间、关系与状态对齐到共享符号词汇表以支持空间推理与规划；提出OntoPlan智能体框架，解析指令、选择性检索任务相关信息、形式化目标与约束并生成可执行计划。

Result: 在5个室内环境、3种场景规模、150个通用任务上，平均任务成功率0.89，最强基线0.27；每任务平均18.1k token，比最高效基线少约5.6倍；随场景规模增大优势保持，基线退化更严重且token更贵；对模糊或不可行指令能反问或报告信息不足而非输出无效计划。

Conclusion: 符号化本体表示与选择性检索的智能体框架可显著提升大场景长时程规划的鲁棒性与效率，并具备指令歧义与不可行性的处理能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based robot task planning is promising for open-ended instruction following, but degrades on long-horizon tasks in large environments. When spatial information is conveyed to the LLM through text, the model can fail to capture spatial context, and token cost grows with environment size. Generating action sequences directly with an LLM also makes it difficult to satisfy the current world state and action preconditions. We address this with an ontology-grounded scene representation that aligns objects, spaces, relations, and states in a shared symbolic vocabulary for spatial reasoning and task planning, and with OntoPlan, an agentic framework that interprets instructions, selectively retrieves task-relevant information, formalizes goals and constraints, and produces executable plans. Across 150 general tasks spanning five indoor environments and three scene scales, OntoPlan achieves 0.89 average task success, compared with 0.27 for the strongest baseline, while using 18.1k total tokens per task on average, about 5.6$\times$ fewer than the most efficient baseline. These advantages persist as scene scale increases, whereas prior methods degrade more sharply in success and remain far more costly in tokens. OntoPlan also responds appropriately to ambiguous or infeasible instructions by asking follow-up questions or reporting insufficient information rather than committing to invalid plans. Code available at this https URL .

</details>


### [181] [Silicon Language: A Robot-Native Knowledge Exchange Framework for Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07650)
*Yi Liu,Xianglin Meng,Chang Chen,Jingjing Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"Silicon Language"机器人原生知识交换框架，让机器人作为能力演化主体，通过知识包编码—发布—检索—翻译—本地试用—渐进融合与自动回滚实现跨本体能力复用；在榆林模拟矿井等场景完成30天概念验证部署，报告单能力适配时间由数天降至数小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨异构机器人复用能力仍需大量人工适配与验证：迁移技能往往意味着重新设计接口、重调参数并重新验证安全性。作者希望把机器人从被动的能力接受者变为自身能力演化的主动主体，从而降低跨本体迁移的工程与安全成本。

Method: 构建三层基础设施（边缘代理、Silicon Transfer Protocol 传输协议、知识中枢）与受人类学术体系启发的"能力栈"；接收方将自身经验编码为知识包并发布，检索同伴知识包后经翻译层适配自身传感器与执行器，通过独立的本地试验进行评估，即"接收端可用性评估"；采用渐进式融合与自动回滚机制降低负迁移风险。在榆林地上模拟矿井实验室部署30天，随后在户外沙路与室内厂房地面追加试验；两台异构机器人经操作员协助的文件拷贝，跨本体编码并翻译三类能力，源端"粉尘锁定跟随"行为在Taurus上记录。

Result: 项目记录显示单能力适配时间由数天缩短至数小时；作者明确将其表述为描述性部署记录而非受控测量。部署为跨本体知识交换提供了初步证据，但机队级自主演化与有无知识包的受控对比仍属未来工作。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了以机器人为主动主体的跨本体知识交换范式及配套协议与评估机制，并通过真实场地的概念验证部署给出初步可行性证据；方法学上缺乏受控实验与定量对比，属于早期探索性系统工作。

Abstract: Reusing a capability across heterogeneous robots still requires substantial human adaptation and verification: transferring a skill often means re-engineering interfaces, retuning parameters, and re-validating safety. We introduce Silicon Language, a robot-native knowledge exchange framework that treats the robot as the active subject of its own capability evolution. In this framework, a robot that wants a capability encodes its own experience into knowledge packets, publishes them, retrieves peer packets, translates them for its own sensors and actuators, and reviews them through independent local trial. A receiver-side usability evaluation procedure lets each robot decide for itself whether an external packet is useful, and progressive blending with automatic rollback is designed to reduce the risk of negative transfer when adopting it. The system combines three infrastructure layers (edge agent, Silicon Transfer Protocol (STP), and knowledge hub) with a capability stack inspired by the human scholarly system. We report a 30-day proof-of-concept deployment at an above-ground simulated-mine laboratory in Yulin, with following trials on an outdoor sand road and an indoor factory floor. Two heterogeneous robots encoded and translated three capabilities across embodiments through operator-assisted file copies mediated by the Silicon Language translation layer; source-side trials of the dust-locked following behavior were recorded on Taurus. Project records indicate that per-capability adaptation time dropped from days to hours; we present these figures as descriptive deployment records rather than controlled measurements. The deployment provides initial evidence for cross-embodiment knowledge exchange; fleet-level autonomous evolution and controlled with/without-packet comparisons remain future work.

</details>


### [182] [SMART: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Articulated Object Manipulation via Large-Scale Synthetic Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07652)
*Jicong Ao,Shuhan Jiang,Yuling Zhong,Yanwen Liu,Yuhan Gao,Jiangyuan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Shiqiang Zhu,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SMART 系统，含关节感知仿真平台 SMART-Sim 与分布式合成管线，生成超 100 万条演示（SMART-Data，44 类原子任务、5 种机器人配置、2507 个铰接物体），预训练 VLA 在仿真基准具竞争力，并实现零样本 sim-to-real 与真实世界可扩展操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接物体交互依赖精密接触与约束跟随，真实演示采集成本高；已有合成数据覆盖类别有限，通用合成管线缺少部件级语义与关节约束的显式设计，难以规模化生成高质量铰接操作演示。

Method: SMART-Sim：关节感知仿真平台，支持任务生成与高效演示采集；在其上引入智能体式（agentic）任务生成，并设计分布式可扩展合成系统，批量产出 SMART-Data；用该数据预训练 VLA 模型。

Result: SMART-Data 含 1M+ 演示，覆盖 44 种原子任务、5 种机器人配置、2507 个铰接物体；预训练 VLA 在仿真基准上表现有竞争力，并实现零样本 sim-to-real 与真实世界铰接物体操作的可扩展性能。

Conclusion: 合成演示可为接触密集型铰接物体操作提供有效且可扩展的监督信号，提升 VLA 模型性能。

Abstract: The ability to interact with articulated objects is essential for embodied intelligent systems, but collecting large-scale real-world demonstrations for these interactions remains challenging due to the precise contact and constraint-following motions involved. Although simulation provides a promising alternative, existing synthetic data efforts cover limited articulated-object categories, while general-purpose synthesis pipelines lack explicit designs for part-level semantics and articulation constraints, hindering agentic task generation and scalable synthesis of high-quality articulated-manipulation demonstrations. To bridge this gap, we introduce SMART, a scalable system leveraging large-scale Synthesized Manipulation demonstrations for ARTiculated-object manipulation. At its core, we develop SMART-Sim, a simulation platform with articulation-aware design that enables effective task generation and efficient demonstration collection. Building on SMART-Sim, we apply agentic task generation and design a scalable distributed synthesis system, using them to synthesize SMART-Data, comprising over 1M demonstrations across 44 atomic task types, 5 robot setups, and 2,507 articulated objects. The vision-language-action (VLA) model pretrained on SMART-Data shows competitive performance on simulation benchmarks and achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer and scalable performance in real-world articulated-object manipulation tasks. This highlights the potential of synthetic demonstrations in providing effective and scalable supervision for improving VLA model performance in contact-rich articulated-object manipulation.

</details>


### [183] [EigenDEXplore: Structured Exploration for Dexterous Manipulation with Human Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07681)
*Harsh Gupta,Tyler Ga Wei Lum,Changhao Wang,Chuer Pan,C. Karen Liu,Jeannette Bohg,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究人类运动先验在灵巧操作中的使用方式，发现将其用于"结构化探索"而非"改变动作表示"更有效，并提出EigenDEXplore：在关节空间独立噪声上沿人类数据导出的特征向量方向添加相关扰动，不改变动作空间。在多种灵巧手、抓取/手中重定向/接触丰富操作任务上，一致优于关节空间与学习动作空间基线，且覆盖无结构RL、参考引导RL、轨迹优化与sim-to-real。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作是高维优化问题，有效行为需多关节协同；RL与采样优化常用独立关节扰动，难以发现协同行为。已有工作用人类手部数据学习低维协同关节空间来缩小搜索空间，但限制了通用操作所需的表现力；将学习动作空间与关节空间结合虽恢复表现力，却增加维度并引入冗余。作者因此想厘清：人类运动先验到底该改变动作表示，还是只用于引导探索。

Method: 系统性研究动作维度、探索策略与人类数据来源的影响，得出"人类先验更适合结构化探索"的结论。据此提出EigenDEXplore：对关节空间独立噪声施加沿人类数据特征向量（eigenvectors）的相关扰动，从而诱导协同探索，同时保持原动作空间不变，避免增加维度与冗余。

Result: 在多种灵巧手上，EigenDEXplore在抓取、手中重定向（in-hand reorientation）和接触丰富操作任务中一致优于关节空间与学习动作空间基线；增益跨无结构RL、参考引导RL、轨迹优化及sim-to-real部署；在奖励塑形与课程设计较少的设置中增益最大。

Conclusion: 人类运动先验的最佳用法是引导/结构化探索而非修改动作表示。EigenDEXplore以极简改动（关节噪声加相关扰动）获得跨任务、跨算法、跨sim-to-real的稳定提升，且越少依赖奖励塑形与课程设计收益越显著，为灵巧操作探索提供了通用且低成本的方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation poses a challenging high-dimensional optimization problem, as useful behaviors require coordinated motion across many hand joints. In reinforcement learning (RL) and sampling-based trajectory optimization, exploration commonly relies on independent robot joint perturbations, making coordinated behaviors difficult to discover. Prior work reduces this search space for grasp learning using low-dimensional spaces of coordinated joint motions learned from human hand data, but this restricts the expressivity required for general manipulation. Some combine learned and joint-space actions to restore expressivity, but this increases dimensionality and introduces redundancy. We study these effects across diverse manipulation settings, varying action dimensionality, exploration strategy, and the source of human data. Our experiments suggest that human-motion priors are most effective when used to structure exploration rather than change the action representation. Motivated by this finding, we propose EigenDEXplore, which induces correlated exploration by adding perturbations along human-derived eigenvectors to independent joint-space noise, leaving the action space unchanged. Across multiple dexterous hands, EigenDEXplore consistently outperforms joint-space and learned action-space baselines in grasping, in-hand reorientation, and contact-rich manipulation. These gains span unstructured and reference-guided RL, trajectory optimization, and sim-to-real deployment, and are largest in settings with less reward shaping and curriculum design.

</details>


### [184] [ExoBridge: Learning a Bare Hand to Hand-Worn Exoskeleton Mapping through Human Limb Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07692)
*Ruitong Tian,Xianyao Li,Noah B. Wilson,Fang Xu,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExoBridge 利用人类双手协同行为，从裸手视频学习到带触觉传感外骨骼状态的桥接函数，从而为灵巧操作提供运动与触觉监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频是灵巧机器人学习的可扩展经验来源，但从视频中获得运动与触觉监督、同时保持裸手自然交互十分困难。现有带传感器的演示会干扰裸手交互，而裸手视频缺乏触觉与精确运动标注。

Method: 收集时一只手保持裸手提供视觉观测，另一只手佩戴带传感的外骨骼提供同步的运动与触觉测量；利用双手协同配对演示，训练一个时序视觉模型，仅从裸手视频预测指尖接触、连续触觉强度与相对编码器运动。外骨骼定义中间状态空间，其运动坐标通过已有标定与灵巧机械手关联。

Result: 在 4 个操作任务、共 1,215 条演示上进行留出采集会话评估，得到汇总的任意接触 AUROC 为 0.916，触觉强度 Pearson 相关系数为 0.790；模型还能从裸手视频预测外骨骼构型的相对变化。

Conclusion: 人类肢体耦合可将外骨骼测量转化为裸手视频的监督信号，建立了从人类视觉演示到面向机器人的操作接口之间的可学习桥梁，为人视频驱动的灵巧操作学习提供了新的可扩展范式。

Abstract: Human video offers a scalable source of experience for dexterous robot learning, but obtaining motion and tactile supervision while preserving bare hand interaction remains challenging. We present ExoBridge, a framework that leverages human limb coupling to learn a bridging function from bare hand video to the motion and tactile state of a sensorized exoskeleton. Our central idea is to use coordinated bimanual behavior to connect an uninstrumented visual source with a measured manipulation interface. During collection, one hand remains bare and provides visual observations, while the opposite hand wears the exoskeleton and supplies synchronized motion and tactile measurements. These paired demonstrations train a temporal visual model to predict fingertip contact, continuous tactile intensity, and relative encoder motion from bare hand video alone. The exoskeleton defines an intermediate state space whose motion coordinates are linked to a dexterous robot hand through existing calibration. Evaluation on 1,215 demonstrations across four manipulation tasks uses held out collection sessions and yields a pooled any contact AUROC of 0.916 and a Pearson correlation of 0.790 for tactile intensity. The learned bridge also predicts relative changes in exoskeleton configuration from bare hand video. These results demonstrate that human limb coupling can turn exoskeleton measurements into supervision for bare hand video, establishing a learned bridge between human visual demonstrations and a robot oriented manipulation interface.

</details>


### [185] [ESP: Energy-Score Policy for One-Step Multimodal Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07696)
*Lilika Makabe,Heecheol Kim,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ESP（Energy-Score Policy），一种无需教师的策略，将策略上下文与噪声单次网络前向直接映射为动作块，用能量分数取代 MSE 训练动作头，在不迭代采样的前提下学习多模态动作分布，显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散与流匹配的生成式动作模型虽能建模同一观测-指令下的多种有效动作序列，但其迭代采样需反复网络评估，导致闭环控制中推理延迟高。作者希望保留多模态建模能力，同时消除迭代采样开销。

Method: ESP 将策略上下文与噪声经单次前向映射到动作块（action chunk），动作头以能量分数（energy score）而非均方误差作为训练目标。由于能量分数是严格 proper scoring rule，其期望值唯一地被目标分布最小化；在模型可表示目标分布时，总体最优解可精确恢复目标分布。由此构成免迭代采样的多模态动作分布学习范式，且无需教师模型蒸馏。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界操作任务上，ESP 取得与流匹配基线相当的任务成功率，同时动作生成延迟显著更低。结果表明直接分布学习可作为迭代式生成机器人策略的高效替代方案。

Conclusion: 单次前向的能量分数式分布学习在保持任务成功率的同时大幅降低动作生成延迟，为 VLA 策略提供了一种高效、有理论依据的免迭代生成替代路径。

Abstract: Generative action models based on diffusion and flow matching have been increasingly adopted in vision-language-action (VLA) policies for their ability to capture diverse behaviors, including multiple valid action sequences under the same observation and instruction. Their iterative sampling procedures, however, require repeated network evaluations to generate each action chunk, increasing inference latency in closed-loop control. We propose ESP (Energy-Score Policy), a teacher-free approach that maps policy context and noise directly to an action chunk in a single network evaluation. ESP trains the action head with the energy score rather than mean squared error. Whereas squared-error regression targets the conditional mean, the energy score is strictly proper: its expected value is uniquely minimized by the target distribution. This provides a principled objective for learning multimodal action distributions without iterative sampling, with exact recovery at the population optimum when the model can represent the target distribution. Experiments on both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate competitive task success with substantially lower action-generation latency than the flow matching baseline. These results support direct distributional learning as an efficient alternative to iterative generative robot policies.

</details>


### [186] [Seeing Through the Displaced Frame: Privileged Noise Distillation for Vision-Force Precision Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07745)
*Ching-Hsiang Chang,Tzu-Yu Chuang,Yi-Hsiu Lee,Yi-Ting Chen,Yuan-Fu Yang,Min Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在FORGE精密装配基准上，位姿噪声（σ=5mm）会使官方状态策略成功率从97-99%崩到32-60%，原因是估计位姿同时进入观测并锚定动作坐标系，该偏移无法仅从本体感知中辨识。作者在训练期以两种方式注入这一缺失信息：特权教师（仿真中直接观测偏移）与闭式重标注（把干净示教转换到位移坐标系）。学生用BC加一轮DAgger训练，测试时仅用带噪状态、原始力/力矩窗口和两个RGB相机。学生在σ=0-5mm下保持92-99%成功率，远超六个非特权基线；匹配的BC消融实验显示重标注示教达95.8%、特权示教88.7%、无偏移访问仅31.5%。零样本部署到Franka上，σ=5mm时83.3%，而最强状态策略仅34.4%。结论：仅有可部署感知不足，鲁棒性需要编码潜坐标系补偿的监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密装配中位姿误差不仅污染机器人观测，还污染其动作所在的坐标系；由于同一估计位姿既进入观测又定义动作帧，接触前仅靠本体状态无法辨识这一潜在偏移，导致状态策略在毫米级位姿噪声下性能骤降。作者的目标是找出并验证一种能在不改变部署感知配置的前提下恢复该鲁棒性的训练监督方式。

Method: 在FORGE基准上，作者用两种训练期特权信息补充缺失的坐标系偏移：一是特权教师在仿真中直接观测偏移；二是闭式地把干净示教重标注到位移坐标系（无需仿真特权）。部署学生先用行为克隆训练，再加一轮DAgger微调；推理时输入仅含带噪状态、原始wrench（力/力矩）时间窗与两路RGB相机。为隔离鲁棒性来源，作者做了匹配的行为克隆对照实验：统一学生架构、数据预算和训练流程，仅改变示教生成中是否含偏移信息（无偏移/特权/重标注）。最后在Franka真机上做零样本部署并与最强状态策略对比。

Result: FORGE原任务上，教师路线学生在σ=0到5mm区间保持92-99%成功率，六个非特权基线跌到2-80%。匹配BC实验中，σ=5mm下无偏移示教仅31.5%，特权示教88.7%，重标注示教95.8%（重标注甚至优于特权，可能因示教质量更干净）。Franka零样本部署在σ=5mm时汇总成功率83.3%，对比最强状态策略34.4%。

Conclusion: 仅依靠可部署的感官输入（相机+力觉+带噪状态）不足以应对毫米级位姿误差；鲁棒性来自训练时对隐式坐标系偏移的显式补偿监督。闭式重标注是一种不依赖仿真的实用替代方案，且在真机上验证了跨域（仿真到Franka）有效性。

Abstract: Pose error in precision assembly can corrupt not only what a robot observes but also the coordinate frame in which it acts. On the FORGE benchmark, the official state-based policy succeeds in 97% to 99% of episodes with the true pose but only 32% to 60% at the benchmark's $\sigma=5$ mm pose-noise setting. The same estimated pose enters the observation and anchors the action frame, making the offset unidentifiable from proprioceptive state alone before contact. We supply this missing information during training in two ways. A privileged teacher observes the offset in simulation, while clean demonstrations can instead be relabelled into the displaced frame in closed form. The deployed student is trained with behaviour cloning followed by one DAgger round and receives only noisy state, a raw wrench window, and two RGB cameras at test time. On the unmodified FORGE tasks, the teacher-route student maintains 92% to 99% success across $\sigma=0$ to 5 mm, while six non-privileged baselines fall to 2% to 80%. A matched behaviour-cloning experiment isolates the source of this robustness. With the same student architecture, data budget, and training procedure, demonstrations generated without offset access yield only 31.5% success at $\sigma=5$ mm, whereas privileged and relabelled demonstrations reach 88.7% and 95.8%. Deployed zero-shot on a Franka, the student reaches 83.3% pooled success at $\sigma=5$ mm against 34.4% for the strongest state-based policy. Deployable sensing alone is insufficient. Robustness requires supervision that encodes compensation for the latent frame this http URL page: this https URL

</details>


### [187] [CoRE: Learning Collaboration-Role Experts for Decentralized Collaborative Manipulation with One Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07752)
*Yanan Zhou,Zhaoyan Qian,Zihao Li,Mingyuan Ba,Ranpeng Qiu,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoRE框架，用共享策略的单策略去中心化协作：从多任务多机器人演示中学习"协作角色专家"，结合外观与几何局部证据、查询条件交叉注意力专家和局部路由器，在无角色标签、无通信、无任务提示下实现互补动作选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作操作要求机器人随交互进展执行互补动作。作者研究"单策略去中心化协作"：各机器人仅凭自身视觉与本体感觉运行同一策略，不使用任务提示、身份标签或机器人间通信。核心难点在于在共享参数中学习互补的团队行为，并仅从各自的局部观测中选择恰当动作。

Method: CoRE从汇总的多任务、多机器人演示中学习协作角色专家。融合外观与几何信息以提供局部交互证据；查询条件的交叉注意力专家提供可适配的预测路径，由局部路由器在每个动作块位置上组合。训练时引入动作-专家对齐损失，在固定输入下以相对强制路由预测误差对齐专家选择，无需角色标签。

Result: 在多项仿真基准中，CoRE在评估的去中心化方法里取得最高平均性能。物理实验显示其可在多样操作任务中有效协作，并对伙伴延迟与降速具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了在共享参数、无通信与无标签条件下学习互补协作行为的可行性，为去中心化多机器人协作提供可扩展方案，并展现出对伙伴时序扰动的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Collaborative manipulation requires robots to perform complementary actions as interactions unfold. We study single-policy decentralized collaboration: every robot runs the same policy from its visual observations and proprioception, without task prompts, identity labels, or inter-robot messages. The challenge is to learn complementary team behaviors within shared parameters and select appropriate actions from each robot's local observations. We introduce CoRE, which learns Collaboration-Role Experts from pooled multi-task, multi-robot demonstrations. Fused appearance and geometry provide local interaction evidence. Query-conditioned cross-attention experts provide adaptable prediction paths, which a local router combines at each action-chunk position. During training, an action-expert alignment loss supervises expert selection using relative forced-route prediction errors against demonstrations under fixed inputs, without role labels. Across simulation benchmarks, CoRE achieves the highest average performance among evaluated decentralized methods. Physical experiments demonstrate effective collaboration across diverse manipulation tasks and robustness to partner delays and slowdowns. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [188] [StairVLA: Stage-Aware Hierarchical Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07756)
*Shangyuan Yuan,Xinda Qi,Yujiang Pu,Wenliang Guo,Xiaobo Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StairVLA 分层动作生成框架，利用扩散去噪过程的条件关注度阶段偏移，以“部分去噪动作”作为高低层接口，高层 VLA 低频生成粗长时程轨迹、轻量 refiner 高频局部修正，在保持成功率的同时显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽采用扩散/流匹配动作头生成连续动作，但普遍对整条去噪轨迹做统一处理。作者观察到去噪阶段的条件关注天然发生偏移：早期阶段融合语言指令与视觉观测以建立粗动作轨迹，后期更依赖当前视觉观测进行动作对齐。这一阶段差异尚未被利用，同时高层 VLA 推理开销大、闭环修正频率不足。

Method: StairVLA 采用阶段感知的分层动作生成：以部分去噪动作作为高层粗生成与局部精修的天然接口。高层 VLA 只执行早期去噪步骤，产出可复用的长时程部分去噪动作轨迹；轻量级 refiner 以更高频率结合最新观测对局部动作块进行细化。该设计摊薄昂贵的高层 VLA 计算，同时保留频繁的闭环纠错能力。文中给出 GR00T 风格实例化，并在两个 VLA 骨干、仿真基准与真机任务上验证。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，GR00T 风格实例化将平均成功率从 96.5% 提升至 97.8%，同时把每个动作块的摊薄推理延迟从 115.0 ms 降至 44.2 ms。跨两个 VLA 骨干、仿真基准和真机任务，StairVLA 在维持强任务性能的同时一致地降低推理成本。

Conclusion: StairVLA 表明扩散/流匹配动作头的去噪阶段存在可利用的条件关注偏移，将其显式分层可同时改善成功率与推理效率。以部分去噪动作为接口的解耦设计为 VLA 高频闭环控制提供了一条可行路径，且具备跨骨干与真机迁移性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly rely on diffusion- or flow-matching-based action heads to generate continuous robot actions. These action heads typically process the denoising trajectory in a largely uniform manner. However, we observe that the conditioning focus naturally shifts across denoising stages: early stages combine language instructions and visual observations to establish a coarse action trajectory, whereas later stages place greater emphasis on current visual observations for action alignment. Based on this insight, we introduce StairVLA, a stage-aware hierarchical action generation framework that uses partially denoised actions as a natural interface between coarse long-horizon action generation and local refinement. A high-level VLA performs early denoising to produce a reusable long-horizon partially denoised action trajectory, while a lightweight refiner operates at a higher frequency to refine local action chunks using the latest observations. This design amortizes expensive high-level VLA computation while preserving frequent closed-loop correction. On LIBERO, our GR00T-style instantiation improves average success from 96.5% to 97.8% while reducing amortized inference latency from 115.0 ms to 44.2 ms per action chunk. More broadly, across two VLA backbones, simulation benchmarks, and real-robot tasks, StairVLA consistently reduces inference cost while maintaining strong task performance.

</details>


### [189] [Model-Based Geometry-Aware Generative Optimization for Constrained Locomotion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07772)
*Zhilin He,Xinyuan Wang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出2GO框架，将主动约束几何转化为可执行的去噪算子（法向诱导度量整形、切空间随机滤波、CFS回缩），并用自适应扩散/流式调度解耦生成传输与反向随机性，用于四足与人形机器人的受限运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限运动规划需同时满足避碰、接触一致性、运动学可行性与支撑约束，在高维动力学与非凸环境下极难求解。现有Model-Based Diffusion方法虽无需示教即可解析估计去噪score，但缺少任务调制的主动约束几何来引导score方向与反向随机性，且采用确定性DDPM式反向传输、缺乏跨生成传输的自适应调度。

Method: 2GO：以法向诱导度量整形、切空间随机滤波与CFS-based retraction将主动约束几何转化为可执行的去噪算子；通过自适应扩散与flow-like调度解耦生成传输与反向随机性。

Result: 在四足与人形受限运动任务（离散落脚点选择、连续姿态规划）上取得更高成功率、更少约束违反与更好的执行兼容性。

Conclusion: 将约束几何内嵌为生成过程的去噪算子并结合自适应调度，可有效提升受限运动规划的可行性与执行可用性。

Abstract: Constrained Locomotion Planning (CLP) for quadrupeds and humanoids, where robots must satisfy collision avoidance, contact consistency, kinematic feasibility, and support constraints, is challenging under high-dimensional dynamics and highly non-convex environments. Recent Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) approaches recast trajectory optimization as posterior sampling over trajectories, using known dynamics and Monte Carlo rollouts to analytically estimate the denoising score function without demonstration learning. While constrained variants further incorporate feasibility into model-based score rollouts and show promising performance, they are still limited by (1) lacking a task-modulated active constraint geometry that shapes the score direction and reverse stochasticity, and (2) using deterministic DDPM-style reverse transport without adaptive scheduling across different generative transports. Therefore, we introduce Model-Based Geometry-Aware Generative Optimization (2GO) for constrained locomotion, which turns active constraint geometry into executable denoising operators through normal- induced metric shaping, tangent-space stochastic filtering, and CFS-based retraction. 2GO further decouples generative transport from reverse stochasticity through an adaptive diffusion and flow-like schedule. Experiments on constrained quadruped and humanoid locomotion demonstrate strong performance in discrete foothold selection and continuous posture planning, with higher success rates, fewer violations, and improved execution compatibility.

</details>


### [190] [Multi-Robot Multi-Goal Motion Planning with Stochastic Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07784)
*William Schnyder,Valentin N. Hartmann,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将随机性操作技能（执行时间可变、轨迹不确定）整合进基于采样的多机器人运动规划，把问题建模为多模态复合路线图上的 MDP，求解后得到可反应式调整的动态协调策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人日益成组部署并共享工作空间，需要在执行具有连续物理过程、随机性（执行时间可变、轨迹不确定）的操作技能时并发规划运动。现有规划器要么限于单机器人，要么使用开环路径，要么只在事后调度，无法动态协调。

Method: 在多模态复合路线图（composite roadmap）上形式化 MDP；对随机技能通过求解 MDP 得到反应式策略，使可控机器人在规划阶段就吸收技能不确定性，从而根据其他机器人的技能执行情况动态调整自身运动；采用基于采样的多机器人规划框架。

Result: 求解 MDP 得到可反应式策略，使机器人能动态响应其他机器人的操作执行；在规划时直接消解技能不确定性，避免保守基线的悲观性，实现稳健的动态多机器人协调。规划器代码已开源。

Conclusion: 在多机器人规划中显式处理随机操作技能，可替代开环路径与事后调度，获得更优的动态协调；未来可扩展到更多技能类型与更大规模团队。

Abstract: As robots are increasingly deployed in groups and share workspaces to execute real-world tasks, planning their concurrent motions around complex manipulation skills becomes essential. These skills involve continuous physical execution and may exhibit stochastic behavior, resulting in variable execution times and uncertain continuous trajectories. Existing planners either limit execution to single-robot scenarios, rely on open-loop paths, or use post-hoc scheduling that prevents dynamic coordination. In this paper, we address this gap by integrating stochastic skills into sampling-based multi-robot planning by formulating the problem as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) over a multi-modal composite roadmap. For stochastic skills, solving the MDP yields a reactive policy that allows controllable robots to dynamically adapt their motions in response to other robots' execution of manipulation skills. By resolving skill uncertainty directly at planning time, this approach avoids the pessimism of conservative baselines and unlocks robust, dynamic multi-robot coordination. Code for the planners is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [191] [CUSP: CUSUM-Governed Survival Hazard Alarms at the Perception Onset for Off-Road Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07882)
*Inuk Kang,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CUSP，一种模型无关的运行时危险报警器：利用离散时间生存模型在人工干预日志中学习"感知起始点"（人判断驾驶由安全转向不安全的时刻），再用 CUSUM 累积风险触发报警，在 5 个未见过场地、相同误报率下检出 85 个事件，远超 9 个基线中最好的 26 个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野导航中，学习型导航虽用安全监督选路，但机器人沿该路径行驶时缺乏运行时危险报警。难点在于：报警所需信号只能从现场日志中学习，而人类干预先于失败发生，导致失败本身从未被观测到；干预时刻标记的是"危险已临近"的晚期判断，而运行时报警需要检测的是更早的"感知起始"判断，此前无监督方法针对这一点。

Method: 将"安全转向不安全"的判断时刻定义为 perception onset，并与干预时刻分开标注。训练视觉 hazard head：以标注的 onset 为目标、用离散时间生存目标函数，使有/无 onset 的驾驶片段都提供监督信号；再用 CUSUM 将预测的 onset 风险累积为报警。方法模型无关，可挂在现有导航模型上。

Result: 在 5 个未见过场地（2 个自主、3 个遥操作）、142 个事件、所有方法调到同一误报率（10 次/小时）下评测：CUSP 检出 85 个事件，9 个适配基线中最好者仅 26 个；优势主要来自导航模型中所有信号均"看不见"的危险。

Conclusion: CUSP 表明，把危险报警的监督目标从"干预"重定义为更早的"感知起始"，配合生存建模与 CUSUM 累积，可在不改变导航模型的前提下显著提升运行时危险检出率，且对导航模型不可感知的危险同样有效。

Abstract: Off-road navigation exposes a robot to potentially hazardous terrain en route. Although learning-based navigation uses safety supervision to choose which path to drive, it provides no runtime alarm when the robot following that path is heading into danger. Such an alarm must be learned from field logs, where human intervention preempts the failure and the failure itself is therefore never observed. The human judges driving unsafe early but typically intervenes only once failure is clearly near, so the intervention marks that judgment late. That earlier judgment is what a runtime alarm must detect, yet no prior intervention-supervised method has targeted it. To address this problem, we introduce CUSP (CUSUM-governed Survival model of the Perception onset), a model-agnostic runtime hazard alarm that learns this moment from intervention-terminated logs. "Cusp" is a word for the point at which one state is about to turn into another, and the moment we target is exactly such a cusp: the point at which safe driving turns unsafe in a human's judgment. We call this point the perception onset and annotate it separately from the intervention. A visual hazard head is trained on the annotated onset with a discrete-time survival objective so that driving with and without an onset both supervise the head, and a CUSUM accumulates the predicted onset risk into alarms. We evaluate CUSP at five unseen sites, two autonomous and three teleoperated, with 142 events and every method tuned to the same rate of ten false alarms per hour. CUSP detected 85 events compared to 26 for the best of nine adapted baselines, and the margin comes from hazards to which every signal in the navigation model is blind.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Retargeting: Low-Latency and Robust Humanoid Whole-Body Teleoperation with Learned Atomic Motion Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07891)
*Xiayan Xu,Jiyu Yu,Xingzhou Chen,Siyi Qian,Zongyu Ma,Lilu Liu,Ling Shi,Haodong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免重定向的人形全身遥操作策略，单次前向将原始人体动作映射为机器人关节指令，并用全身运动基元码本增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形全身遥操作依赖在线运动重定向以弥合人-机形态差异，带来延迟且易生成物理不可行目标；同时多样、含噪、部分可观测的人体动作常落在训练分布外，导致机器人行为不稳定。

Method: 学习免重定向策略：单次前向直接将原始人体动作映射到机器人关节命令，取消在线运动学适配；并学习一个全身运动基元码本，将分布外观测投影到合理的运动原型上，同时从部分输入中恢复全身运动。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 上进行仿真与真机实验，输入涵盖 VR、光学动捕、文本生成动作和单目视频，方法在延迟与鲁棒性上优于基线。

Conclusion: 免重定向加上运动基元码本的范式可同时降低延迟并提升对噪声、部分观测的鲁棒性，为跨形态全身遥操作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body teleoperation translates human motion into stable robot behavior in real time. Existing systems typically rely on online motion retargeting to bridge human--robot morphological differences, but this process adds latency and can produce physically infeasible targets. Meanwhile, diverse, noisy, and partial human-motion observations often fall outside the training distribution, potentially causing unstable robot behavior. We propose a retargeting-free policy that maps raw human motion directly to robot joint commands in a single forward pass, eliminating online kinematic adaptation. To improve robustness, we learn a codebook of full-body motion primitives that projects out-of-distribution observations onto plausible motion prototypes and recovers full-body motion from partial inputs. Experiments on a Unitree~G1 in simulation and on hardware, using virtual reality, optical mocap, text-to-motion generation, and monocular video inputs, show that our method outperforms baselines in latency and robustness.

</details>


### [193] [From Model to Prototype: Design and Motor-Flap Propulsion Control of a Twin-Wing Metamorphic UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07896)
*Anja Bosak,Dorian Erić,Ana Milas,Stjepan Bogdan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出变形无人机 MetaMorpher 的悬停控制策略：通过与机体旋转相位同步的电机脉冲（motor propulsion）或升降副翼脉冲（flap propulsion）实现悬停控制，仿真验证两种策略的稳定跟踪，并用实物原型验证垂向动力学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MetaMorpher 兼具旋翼悬停与固定翼巡航两种构型，巡航控制已有较成熟基础，而旋转悬停构型的控制尚缺乏合适方案；同时希望找到一种轻量、解耦的悬停控制手段。

Method: 基于前期已验证的飞行动力学模型与概念设计，采用两种相位同步控制策略：(1) motor propulsion，使电机推力脉冲与机体旋转相位同步；(2) flap propulsion，即脉冲式偏转翼面升降副翼。通过多组仿真飞行实验对两种策略进行对比评估，并用物理原型开展实验验证垂向动力学推进模型。

Result: 仿真结果与数学模型吻合，参考轨迹跟踪稳定，表明 flap propulsion 是一种轻量且解耦的悬停控制替代方案；实物实验在不同构型下与垂向动力学推进模型呈现良好稳态一致性。

Conclusion: 相位同步的升降副翼脉冲控制可用于变形无人机悬停，为变形无人机控制提供了一种轻量、解耦的实现路径；后续可进一步开展完整飞行验证。

Abstract: This paper presents the design and control strategy for MetaMorpher, a metamorphic Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV), capable of both spinning-wing hover and fixed flying-wing cruise flight. Since control of the cruise configuration is comparatively well established in the literature, this paper focuses on control of the MetaMorpher in hover mode, building on the flight dynamics model and conceptual design validated in previous work. The control algorithm is implemented using one of two phase-synchronized strategies: motor propulsion, which pulses motor thrust in synchrony with the vehicle's rotation, or flap propulsion, a novel strategy that pulses the deflection of the wing-mounted elevons. We evaluate both strategies in simulation through different flight experiments. Simulation results confirm the mathematical model and show stable reference tracking, demonstrating flap propulsion as a lightweight, decoupled alternative for hover control. Experimental testing validated the vertical-dynamics propulsion model against the physical prototype, demonstrating very good steady-state agreement across different configurations.

</details>


### [194] [PACE: Stage-Consistent Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation via Progress-Aligned Context for Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07917)
*Yenan Chen,Junjie Shi,Lu Chen,Zhongxiang Zhou,Rong Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACE 提出一种状态化策略方法，通过将演示压缩为有序多模态提示词并用因果快权重记忆编码执行进度，缓解长程操作中的"阶段混淆"，在 LIBERO-Gen 等任务上提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程操作中，不同阶段会出现视觉相似状态，且演示与执行速度不一致，导致策略误判当前所处阶段（阶段混淆）。作者希望在不依赖测试期阶段标签或分阶段策略的前提下，让策略持续按实际执行进度重解释完整演示。

Method: PACE 将完整演示压缩为有序多模态提示词 token，并采用仅训练时使用的双边注意力监督来显式暴露演示的潜阶段结构；执行时用回合内的快权重记忆因果编码已实现的动作-观测转移，据此调制对提示词的交叉注意力，形成"进度对齐上下文"，驱动统一的扩散动作专家。

Result: LIBERO-Gen Goal Chain 成功率由 88.9% 提升到 94.0%；Spatial Combination 由 79.1% 提升到 83.3%；两步 Block Routing 由 33.3% 提升到 73.3%。失败分析表明结构化演示对齐与因果执行记忆共同缓解了阶段混淆。

Conclusion: 将演示结构对齐与因果执行记忆结合，可在单一统一策略中实现无阶段标签的进度感知条件化，显著改善长程操作中的阶段混淆问题，为演示条件化策略提供新范式。

Abstract: Demonstration-conditioned policies provide a natural interface for specifying robot behavior, yet long-horizon manipulation remains difficult when visually similar states recur across different stages or when demonstrations and executions proceed at different speeds. We identify the resulting failure mode as stage confusion and introduce Progress-Aligned Context for Execution (PACE), a stateful method that continually reinterprets a complete demonstration according to realized execution progress. PACE compresses the demonstration into ordered multimodal prompt tokens and uses training-only dual-edge attention supervision to expose its latent stage structure. During execution, an episode-local fast-weight memory causally encodes realized action-observation transitions and modulates prompt cross-attention, producing a progress-aligned context for a unified diffusion action expert without test-time stage labels or stage-specific policies. PACE improves success from 88.9% to 94.0% on LIBERO-Gen Goal Chain, from 79.1% to 83.3% on Spatial Combination, and from 33.3% to 73.3% on the two-step Block Routing tasks. Failure analysis further indicates that structured demonstration alignment and causal execution memory jointly mitigate stage confusion.

</details>


### [195] [OpenWAM: An Open Framework for Composable World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07922)
*Heng Yu,David D. Yuan,Juze Zhang,Changan Chen,Yao Feng,Michelle Baldonado,Steve Cousins,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OPENWAM 是一个开放的世界-动作建模框架，以统一的因果机器人视频基础模型和可配置的视频-动作交互为核心，系统地比较 WAM 设计选择并研究反事实动力学学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 系统同时改变视频骨干、交互结构、监督方式与推理流程，导致设计选择难以公平比较；同时动力学学习通常仅依赖成功演示数据，缺乏对反事实转移的系统研究。

Method: 从 Wan2.2-5B 出发，在超 10,000 小时机器人视频上做因果机器人视频预训练，并通过共享 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构集成动作专家，支持 joint、video-then-action、action-then-video、decoupled 四种生成模式；同一架构自然扩展到逆动力学与前向动力学，并引入反事实监督。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO 套件与真实双臂任务上取得高成功率；因果适配的机器人视频训练将 LIBERO-Long 的 VTA 成功率从 68.4% 提升至 97.8%。冻结的局部上下文逆动力学模型（反事实+演示训练）在四个留出 LIBERO-90 任务上平均成功率 84.0%，优于全上下文逆模型（47.0%）和仅演示的局部模型（21.5%）。前向动力学中反事实监督将 RGB 预测误差降低 34.5%，并把 16 个同状态结果辨识准确率从 21.1% 提升到 71.3%。

Conclusion: OPENWAM 为比较 WAM 交互设计提供了统一测试平台，并展示了超越成功演示的视频动力学学习范式。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple future prediction with robot control, yet existing systems often vary the video backbone, interaction structure, supervision, and inference procedure simultaneously, making their design choices difficult to compare. We introduce OPENWAM, an open world-action modeling framework built around a common causal robot-video foundation and configurable video-action interaction. Starting from Wan2.2-5B, we perform causal robot-video pretraining on over 10,000 hours of video, then integrate an action expert through a shared Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that supports joint, video-then-action, action-then-video, and decoupled generation. OPENWAM achieves high success rates on four LIBERO suites and real-world bimanual tasks; robot-video training with causal adaptation improves VTA success on LIBERO-Long from 68.4% to 97.8%. The same configurable architecture naturally extends to inverse and forward dynamics, allowing us to study how counterfactual transitions improve independently trained dynamics models beyond demonstrations alone. When only the video predictor is adapted to a new task, a frozen local-context inverse dynamics model trained on counterfactual data and demonstrations achieves 84.0% mean success across four held-out LIBERO-90 tasks, compared with 47.0% for a full-context inverse model and 21.5% for a local-context model trained only on demonstrations. For forward dynamics, counterfactual supervision reduces RGB prediction error by 34.5% and raises outcome identification from 21.1% to 71.3% among 16 same-state outcomes. OPENWAM provides a common testbed for comparing WAM interaction designs and for studying dynamics learning from video data beyond successful demonstrations.

</details>


### [196] [Adapting Vision-Language-Action Models to Unknown Visual Disruptions During Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07946)
*Ahin Lee,Jinwoo Seo,Youngsoo Jang,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SALT利用上一动作块的"剩余轨迹"作为自监督信号进行测试时自适应，无需标注或专家数据，缓解VLA策略在视觉扰动下的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在执行任务时视觉输入可能突然发生扰动（如遮挡、光照变化），而VLA策略事先既不知道扰动类型也不知道发生时点。现有测试时自适应方法通常需要扰动标注、专家动作或目标域示范，代价高且不实用。

Method: SALT以"剩余轨迹"（上一动作块未执行部分）作为自监督目标——由于相邻动作块在时间上重叠，剩余轨迹与当前预测覆盖同一未来控制区间，构成时间对齐的监督信号。两个机制：Transition Anchoring（扰动发生时剩余轨迹保留了污染前的规划，向它更新可锚定自适应跨过扰动切换点）与Sequential Correction Propagation（保留自适应后的策略并重生成当前块，其剩余轨迹成为下次重规划的监督，把修正沿执行轨迹传递）。监督完全来自策略自身预测，配一个仅在正常轨迹上校准的轻量自适应门控来决定何时开始更新。

Result: LIBERO-10上五种持续视觉扰动下，SmolVLA平均成功率由43.9%提升至53.2%，GR00T N1.7由58.7%提升至66.0%，正常性能基本保持。真机上数字与物理扰动平均任务进度由0.49提升至0.61。

Conclusion: 剩余轨迹是一种有效的时间对齐自监督信号，可在无标注、无专家数据、无目标域示范条件下实现VLA策略的在线自适应，并在仿真与真机上均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Visual disruptions can arise while a robot is executing a task, leaving a vision-language-action (VLA) policy to respond without knowing the disruption type or timing. We introduce Self-supervised Adaptation from Leftover Trajectories (SALT), which uses the leftover trajectory, the unexecuted part of the previous action chunk, as self-supervision for test-time adaptation. Because consecutive chunks overlap in time, the leftover provides a temporally aligned target for the current prediction over the same future control interval. At the onset of a visual shift, the leftover can retain a plan formed before the corruption, so updating the policy toward it anchors the adaptation across the shift (Transition Anchoring). SALT keeps the adapted policy and regenerates the current chunk, whose leftover becomes the target at the next replan, carrying the correction forward along the execution trajectory (Sequential Correction Propagation). Supervision comes entirely from the policy's own predictions, requiring no disruption annotations, expert actions, or target-domain demonstrations, and a lightweight adaptation gate calibrated only on nominal trajectories decides when updates begin. On LIBERO-10, SALT increases average success across five persistent visual corruptions from 43.9% to 53.2% with SmolVLA and from 58.7% to 66.0% with GR00T N1.7, while largely preserving nominal performance. On a real robot, it raises task progress averaged over digital and physical disruptions from 0.49 to 0.61.

</details>


### [197] [Commit While Futures Agree: Consequence-Aware Adaptive Action Chunking for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07949)
*Yuyan Li,Yujia Wang,Yusong Huang,Junjie Yang,Yanggang Sheng,Ziyi Shi,Wenpeng Xu,Xiaoyang Zhou,Haoang Li,Hongliang Lu,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CA³C推理时框架，用动作条件世界模型想象多个候选动作块的未来后果，以"想象未来一致则执行、分歧则重规划"为原则，将执行视野估计建模为贝叶斯变点推断问题，无需重训基座策略，在多仿真基准与真实机器人操作任务上将失败率最多相对降低71.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块策略（action-chunking）会预测多步控制序列，但一个根本问题未解决：预测出的动作块中应提交多少步再重规划？现有系统通常执行固定长度前缀，隐含假设同一执行视野在各状态下同样可信；部分自适应方法从预测动作的相似性或稳定性估计该视野，但不同动作可能导致相同成功结果，而相似动作也可能产生不同未来，说明承诺应由"想象未来之间的一致性"而非动作空间相似性来决定。

Method: 提出Consequence-Aware Adaptive Action Chunking（CA³C），一个推理时框架，原则为"想象未来一致时继续执行，分歧时重规划"。不修改也不重训基座策略，使用动作条件世界模型，在同一采样噪声下想象多个候选动作块的未来后果；基于这些想象后果，将执行视野估计形式化为贝叶斯变点推断问题，并通过未来共识选择执行候选。

Result: 在多个仿真基准和真实机器人操作任务上，CA³C一致地提升了多种动作分块策略的表现，相较对应的基座策略实现最高71.8%的失败率相对下降。

Conclusion: 在执行视野估计中，应以想象未来的一致性（后果层面）而非动作空间相似性作为承诺依据；所提出的CA³C作为即插即用的推理时模块，无需重训即可显著提升现有动作分块策略的鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: Action-chunking policies predict multi-step control sequences, but a fundamental question remains: how much of a predicted action chunk should be committed before replanning? Existing systems typically execute a fixed-length prefix, implicitly assuming that the same execution horizon remains trustworthy across states. Some adaptive methods estimate this horizon from the similarity or stability of predicted actions. However, different actions may lead to the same successful outcome, whereas similar actions can produce different futures, suggesting that commitment should be determined by agreement among imagined futures rather than by similarity in action space. To this end, we propose Consequence-Aware Adaptive Action Chunking (CA$^3$C), an inference-time framework built on a simple principle: commit while imagined futures agree, and replan when they diverge. Without modifying or retraining the base policy, CA$^3$C uses an action-conditioned world model to imagine the future consequences of multiple candidate action chunks under the same sampling noise. Using these imagined consequences, we formulate execution-horizon estimation as a Bayesian change-point inference problem and select the execution candidate through future consensus. Across multiple simulation benchmarks and real-world robot manipulation tasks, CA$^3$C consistently improves diverse action-chunking policies, achieving up to a 71.8% relative reduction in failure rate over the corresponding base policies.

</details>


### [198] [IronMan: Information-Constrained Video-Action Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07961)
*Yuanshuo Zhang,Wenzhe Zhao,Zixing Lei,Bin Chen,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 IronMan 框架，基于信息瓶颈原理约束视频-动作学习中的视觉信息，用动力学感知瓶颈蒸馏紧凑世界表征，在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 及 LIBERO-Plus 上取得领先成功率与 OOD 鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频动作模型（VAMs）将视觉动力学建模与动作生成耦合用于机器人操作，但视频表征并非天然适合动作生成：策略暴露于过多视觉细节会损害其泛化能力。因此需要抑制无关视觉信息、保留动作相关动力学线索。

Method: IronMan 基于信息瓶颈原理构建。核心是施加信息约束，压制无关视觉信息同时保留动作相关的动力学线索；具体采用动力学感知瓶颈（dynamics-aware bottleneck），将嘈杂、纠缠的单步视频特征蒸馏为紧凑的世界表征。

Result: 在大量仿真与真实世界实验中，ID 性能强且 OOD 鲁棒，推理高效。LIBERO 成功率 99.0%，RoboTwin clean2clean 79.4%，优于所有对比基线；在 OOD 偏移下 LIBERO-Plus 达 79.1%，超出最强基线 10.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: 信息约束式的视频-动作学习为机器人操作提供了一条兼顾泛化性与效率的稳健路径，实验验证了其在分布内与分布外场景下的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Video Action Models (VAMs) couple visual dynamics modeling with action generation for robot manipulation. However, video representations are not naturally suited to action generation, as exposing the action policy to excessive visual detail can impair its generalization ability. Therefore, we introduce IronMan (Information-constRained videO-actioN learning for robot MANipulation), a robust video-action learning framework built on the information bottleneck principle. The core principle of this framework is to impose information constraints that suppress irrelevant visual information while preserving action-relevant dynamics cues. IronMan employs a dynamics-aware bottleneck that distills noisy, entangled one-step video features into compact world representations. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate strong in-distribution (ID) performance and out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness while maintaining efficient inference. IronMan achieves success rates of 99.0% on LIBERO and 79.4% on RoboTwin clean2clean, outperforming all the evaluated baselines. Under OOD shifts, IronMan achieves a success rate of 79.1% on LIBERO-Plus, exceeding the strongest baseline by 10.4 percentage points. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [199] [Reactive Task-Oriented Robot-Human Handovers via Generative Hypothesis Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08003)
*Carmen Scheidemann,Andreea Tulbure,Pascal Burkhardt,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GENESIS-Handover 方法，利用 VLM 图像生成产生任务特定的手-物交互假设，并实时与观测到的人手姿态匹配，从而为未见过的物体-任务组合推断合适的交接配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向的机器人-人手递研究已从建模物体几何发展到融入可供性，但通常忽略了人类为使用物体而选择的显式任务特定手部姿态；同一物体支持多种交互模态（如羊角锤敲击或拔钉），若不建模这种多样性，难以实现鲁棒的任务导向手递。

Method: 以 VLM 图像生成作为手-物交互先验，生成多种任务条件下的手-物交互假设；将这些假设与实时观测的人手姿态进行匹配，推断最合适的交接构型；先单独评估交互提议模块，再将完整系统部署到移动操作平台上验证。

Result: 在 12 名参与者、5 组任务-物体配对的用户研究中，83.3% 的参与者认为该方法相比此前最先进方法具有更好的任务理解能力。

Conclusion: 利用 VLM 作为手-物交互先验可实现任务条件下的交接策略，并泛化到未见过的物体-任务组合，提升任务导向机器人手递的语义理解。

Abstract: When humans hand each other objects, they incorporate both geometric and semantic information into this process. For example, passing a knife with the handle towards the recipient, rather than the blade, is both more ergonomic and safer. Recent state-of-the-art methods for task-oriented robot-human handovers have progressed from modeling object geometry to incorporating object affordances. However, they often forgo predicting the explicit, task-specific hand poses a human selects to utilize an object. Since many objects support multiple interaction modalities, e.g., a claw hammer used to strike or pull nails, this variability must be modeled to achieve robust task-oriented handovers. To tackle this, we propose a novel approach, GENESIS-Handover (GENErative HypotheSIS), which leverages VLM image generation to produce a variety of task-specific hand-object interaction hypotheses. These hypotheses are matched in real time to the observed human hand pose, enabling inference of the most suitable handover configuration. By leveraging VLMs as priors of plausible hand-object interactions, the method produces task-conditioned handover strategies for previously unseen object-task pairs. We evaluate the standalone interaction proposal module before deploying the full system on a mobile manipulator. In a user study with 12 participants across five task-object pairs, 83.3% perceived our method to have better task understanding than the previous state of the art.

</details>


### [200] [Vector Map Quality Metrics for Contextual Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08043)
*Marie-Ngoïe Badibanga Kalenda,Philippe Bonnifait,Marie-Anne Mittet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出地图质量指标GOSPAM，从定位误差、存在性与完整性三方面统一度量矢量地图差异，并用仿真验证其对偏置、误检、漏检和坐标误差的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全依赖持续的地图维护，需要可靠的质量指标来判断何时、何地触发地图更新（如众包数据）以及新编译地图何时可部署；而现有矢量地图质量评估缺乏统一、可解释的度量。

Method: 设计名为GOSPAM的新指标，按定位误差（location errors）、存在性（existence）与完整性（completeness）三方面量化地图间差异；在点特征与折线特征地图上进行详细仿真，考察其对偏置、假阳性、假阴性和坐标误差等常见退化形式的敏感性。

Result: 仿真结果表明GOSPAM能统一且可解释地捕捉各类地图偏差，对多种退化模式均表现敏感，是汽车应用中地图质量评估的有力候选。

Conclusion: GOSPAM为矢量地图质量提供了统一、可解释的度量，可作为触发地图更新和部署决策的依据；但验证限于仿真，尚未见真实车队或众包数据上的实证。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in autonomous driving requires continuous map maintenance supported by reliable quality indicators. In this context, it is crucial to identify when and where map updates should be triggered, for instance through crowdsourced data, and under which conditions a new map compilation should be deployed. This paper focuses on effective metrics for assessing the quality of vector maps and guiding such decisions. We present a new metric called GOSPAM designed to measure map discrepancies in terms of location errors, existence, and completeness. Through detailed simulations on both point and polyline feature maps, we analyze its sensitivity to common map degradation such as bias, false positives, false negatives, and coordinate errors. The results demonstrate that GOSPAM offers a unified and interpretable measure that effectively captures various forms of map deviation, making it a strong candidate for map quality assessment in automotive applications.

</details>


### [201] [Navigation with RF Cues: Embodied Perception Action under Multipath Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08105)
*Wenlihan Lu,Tianshun Li,Liuqing Yang,Shijian Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 构建Habitat Sionna RT多模态基准（视觉+RF+位姿），提出不确定性感知多模态导航框架，从RF/视觉/位姿历史联合估计目标方向及其不确定性以指导动作选择，未见场景中SR提升18.2%、SPL提升11.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工厂巡检中机器人需在无先验地图、无目标坐标条件下抵达设备；RF信号可提供方向线索以补偿遮挡或弱光导致的视觉失效，但多径传播会扭曲瞬时RF测量，使真实方向推断困难。

Method: 1) 构建Habitat Sionna RT基准，利用精细场景几何与材质属性生成与机器人动作对齐的视觉与RF观测；2) 提出不确定性感知多模态导航框架，从RF、视觉、位姿观测历史中联合估计目标方向与不确定性；3) 将方向估计及不确定性结合视觉上下文用于动作选择。

Result: 在未见场景中，成功率(SR)相对最强基线提升18.2%，路径长度加权成功率(SPL)提升11.5%。

Conclusion: 融合RF与视觉的多模态不确定性建模可有效缓解多径干扰与视觉退化，为无地图目标搜索导航提供了仿真基准与方法基础。

Abstract: Smart factory inspection requires robots to reach connected equipment without a prior map or known target coordinates. Radio frequency (RF) signals from the target can provide directional cues to complement visual observations when occlusion or poor lighting limits target detection. However, multipath propagation can distort these cues, making it difficult to infer the target's true direction from instantaneous RF measurements. To enable navigation research under these conditions, we first construct a Habitat Sionna RT benchmark that uses detailed scene geometry and assigned material properties to generate aligned visual and RF observations in response to robot actions. Building on this benchmark, we propose an uncertainty aware multimodal navigation framework that jointly estimates target direction and its uncertainty from a history of RF, visual, and pose observations. These estimates inform action selection alongside visual context. Experiments in unseen scenes show relative improvements of 18.2% in success rate (SR) and 11.5% in success weighted by path length (SPL) over the strongest evaluated baseline.

</details>


### [202] [Reactive Exploration of Unknown Environments for Redundant Robots using Virtual Model Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08110)
*Alessio Canzolino,Omar Faris,Alessandro De Blasi,Fulvio Forni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向冗余机械臂+眼在手相机的主动探索方法，基于信息增益的体素评分选点，并用虚拟模型控制实现全身反应式避障，在受限与开阔环境中实现>90%建图覆盖率且无碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限、被遮挡、部分已知空间中的机器人操作极具挑战：机械臂整体位姿需在满足紧致几何约束的同时避开新发现的障碍。已有方法多依赖预先规划或路径重规划，难以在线兼顾安全性与探索效率。

Method: 1) 主动探索框架：结合机器人当前状态与期望信息增益，设计新的评分函数，从未探索空间中直接选取目标体素，实现在线导航与信息采集；2) 使用虚拟模型控制（Virtual Model Control）驱动机器人安全趋近目标体素，保证柔顺性，并实现全身反应式避障，无需路径重规划；3) 冗余机械臂+眼在手相机配置。

Result: 在受限与开阔环境的仿真和真机实验中，所有测试环境均达到90%以上的建图覆盖率，耗时低于120秒，且未与障碍物发生碰撞。

Conclusion: 该主动探索+虚拟模型控制方案能有效应对受限空间的探索与安全运动问题，具备仿真到真机的可行性。但方法偏系统集成与工程实现，体素评分与信息增益的增益幅度有限，缺少与强基线的大规模定量对比。

Abstract: The exploration of confined, occluded, and partially known spaces poses significant challenges in robotic manipulation. The overall pose of the robotic arm must be carefully controlled to respect tight geometric constraints while avoiding newly discovered obstacles. We address this problem by proposing an active exploration approach for redundant robotic arms with an eye-in-hand camera configuration. Our approach navigates and acquires information in real-time based on a novel scoring method that directly selects a target voxel from the unexplored space using the robot's current state and expected information gain. To move the robot safely toward the target voxel, we utilize Virtual Model Control, which guarantees compliance and enables whole-body reactive obstacle avoidance without the need for path replanning. Simulated and real-robot experiments in both confined and open environments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, achieving over $90\%$ mapping coverage across all tested environments in under $120$ seconds without colliding with obstacles.

</details>


### [203] [Energy-Aware Path Following: Comparative Analysis of Reinforcement Learning and NMPC for Electric Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08112)
*Mohamed Sabaa,Mostafa Emam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在统一 Frenet 坐标系运动学模型与含再生制动的 VT-CPEM 能量模型下，对比 NMPC、PPO、增益调度 Ackermann 状态反馈与 Stanley 四种路径跟踪控制器，并验证 PPO 的跨赛道与跨车辆模型迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径跟踪控制存在"减小路径偏差"与"保持平滑速度曲线"的双目标权衡；对电动车而言，后者尤为关键，因为平滑驾驶可通过再生制动回收能量、延长续驶里程，而现有文献对该方面研究不足。

Method: 基于 Frenet 框架的统一运动学车辆模型，集成经校验的显式再生制动能量模型（VT-CPEM）；实现并对比四种控制器：NMPC（JIT 编译 CasADi 满足实时性）、PPO、增益调度 Ackermann 状态反馈基线（PID-SF）与 Stanley 几何基线。PPO 仅在直线与 S 形弯道训练，随后将未修改策略迁移至未见赛道（ISO 3888-1 换道、chicane、随机参数化样条、±3° 坡道），并零样本迁移到线性轮胎动态单轨车辆模型后再做少量微调。

Result: PPO 策略可直接迁移至多种未见赛道以及在训练中未出现的车辆模型，先零样本获得可接受的初始性能，微调后进一步优化；论文给出四种控制器在能量回收与跟踪精度等指标上的对比分析。

Conclusion: 在显式再生制动建模的统一框架下，学习型控制器（PPO）在保持跟踪性能的同时具备良好的跨场景、跨模型迁移能力，为兼顾能耗与跟踪的路径跟踪控制提供可行方案；作者给出未来工作方向。

Abstract: Path-following control strategies typically follow the bi-objective optimization dilemma: minimizing deviations from a reference path while maintaining smooth speed profiles. The latter objective is especially relevant for Electric Vehicles (EVs), since their limited driving range can be extended by recovering energy through regenerative braking, a feature that has not yet been sufficiently studied in the literature. In this work, we perform a comparative analysis of four controllers under one common Frenet frame-based kinematic vehicle model, utilizing a validated energy model (VT-CPEM) with explicit regenerative braking. Herein, we implement the following controllers: Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), gain-scheduled Ackermann state-feedback baseline (PID-SF), and a Stanley geometric baseline. To satisfy real-time requirements, we implement the NMPC using JIT-compiled CasADi. Moreover, we train the PPO using traditional straight and S-curve tracks, after which we successfully transfer the unmodified policy to unseen tracks, including: an ISO 3888-1 lane-change, a chicane, randomly-generated parameterized-splines, and a $\pm3^\circ$ graded road. In addition, the policy transfers to a dynamic single-track vehicle model with linear tires, zero-shot with an acceptable initial performance, which was optimized after brief fine-tuning. Thereby, we demonstrate that our PPO is readily transferable to more comprehensive vehicle models. We conclude with a performance analysis of developed controllers and discuss ideas for future work.

</details>


### [204] [AutodidactWAM: Cross-Modal Self-Distillation from Generated Video to Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08119)
*Sergei Kurchev,Iaroslav Kolomiets,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Artem Lykov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对世界-动作模型(WAM)在新机器人本体上的"视频-动作不对称"问题，提出自蒸馏框架 AutodidactWAM：用免遥操作的手部姿态估计+逆运动学从生成视频中恢复动作，仅微调动作层，比较监督重标注与 rectified-flow DPO，并组合 DPO+SFT+DTW 取得最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Cosmos 3 类世界-动作模型经 LoRA 微调到未见过的 Unitree G1 人形机器人（BrainCo 五指手）后，暴露出视频-动作不对称：生成的视频看起来合理，但共同生成的动作系统性错位。原生动作在 pre-grasp/grasp/pick-and-place 三阶段成功率仅约 17%/10%/7%，对留出物体更差；而传统解法需要昂贵的任务专用遥操作数据。

Method: 无需遥操作训练手部姿态估计器 AutodidactWAM，先在模型生成视频上提手姿，再经逆运动学恢复动作估计；将恢复动作与原预测配对，形成偏好目标，仅微调动作相关层，生成视频作 teacher forcing。比较监督重标注与基于 rectified flow 的 Diffusion-DPO 适配，并进一步提出 DPO+SFT+DTW 混合目标（偏好监督+监督目标拟合+笛卡尔轨迹锚定）。

Result: 恢复动作门控在 pre-grasp/grasp/pick-and-place 达约 75%/47%/42%，vs 原生 17%/10%/7%。混合目标 DPO+SFT+DTW 最优：训练物体 Oreo 上 90% pre-grasp、20% 全任务；留出物体 80%/30%。纯 Flow-DPO 虽验证偏好准确率 1.000 却 0% 成功率，说明增益来自训练目标组合而非对比目标本身。

Conclusion: 世界-动作模型的动作分支在本体迁移下显著弱于视频分支，可从自身生成视频中自蒸馏出可用的动作监督；一次性本体适配后可免任务级遥操作。有效关键在于多目标组合（偏好+监督拟合+轨迹锚定），单纯对比式目标无益。属于具身泛化与自蒸馏方向的有价值实证。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) such as Cosmos 3 jointly generate future video and robot actions from an observation and instruction. Adapting one such model with a lightweight LoRA fine-tune to a previously unseen robot, a Unitree G1 humanoid with five-fingered BrainCo hands, exposes a video-action asymmetry: the video renders plausible task executions, while the co-generated action is systematically mis-targeted. We evaluate closed-loop real-robot trials at three cumulative stages: pre-grasp, grasp, and pick-and-place. The native action succeeds only approximately 17%, 10%, and 7% of the time, respectively, and performs worse on held-out objects. We propose AutodidactWAM, a hand-pose estimator trained without teleoperation, followed by inverse kinematics, that runs on the model's generated video to recover action estimates. Paired with the native prediction, these recovered actions provide preferred targets for fine-tuning only the action-related layers, while the generated video is teacher-forced. We compare supervised relabeling with a rectified-flow adaptation of Diffusion-DPO. After one-time embodiment adaptation, self-distillation requires no additional task-specific teleoperation. The recovered-action gate reaches approximately 75%, 47%, and 42% pre-grasp, grasp, and pick-and-place success, compared with 17%, 10%, and 7% for the native action. A hybrid objective combining preference supervision, supervised target fitting, and Cartesian trajectory anchoring (DPO+SFT+DTW) performs best: on Oreo, the training object, it reaches 90% pre-grasp and 20% full-task success; on a held-out object, it reaches 80% and 30%. Plain Flow-DPO reaches 0% success despite 1.000 validation preference accuracy, indicating that the combination of training objectives, rather than the contrastive objective alone, drives the observed gains.

</details>


### [205] [iGPC: Generative Motion Priors for Object-Aware Humanoid Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08120)
*Anujith Muraleedharan,Abdul Ahad Butt,Nolan Fey,Yash Prabhu,Anamika J H,Sandor Felber,Maurice Rahme,Ivan Laptev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将生成式预训练控制器（GPC）从通用人体运动扩展到感知驱动的全身人形-环境交互：先用场景可供性与特权状态训练交互专家，再通过两种互补蒸馏目标将技能迁移到仅依赖机载传感的感知学生策略，实现可部署的接触丰富交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在非结构化环境中需要兼具鲁棒全身控制与对物体的感知-物理交互能力。大规模人体运动数据虽能提供自然、多样的控制先验，但如何把这种先验有效迁移到感知驱动的物体交互任务上仍是瓶颈：先验策略缺少任务特定的接触行为，而专家训练依赖难以实机获取的特权状态信息。

Method: 1) 将预训练GPC改造为以场景可供性线索和特权状态为条件的交互专家，在保留人体运动先验的同时学习伸手、抓握环境支撑以稳定、推动可移动物体等任务特定接触行为；2) 构建感知驱使学生，保留预训练GPC策略，利用机载观测从专家蒸馏交互技能；3) 提出两个互补训练目标，弥合特权专家观测与真实传感输入之间的差距，使预训练运动先验在蒸馏中有效适配。整体为“预训练先验—特权专家—感知学生”的三段式框架。
